bhāsamāna: ᵥtvam ᵥanu ᵥcara: taṃ śuṣṇaṃ hantumanvagaccha: | yadvā | atha śuṣṇasya purabhedanamantaraṃ bhā dīsīruvamanu cara: anvagaccha: | prāsavānityartha: | he ᵥindra tvaṃ ᵥyat yadā ᵥdvitā dvidhā dvividhai: stotṛbhiryaṣṭṛbhiśca ᵥhavya: hātavya: ᵥbhuva: bhave: | tadānīṃ tvaṃ śuṣṇasya puraṃ saṃ piṇagītyanvaya: | bhavaterleṭi sipyaḍāgama: | chāndasa: śapo luk | 'bhūsuvostiṅi' iti guṇapratiṣedhāduvaṅ || cāturviṃśike'hani mādhyandinasavane brahmaśastre 'mama svā' iti vaikalpiko'nurūpastvaca: | sūtraṃ tu pūrvamudāhṛtam || marma tvā sūra udite marma mudhyaṃdine diva: | marma prapitve apiśarvare vasvā stomāso avṛtsata || 29 || marma | tvā | sūrai | ut'ite | marma | mudhyaṃdine | diva: | marma | pra'pitve | api'śarvare | vaso iti | ā | stomāsa: | avṛtsata || 29 || ᵥsūre sūrye ᵥudite udayaṃ prāpte pūrvāhnasamaye ᵥmama ᵥstomāsa: stotrāṇi he ᵥvaso vāsakendra tvām ᵥā ᵥavṛtsata āvartayantu | asmadabhimukhaṃ gamayantu | tathā ᵥdiva: divasasya ᵥmadhyaṃdine madhyāhne'pi madīyā: stomāstvāmāvartayantu | tathā ᵥprapitve prāpte divasasyāvasāne sāyāhne'pi madīyā: stomāstvāmāvartayantu | ᵥapiśarvare | śarvarīṃ rātrimapigata: kāla: apiśarvare: | śarvare kāle'pi madīyā: stomāstvāmāvartayantu || stuhi stuhidete ghā te maṃheṣṭhāso maghonām | ninditāśva: prapathī paramajyā maghasye medhyātithe || 30 || stuhi | stuhi | ita | ete | gha | te | maṃheṣṭhāsa: | maghonām | nindita'aśva: | pra'pathī | parama'jyā: | maghasya | medhya'atithe || 30 || āsaṅko rājarṣirmedhyātithe bahu dhanaṃ datvā tampṛṃ dattadānasya svasya stutau prerayati | he ᵥmedhyātithe yajñārhātitha etatsaṃjajñe ᵥstuhi ᵥstuhīt | puna:punarasmān praśaṃseva | modāsva audāsīnyaṃ mā kārṣī: | ᵥete ᵥgha ete khalu vayaṃ ᵥmaghonāṃ dhanavatāṃ madhye ᵥte tubhyaṃ ᵥmaghasya dhanasya ᵥmaṃhiṣṭhāsa: dātṛtamā: | ^vananvataḥ vananvataḥ saṃbhaktavataḥ ^aśvān turagān ^ahaṃ prāyogiḥ ^śraddhayā ādaratiśayena yuktaḥ san ^yat yadā he medhyātithe tvadīye ^rathe ^ā ^rahaṃ ārohayam | raherantāṃbhavitavyarthāluṅi ‘kṛmṭraruhibhyaḥ’ ’ iti cleraṅādeśaḥ | tadānīṃ māmevaṃ stuhi | ^uta api ca | prakṛtastyupekṣa eṣa samuccayaḥ | ^vāmasya vananīyasya ^vasunaḥ dhanasya | pūrvavat karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī | īdṛśaṃ dhanaṃ ^ciketati | eṣa āsaṅgo dātuṃ jānāti | ^yādṛḥ yaduvaṃśīyavaḥ | yadvā | yadavo manuṣyāḥ | teṣu prasiddhaḥ | ^paśuḥ | lusamatvarthametat | paśumān | yadvā | paśuḥ paśyateḥ | sūkṣmasya draṣṭā | ^yaḥ āsaṅgaḥ ^asti vidyate eṣa ciketatītyanvayaḥ ||

paṇibhirdevagīvvapahatāsu ᵥpadebhyaḥ gatānāṃ gavāṃ mārge saṃlagnebhyo'nveṣaṇasādhanabhūtebhyaḥ ᵥṛte ᵥcit ṛte'pi vināpi ᵥsakhā mitrabhūtaḥ ᵥśacīvān karmavān praśastakarmā ᵥyaḥ indraḥ ᵥnṛbhyaḥ netṛbhyaḥ devebhyaḥ ᵥgāḥ paṇibhirapahatā ᵥdāt punardattavān |

'pibā sutasya' iti caturviṃśatyṛcaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktaṃ kāṇvasya medhyātitherārṣam|
ayujo bṛhatyo yujaḥ satobṛhattyaḥ ekaviṃśatyanuṣṭubdvāviṃśatitrayoviṃśatyau gāyatryau caturviṃśatī bṛhatī|
etāścatasraḥ kurayāṇasya putrasya pākasthāmanāmno rājño dānastutipratipādikāḥ|
atastaddevatākāḥ|
śiṣṭā aindrāḥ|
tathā cānu- krāntaṃ—'piba caturviṃśatimedhyātithiḥ prāgārthaṃ tvanuṣṭubgāyatryau bṛhatī cānyāḥ|
kaurayāṇasya pāka- sthāgno dānastutiḥ' iti|
mahāvrate niṣkevalye bārhatatṛcāśītau dānastutirvinedaṃ sūktaṃ saptamyaṣṭa- myoścoddhāraḥ|
tathaiva pañcamāraṇyake sūtritaṃ—'pibā sutasya rasina iti viṃśateḥ saptamīṃ aṣṭamīṃ coddharati' (ai. ā. 5. 2. 4) iti|
jyotiṣṭome niṣkevalye ādyo rathantarasāmapragāthaḥ śaṃsanīyaḥ|
sūyate hi—'pibā sutasya rasina iti sāmapragāthaḥ' (āśva. śrau. 5. 15) iti|
cāturviṃśike'hani niṣkevalye'pyayaṃ pragāthaḥ|
sūyate hi—'ukto rathantarasyobhayaṃ śṛṇavacca na iti bṛhataḥ' (āśva. śrau. 7. 3) iti|
evamanyatrāpi yadi rathantaraṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavati tatra sarvatrāyaṃ pragātho draṣṭavyaḥ|
pañcame'- hani praugaśastre 'pibā sutasya' ityayamaindraḥ pragāthaḥ|
sūtritaṃ ca—'pibā sutasya rasino devadevaṃ voḍvase' (āśva. śrau. 7. 12) iti|
pibā sutasya rasino matsavā na indra gomataḥ| āpinau bodhi sadhamādyau vṛdhe'smā~ avantu te dhiyaḥ|| 1||
piba| sutasya| rasinaḥ| matsavā| naḥ| indra| gomataḥ| āpinau| naḥ| bodhi| sadhamādyau| vṛdhe| asmān| avantu| te| dhiyaḥ|| 1||
he indra rasinaḥ rasavataḥ gomataḥ govikāraiḥ payaḥprabhṛtibhiḥ śrayaṇadravyairyuktasya naḥ asmadīyasya sutasya abhiputasya|
'kriyāgrahaṇaṃ kartavyam' iti karmaṇaḥ saṃpradānatvāccaturthyarthe ṣaṣṭhī|
īdṛśaṃ somaṃ piba|
pītvā ca matsva nasto bhava|
api ca sadhamādyaḥ saha mādayitavyaḥ sahitai- rasmābhistarpayitavyasvam āpiḥ āpayitā bandhuḥ san naḥ asmākaṃ vṛdhe vardhanāya bodhi budhyasva|

ṛgveda: [a. 5. a. 8. va. 2]
purupriyā ṇaṃ ūtaye purumandrā puruvasū |
stuṣe kaṇvaso aśvinā ||
4 ||
puru'priyā | naḥ | ūtaye | puru'mandrā | puruvasu iti puru'vasū | stuṣe | kaṇvasaḥ | aśvinā || 4 ||
purupriyā bahūnāṃ priyo purumandrā bahumadau bahūnāṃ mādayitārau vā |
yadvā |
puru bahulaṃ mādayantau |
puruvasū bahudhanau īdṛśau aśvinā aśvinau naḥ asmākam ūtaye rakṣaṇāya kaṇvasaḥ kaṇvagotrā vayaṃ stuṣe stumahe |
puruṣavacanayordhyatyayaḥ |
yadvā |
kaṇvasa iti pūjārthaṃ bahuvacanam |
ṛṣirātmānaṃ saṃbodhya brūte |
he antarātman kaṇvasaḥ kaṇvagotrastvaṃ stuṣe aśvinau stuhi ||
maṃhiṣṭhā vājasātameṣyantā śubhaspatī |
gantārā dāśuṣo gṛham ||
5 ||
maṃhiṣṭhā | vāja'sātamā | iṣyantā | śubhaḥ | patī iti | gantārā | dāśuṣaḥ | gṛham || 5 ||
maṃhiṣṭhā maṃhiṣṭhau maṃhanīyau pūjanīyau yadvā dātṛtamau vājasātamā |
vājo'nnaṃ balaṃ vā |
tasya atiśayena dātārau |
yadvā vājo havirlakṣaṇamannam |
tasya saṃbhaktṛtamau |
sanoteḥ sanatervā 'janasana' ' iti viṭ |
viḍvanoranunāsikasyātvam |
iṣyantā stotṛbhya iṣamannaṃ kurvantau |
iṣaśabdāt 'tak-roti' ' iti ṇic |
ṇeracavajāvena ṭilope prāpte 'prakṛtyaikāc' iti prakṛtibhāvaḥ |
yadvā |
iṣyantā eṣayantau śreyāṃsi prāpayantau |
'iṣa gatau' |
asmāddhetumaṇic |
saṃjñāpūrvakasya vidheranityatvā- duṇābhāvaḥ |
śubhaḥ śobhanasya dhanasyodayakasya vā patī svāminau |
'ṣaṣṭhyāḥ patiputra' ' iti visarjanīyasya satvam |
dāśuṣaḥ carupuroḍāśādīni datsavato yajamānasya gṛhaṃ gantārā gamana- śīlau |
gamestācchīlikastṛn |
ataḥ 'na lokāvyaya' ' iti karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhyāḥ pratiṣedhaḥ |
īdṛśā- vaśvinau stuma iti śeṣaḥ ||
1 ||
tā sudevāya dāśuṣe sumedhāmavitāriṇīm |
ghṛtairgavyūtimṛkṣatam ||
6 ||
tā | su'devāya | dāśuṣe | sumedhām | avi'tāriṇīm | ghṛtaiḥ | gavyūtim | ṛkṣatam || 6 ||
he aśvinau tā tau tādṛśau yuvāṃ sudevāya |
śobhanā devā yena yaṣṭavyāḥ sa tathoktaḥ |
bahu- vrīhau 'nansubhyām' ityuttarapadāntodāttatvam |
tasmai dāśuṣe havirdattavate yajamānāya |
tādarthye caturthī |
īdṛgyajamānāya sumedhāṃ śobhanayajñām avitāriṇīm |
vitaraṇaṃ vigamanamapāyaḥ |
ana- pāthinīm |
nansamāse'vyayapūrvapadaprakṛtisvaratvam |
īdṛśīṃ gavyūtim |
gāvo yūthante saṃyujyante'- treti gavyūtirgosaṃcārabhūmiḥ |
'gavyūtau chandasi' (pā. sū. 6. 1. 79. 2) ityavādeśaḥ |
tāṃ ghṛtaiḥ kṣaraṇaśīlairudakaiḥ 'ukṣataṃ siñcatam |
'ukṣa secane' ||
ā naḥ stomupa dravatūryyaṃ śyenebhirāśibhiḥ |
yātamebhiraśvinā ||
7 ||
ā | naḥ | stomam | upa | dravata | tūryam | śyenebhiḥ | āśu'bhiḥ | yātam | aśvaibhiḥ | aśvinā ||7||
he aśvinau aśvinau naḥ asmākaṃ stomaṃ stotram aśvebhiḥ aśvaiḥ |
'dravat tūryam' ityubhe kṣipranāmanī |
ekaḥ pūrakaḥ |
tūrya kṣipram upa ā yātam upāgacchatam |
yadvā |
dravaditi stomaviśeṣaṇam |
dravat śīghraṃ pravartamānaṃ stomamityarthaḥ |
kīdṛśairaśvaiḥ |
śyenebhiḥ śaṃsanīyagatibhiḥ prahastagamanaiḥ āśubhiḥ śīghragaiḥ ||
1. kha-bha-śa- 'syādityātve |
2. jha-ta-śa- 'detumati ṇic |
yebhi̭stisraḥ pa̭rāva॒tau di॒vo vi॒śvāni̭ roca॒nā | śrīra̭ktūna॒pari̭dīryathaḥ ||8|| yebhi̭: | ti॒sraḥ | pa॒rā॒'va॒taḥ | di॒vaḥ | vi॒śvāni̭ | roca॒nā | trīn | a॒ktūn | pa॒ri॒'dīryathaḥ ||8||
he aśvinau 'tisraḥ 'divaḥ trīn divasān 'trīn 'aktūn tisro rātrīśca |
atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā |
etāvantaṃ kālaṃ 'viśvāni sarvāṇi vyāptāni vā 'rocanā rocamānāni nakṣatrarūpāṇi devagṛhāṇi 'parāvataḥ dūradeśāt 'yebhiḥ yairaśvaiḥ 'paridīryathaḥ parigacchathaḥ |
dīyatirgatikarmā |
tairasmākaṃ stotramupayātabhiti pūrvatrānvayaḥ ||
u॒ta no॒ goma̭tīri॒ṣa u॒ta sā॒tīra̭harvidā | vi॒ pṛ॒thaḥ sā॒tayḙ si॒tam ||9|| u॒ta | naḥ | go'ma̭tīḥ | i॒ṣaḥ | u॒ta | sā॒tīḥ | a॒haḥ'vidā | vi | pṛ॒thaḥ | sā॒tayḙ | si॒tam ||9||
'uta api ca he 'aharvidā ahno lambhayitārau |
yadvā |
ahni prabhātasamaye veditavyau stotavyau |
'naḥ asmabhyaṃ 'gomatīḥ bahubhirgobhiryuktāḥ 'iṣaḥ annāni dattamiti śeṣaḥ |
'uta api ca 'sātīḥ saṃbhajanīyā dātavyā vā rāyaścāsmabhyaṃ dattam |
sanateḥ sanotervā karmaṇi ktin |
‘janasanakhanām’ ityātvam |
‘utiyūti’ ityādinā ktina udāttatvaṃ nipātyate |
api cāsmākaṃ 'sātaye uktānāṃ gavādīnāṃ lābhāya saṃbhajanāya vā 'pathaḥ tadupāyarūpān mārgān 'vi 'sitaṃ viśeṣeṇa badhnītam |
yathānye na praviśanti tathā kurutamityarthaḥ |
‘ṣiñ bandhane’ |
chāndaso vikaraṇasya luk |
yadvā |
upasargavaśādayaṃ dhātuḥ svārthaviparīte bandhanābhāve vartate |
prasmaraṇaṃ prasthānamiti yathā |
pathau mārgān vi sitaṃ vimuñcatam |
pradarśayatamatyarthaḥ |
pathicchando'ntodāttaḥ |
tasya śasi ṭilopa udāttannivṛttiścareṇa śasa udāttatvam ||
ā no̭ goma̭nta॒maśva̭nā su॒vīra̭ṃ su॒ratha̭ṃ ra॒yim | vo॒ḻhamaśvā̭vatīri॒ṣaḥ ||10|| ā | naḥ | go'ma̭ntam | a॒śva॒nā | su॒'vīra̭m | su॒'ratha̭m | ra॒yim | vo॒ḻham | a॒śva'vatīḥ | i॒ṣaḥ ||10||
he 'aśvinā aśvinau 'naḥ asmabhyaṃ 'rathaṃ dhanam 'ā 'voḻham āvahatam āharatam | vaherloṭi chāndasaḥ śāpo luk | 'rathavattvadavalopeṣu kṛteṣu ‘sahibahorudavarṇastha’ ityotvam | kīdṛśaṃ rayim | 'gomantaṃ bahubhirgobhiryuktaṃ 'suvīram | vīryājāyanta iti vīrāḥ putrāḥ | śobhanaistairupetam | vividhamīrayanti śatrūniti vā vīrāḥ śūrāḥ | tairupetam | 'surathaṃ śobhanarathena yuktam | api ca 'aśvāvatīḥ aśvayuktāḥ | ‘mantre somāśva’ iti matau dīrghaḥ | 'iṣaḥ ipyamāṇānyannāni cāsmabhyāvahatam || ||2||
dvidevatyasyāśvinagrahasya ‘vāvṛdhānā’ ityeṣā yājyā |
sūyate hi—‘hotā yakṣadaśvinā nāsatyā vāvṛdhānā śubhaspatī’ (āśva. śrau. 5. 5) iti ||
vāvṛ̭dhānā śu॒bhaspa̭tī da॒strā hi॒ra॒ṇyava̭rtanī | pi॒ba̭ta॒ṃ somya॒ṃ madhṷ ||11|| va॒vṛ॒dhānā | śu॒bhaḥ | pa॒tī iti̭ | da॒strā | hi॒ra॒ṇyava̭rtanī iti hi॒ra॒ṇya'va̭rtanī | pi॒ba̭tam | somya̭m | madhṷ ||11||
he 'śubhaspatī śubhaḥ śobhanasyālaṃkārasyodakasya vā patī svāmī he aśvinau ||
‘subāmantrita’ iti ṣaṣṭhyantasya parāṅgavadbhāvāt ṣaṣṭhyāmantritasamudāyasyāṣṭāṅgikaṃ sarvānudāttatvam ||
1. ta-śubhaspatīti |
dastrā darśanīyau śatrūṇāmupakṣapayitārau vā hiraṇyavartanī hiraṇyamāgau | yadvā | vartate'sminniti vartanī rathaḥ | hiraṇyayo ratho yayostau | yadvā | vartani vartanamacaraṇam | hitaramaṇīyācaraṇau | vavṛdhānā pravṛddhau | vṛdherliṭ: kānac | īdṛśau yuvāṃ somyaṃ somamayaṃ madhu mādhuryopetaṃ madakaraṃ vā rasaṃ pibatam || asmabhyaṃ vājinīvasū maghavaddyaśca saprathaḥ | chardiyaintarmadābhyam || 12 || asmabhyam | vājinīvasū iti vājinīvasū | maghavat'bhyaḥ | ca | su'prathaḥ | chardiḥ | yantam | adābhyam || 12 || he vājinīvasū | vājinī haviryuktā yāgakriyā | tasyāṃ vasu dhanaṃ havirbhāgalakṣaṇaṃ yayostathā- vidhau he aśvinau asmabhyaṃ stotṛbhyaḥ maghavadbhyaḥ | maghaṃ dhanaṃ havirlakṣaṇam | taddvayo yajamānebhyaḥ ca saprathaḥ sarvataḥ pṛthu vistīrṇam adābhyaṃ kenāpyahiṃsyaṃ chardiḥ | gṛhaṇāmaitat | gṛhaṃ yantaṃ niyacchataṃ dattamiti yāvat || yameichāndasaḥ | śapo luk | 'chandasyubhayathā' ityārṣadhātukaravena ṅitvābhāvādamunāsikalopābhāvaḥ || ni ṣu brahma janānāṃ yāviṣṭhaṃ tūyamā gatam | mo ṣvnyā~ upāratam ||13|| ni | su | brahma | janānām | yā | aviṣṭham | tūyam | ā | gatam | mo iti | su | anyān | upa | aratam || 13 || he aśvinau yā yau yuvāṃ janānāṃ prāṇināṃ madhye brahma brāhmaṇajātiṃ su suṣṭhu ni nitarām aviṣṭham arakṣitam | yadvā | janānāṃ yajamānānāṃ brahma parivṛḍhaṃ stotraṃ havirlakṣaṇamaṃ vā yau yuvāṃ nyaviṣṭaṃ nyagacchatam | avatirgatyarthaḥ | tau yuvāṃ tūyaṃ kṣipram ā gatam asmānapyāgacchatam | anyān asmadyatiriktān yajamānān mo maiva su upāratam upagatam | kadācidapi maiva prāpnumityarthaḥ || artairmāṅi luṅi 'sartiśāsrtibhyaśca' iti chleraḍādeśaḥ || āprāhnike pravargye dharmasya haviṣaḥ : 'asya pibatama' iti dvitīyā yājyā | sūtritaṃ ca- 'asya pibatamaśvineti cāpreṣito hotā' (āśva. śrau. 4. 7) iti || asya pibatamaśvinā yuvaṃ madasya cāruṇaḥ | madhvo rātasya dhiṣṇyā ||14|| asya | pibatama | aśvinā | yuvam | madasya | cāruṇaḥ | madhvaḥ | rātasya | dhiṣṇyā || 14 || he aśvinā aśvinau he dhiṣṇyā | dhiṣaṇā stutiḥ | tadahau yuvaṃ yuvāṃ madasya madakarasya cāruṇaḥ śobhanasya rātasya asmābhirdattasya asya madhvaḥ madhurasya somasya svāṃśalakṣaṇaṃ bhāgaṃ pibatama | yadvā | dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī | imaṃ somaṃ pibatama || asme ā vaṃhataṃ rayiṃ śatavantaṃ sahasriṇam | purukṣuṃ viśvadhāyasam ||15|| asme iti | ā | vahatam | rayim | śata'vantam | sahasriṇam | puru'kṣum | viśva'dhāyasam ||15|| he aśvinau asme asmabhyaṃ rayiṃ dhanaṃ ā vahatam ānayatam | kathaṃbhūtam | śatavantaṃ śatasaṃkhyāopetaṃ sahasriṇaṃ sahasrasaṃkhyāopetaṃ ca purukṣuṃ bahunivāsaṃ yadvā purubhirbahubhiḥ stutyaṃ viśva- dhāyasaṃ viśveṣāṃ sarveṣāmasmadīyānāṃ dhārakam | 'vahihādhābhyaśchandasi' iti dadhāterasun | 'ṇit' ityanuvṛtterṇidbhāvena yugāgamaḥ || || 3 || 1. jha-ta3-vāgdhānā |
purutrā ciddhi vāṃ narā vihvayante manīṣiṇaḥ | vāghadbhirśvinā gatam ||16|| purutrā | cit | hi | vām | narā | vi'hvayante | manīṣiṇaḥ | vāghadbhiḥ | aśvinā | ā | gatam || 16 || he narā narau stotṛṇāṃ dhanasya netāvaśvinau manīṣiṇaḥ manasa īśitāraḥ stotāraḥ vāṃ yuvāṃ purutrā ciddhi bahuṣu hi deśeṣu vihvayante vividhamāhvayanti | tathā sati he aśvinau vāghadbhiḥ vāhakairaśvaiḥ ā gatam asmānevāgacchatam ||
janāso vṛktabarhiṣo haviṣmanto araṃkṛtaḥ | yuvāṃ havante aśvinā ||17|| janāsaḥ | vṛkta'barhiṣaḥ | haviṣmantaḥ | aram'kṛtaḥ | yuvām | havante | aśvinā || 17 || vṛktabarhiṣaḥ | vṛktaṃ chinnaṃ barhiryastai tathoktāḥ | haviṣmantaḥ havirbhiryuktāḥ | araṃkṛtaḥ paryāpta- kāriṇaḥ yadvā havirādīnāmalaṃkartāraḥ janāsaḥ ṛtviglakṣaṇā janā he aśvinā aśvinau yuvāṃ havante āhvayanti | ata āgacchatamiti śeṣaḥ ||
asmākamadya vāmayaṃ stomo vāhiṣṭho antam | yuvābhyāṃ bhūtadhvinā ||18|| asmākam | adya | vām | ayam | stomaḥ | vāhiṣṭhaḥ | antam | yuvābhyām | bhūtam | aśvinā || 18 || adya idānīm asmākam ayaṃ stomaḥ asmadīyaṃ stotraṃ he aśvinau vāṃ yuvayoḥ vāhiṣṭhaḥ vāhayitṛtamaḥ prāpayitṛtamaḥ san yuvābhyām antam antikatamaḥ atiśayena samīpavartī bhūta bhavatu | 'tame tādeśa' (pā. sū. 6. 4. 149. 1) ityantikaśabdasya tādirlupte | bhavateśchāndasaḥ śapo luk ||
yo ha vāṃ madhunā datirāhaṃto rathacarṣaṇe | tataḥ pibatamaśvinā ||19|| yaḥ | ha | vām | madhunā | datiḥ | ā'hitaḥ | ratha'carṣaṇe | tataḥ | pibatam | aśvinā || 19 || he aśvinā aśvinau rathacarṣaṇe rathasya carṣaṇe draṣṭavye madhye deśe yaḥ datiḥ madhunā madhurasyāsmābhirdattasya somasya saṃbandhī tena madhunā pūrṇaḥ āhitaḥ sthāpito vartate tataḥ dateḥ sakāśāt somaṃ pibatam ||
tena no vājinīvasū paśve tokāya śaṃ gave | vahataṃ pīvarīriṣaḥ || 20 || tena | naḥ | vājinīvasū iti vājinī'vasū | paśve | tokāya | śam | gave | vahatam | pīvarīḥ | iṣaḥ || 20 || he vājinīvasū | vājo'nnaṃ balaṃ vā | tadyuktadhanau naḥ asmākaṃ paśve paśave aśvādilakṣaṇāya || 'jasādiṣu chandasi vāvacanam' iti 'verḍiti' iti guṇasya vikalpitatvādyaṇ || 'tokāya putrāya gave ca | jātyabhiprāyaṃ sarvatraikavacanam | paśuprabhṛtibhyaḥ śaṃ sukhaṃ yathā bhavati tathā pīvarīḥ pravṛddhān iṣaḥ annāni tena bhavadīyena rathena vahatam āvahataṃ prāpayatam | dattamityarthaḥ || 4 ||
uta no divyā iṣa uta sindhūairahaviṃdā |
apu dvāreva varṣathaḥ ||

1. kha-bha-yuvābhyāṃ yuvayoḥ |
uta | naḥ | divyāḥ | iṣaḥ | uta | sindhūn | ahaḥ'vidā | arpa | dvārā'iva | varṣyaḥ ||21||
ma. 8 a. 1. sū. 5 ] pañcamo'ṣṭaka: 561
yathota kṛtyai dhane'śuṃ goṣvagastyam | yathā vājeṣu sobharim || 26 || yathā | uta | kṛtyai | dhane | aṃśam | goṣu | agastyam | yathā | vājeṣu | sobharim || 26 || 7uta api ca 7yathā yena prakāreṇa 7dhane 7kṛtye kartavye prāptavye sati 7aṃśam etatsaṃjñaṃ stotāramāvatamarakṣatam | 7goṣu ca labdhavyāsu 7yathā 7agastyam ṛṣimarakṣatam | 7vājeṣu ajñeṣu labdhavyeṣu yathā yena prakāreṇa 7sobharim etatsaṃjñamṛṣi cārakṣatam | atrāpi pūrvavadvākyaśeṣa uttarayaikavākyatā vā ||
etāvadvāṃ vṛṣaṇavasū atau vā bhūyau aśvinā | guṇantaḥ sunnamīmahe || 27 || etāvat | vām | vṛṣaṇavasū iti vṛṣaṇa'vasū | ataḥ | vā | bhūyaḥ | aśvinā | guṇantaḥ | sunnam | īmahe || 27 || he 7vṛṣaṇavasū varṣaṇadhanau 7aśvinā aśvinau 7guṇantaḥ stuvanto vayam 7etāvat ‘yathā cit kaṇvam’ ityādinā yāvadanukāntametatparimāṇaṃ 7sunnaṃ sukham 7ato 7vā asmādvā 7bhūyaḥ bahutara- madhikaṃ sunnaṃ 7vāṃ yuvām 7īmahe yācāmahe ||
rathaṃ hiraṇyavandhuraṃ hiraṇyābhīśamaśvinā | ā hi sthāthau divispṛśam ||28|| ratham | hiraṇya'vandhuram | hiraṇya'abhīśam | aśvinā | ā | hi | sthāthaḥ | divi'spṛśam ||28|| he 7aśvinā aśvinau 7hiraṇyavandhuram | sārathisthānaṃ vandhuram | hiraṇyamasārathisthānaṃ 7hiraṇyābhīśaṃ hiraṇyamayapragrahaṃ 7divispṛśam atyunnatatvādivaṃ spṛśantam | spṛśeḥ kin | divaśabdāddvi- tīyārthe saptamī | ‘hṛdaybhyāṃ ḍerupesaṃkhyānam’ ityaluk | īdṛśaṃ 7ratham 7ā 7hi 7sthāthaḥ yuvāmātiṣṭhatha eva | hiravadhāraṇe | asmadīyāṃ stutiṃ śrotuṃ śīghraṃ rathamāsthāyāgatamiti bhāvaḥ | ‘chā gatinivṛttau’ | laṭi chāndasaḥ śapo luk ||

sa tvaṃ vasu dhanaṃ coṣkūyase punaḥpunaḥ stotṛbhyo dadāsi |
skuñ āpravaṇe iha dānārthaḥ |
tathā coktaṃ— coṣkūyamāṇa indra bhūri vāmaṃ dadadindra bahu vananīyam (niru. 6. 22) iti ||
asmākaṃ tvā sutā~ upa vītapṛṣṭhā abhi prayaḥ | śataṃ vahantu harayaḥ ||42|| asmākam | tvā | sutān | upa | vīta'pṛṣṭhāḥ | abhi | prayaḥ | śatam | vahantu | harayaḥ || 42 ||
1. kha-sa-ta-na-bha-śa-vidyamāne |
2. kha-bha-vardhamānaḥ |
he indra 'asmākam asmadīyān' somānupalakṣya 'prayaḥ |
annāmaitat |
dhānākarambhādi- havilakṣaṇamacaṃ cābhilakṣya 'vītapṛṣṭhāḥ praśastoparibhāgāḥ 'śataṃ śatasaṃkhyākāḥ 'harayaḥ aśvāḥ tvāṃ 'vahantu prāpayantu ||
imāṃ su pūyāṃ dhiyaṃ madhorghṛtasya pipyuṣīm | kaṇvā ukthena vāvṛdhuḥ ||43|| imām | su | puṇyām | dhiyam | madhoḥ | ghṛtasya | pipyuṣīm | kaṇvāḥ | ukthena | vāvṛdhuḥ ||43||
'imām idānīṃ kriyamāṇāṃ 'su suṣṭhu 'pūyāṃ pūrvaiḥ pitrādibhiḥ kṛtāṃ 'madhoḥ madhurasya 'ghṛtasya kṣaraṇaśīlasyodakasya 'pipyuṣīṃ vardhayitrīṃ yadvā madhureṇa ghṛtenājyena pravṛddhāṃ 'dhiyaṃ yāgakriyāṃ 'kaṇvāḥ kaṇvagotrā ṛṣayaḥ 'ukthena śasreṇa 'vāvṛdhuḥ indrārthaṃ vardhayanti |
ukthairhi
sahasrāṇi ca pannāya stutīnāṃ prārjakāya sājhe etatsaṃjñāyarpaye | yadvā | sājhe | māma stotram | tadte pannāya pannakulajātāya kakṣīvate | vadu: tirindarākhyā rājāno dattavantaḥ ||

17 ||
'pra yadvaḥ' iti ṣaṭtriṃśadca dvitīyaṃ sūktaṃ kaṇvagotrasya punarvatsasyārṣaṃ mārutaṃ gāyatram |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—'pra yadvaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśatpunarvatso mārutam' iti |
vyūḍhe daśarātre prathame chandoma ābhi- mārutaśastra idaṃ sūktaṃ mārutanidvidvānam |
sutritaṃ ca—'pra yadvastriṣṭubhaṃ dūtaṃ va ityābhimārutam' (āśva. śrau. 8. 9) iti |
pra yadvastriṣṭubhamiṣaṃ maruto vipro akṣarata | vi parvateṣu rājatha || 1 || pra | yat | vaḥ | tri'stubham | iṣam | marutaḥ | vipraḥ | akṣarata | vi | parvateṣu | rājatha || 1 ||
he marutaḥ mitarāviṇo mitarocino vā etatsaṃjñā mādhyamakā devagaṇāḥ |
pādāditvāt apādādau iti paryudāsādāṣṭamikanighātābhāve pāṣṭikamāmantritādyudāttatvam |
vaḥ yuṣmabhyaṃ vipraḥ medhāvī stotā triṣṭubhaṃ triṣu savaneṣu praśasyāṃ tribhirdevaiḥ stutāṃ vā yadvā triṣṭupchandasā saṃbaddhāṃ mādhyaṃ- dinasavanikīm iṣaṃ somalakṣaṇamannaṃ yat yadā pra akṣarata prāsiñcat abhau prākṣipan |
yadvā |
triṣṭubhaṃ triṣṭupchandaskaṃ stotramiṣaṃ somaṃ ceti yojyam |
tadānīṃ yūyaṃ parvateṣu parvatavatsu śiloccayeṣu vi rājatha |
tena somena labdhabalāḥ santo viśeṣeṇa dīptā bhavatha |
yaduṅga tavipīyavo yāmaṃ śubhrā acidhvam | ni parvatā ahāsata || 2 || yat | aṅga | tavipī'yavaḥ | yāmam | śubhrāḥ | acidhvam | ni | parvatāḥ | ahāsata || 2 ||
he tavipīyavaḥ |
tavipīti balanāma |
tāṃ kāmayamānāḥ |

balayuktāḥ |
he śubhrāḥ śobha- mānāḥ aṅga he marutaḥ yāmam yāni gacchantīti yāmo rathaḥ |
taṃ yat yadā acidhvaṃ samacinudhvaṃ aśvādibhiḥ sādhanaiḥ saṃcitaṃ saṃśliṣṭaṃ kurutha gamanāthaṃ tadānīṃ parvatāḥ girayo'pi ni ahāsata nitarāṃ gacchanti yuṣmadrathavegādbhītāḥ santaḥ svasthānāt pracalanti |

udīrayanta vāyubhirvāśrāsaḥ pṛśnimātaraḥ | dhukṣanta pipyuṣīmiṣam || 3 || uta | īrayanta | vāyu'bhiḥ | vāśrāsaḥ | pṛśni'mātaraḥ | dhukṣanta | pipyuṣīm | iṣam || 3 ||

pṛśnirmādhyamikā vāk |

īdṛśā marutaḥ vāyubhiḥ |

1. jha-ta1-2-maruto maruto |
2. jha-ta1-2-mitarāviṇo vā |
3. jha-vā mahadravaṃtīti veti niruktaṃ; ta1 (prānte) -vā mahadravaṃtīti vā |
varpanti maruto mihaṃ pra vaipayanti parvatān | yadhāmaṃ yānti vāyubhiḥ ||4|| varpanti | marutaḥ | miham | pra | vepayanti | parvatān | yat | yāmam | yānti | vāyubhiḥ ||4||
ᵥmarutaḥ etatsaṃjñā devāḥ ᵥmihaṃ vṛṣṭiṃ ᵥvapanti vikiranti vikṣipanti |
tathā ᵥparvatān girīn ᵥpra ᵥvepayanti prakampayanti |
ayamarthaḥ kadeti cet |
ᵥyat yadā ᵥvāyubhiḥ sārdhaṃ ᵥyāmaṃ rathaṃ gamanaṃ vā ᵥyānti prāpnuvanti tadānīmityarthaḥ ||
ni yadhāmāya vo girini sindhavo vidharmaṇe |
mahe śuṣmāya yemire ||
5||
ni | yadhāmāya | vaḥ | giriḥ | ni | sindhavaḥ | vi'dharmaṇe | mahe | śuṣmāya | yemire ||5||
he marutaḥ ᵥvaḥ yuṣmākaṃ ᵥyāmāya rathāya gamanāya vā ᵥgiriḥ |
‘supāṃ suluk’ ’ iti jasaḥ suḥ |
girayaḥ parvatāḥ ᵥyat yadā ᵥni ᵥyemire svayameva niyamyante |
tathā ᵥsindhavaḥ syandanaśīlāḥ samudrā nadyo vā ᵥvidharmaṇe vidharaṇāya ᵥmahe mahate ᵥśuṣmāya śoṣakāya yuṣmadīyāya balāya ᵥni yemire svayameva niyamyante |

yenāva turvaśaṃ yaduṃ yen kaṇvaṃ dhanuspṛtam | rāye su tasya dhīmahi || 18 || yena | āva | turvaśam | yadum | yena | kaṇvam | dhanu'spṛtam | rāye | su | tasya | dhīmahi ||18|| 'yena' ātmīyena rakṣaṇena 'turvaśam etatsaṃjñaṃ 'yadum etatsaṃjñaṃ ca rājarṣim 'āva yūyaṃ rakṣa- tavantaḥ stha | avaterliṭi madhyamabahuvacane rūpametat | 'yena ca 'dhanuspṛtaṃ dhanakāmaṃ 'kaṇvam ṛṣiṃ rakṣitavantaḥ stha 'tasya yuṣmadīyaṃ rakṣaṇaṃ 'rāye dhanārthaṃ 'su 'dhīmahi śobhanaṃ dhyāyāma || imā u vaḥ sudānavo ghṛtaṃ na pipyuṣīriṣaḥ | vardhīnkāṇvasya manmabhiḥ ||19|| imāḥ | ū~ iti | vaḥ | su'dānavaḥ | ghṛtam | na | pipyuṣīḥ | iṣaḥ | vardhīn | kāṇvasya | manmabhiḥ || 19 || he 'sudānavaḥ śobhanadānā marutaḥ 'ghṛtaṃ 'na ghṛtamiva 'pipa

' ā nūnam ' ityekaviṃśatyṛcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ śaśakaṇasyārṣamaśvidevatyam |
viṃśayekaviṃśayau dvitīyā- tṛtīye ceti catasro gāyatryaḥ prathamā caturthī pañcadaśī pañcadaśī ceti pañca bṛhatyaḥ pañcamī kakup ' madhyamaścetakap ' (anu. 5. 3) ityuktalakṣaṇasaṃjñātvāt |
daśamī triṣṭubekādaśī virāḍdvādaśī jagatī śiṣṭā anuṣṭubhaḥ |
tathā cānukrāntam— ' ā nūnaṃ saikā śaśakaṇo'nye gāyatryāvupāce cādyā caturthī ṣaṣṭhī caturdaśyāce ca bṛhatyaḥ pañcamī kakubdāśyādyāstriṣṭubbirāḍjagattyaḥ ' iti |
asoryāme brāhmaṇācchaṃsino'tiriktauthyaṃ idaṃ sūktam |
sūyate hi— ' ā nūnamaśvinā taṃ vāṃ ratham ' (ābha. śrau. 9. 11) iti ||
ā nūnamaśvinā yuvaṃ vṛtasya gantavase | prāsmai yacchatamavukaṃ pṛthu cchardiryutaṃ yā arātayaḥ || 1 || ā | nunam | aśvinā | yuvam | vṛtasya | gantam | avase | pra | asmai | yacchatam | avukam | pṛthu | chardiḥ | yutam | yāḥ | arātayaḥ || 1 ||
he aśvinā aśvinau yuvaṃ yuvāṃ vṛtasya stotumama avase rakṣaṇārthaṃ nūnam avaśyam ā gantam āgacchatam |
āgatya ca asmai ṛṣaye avukaṃ bādhakarahitaṃ pṛthu vistīrṇaṃ chardiḥ gṛhaṃ pra yacchataṃ pradattam |
tathā yāḥ arātayaḥ adānaśīlāḥ śatrubhūtāḥ prajāstāḥ yuyutaṃ stotumyaḥ pṛthakkurutam ||
yadantarikṣe yadivi yatpaśva mānuṣaॉ anu | nṛmṇaṃ tadvattamaśvinā || 2 || yat | antarikṣe | yat | divi | yat | pañca | mānuṣān | anu | nṛmṇam | tat | dhattam | aśvinā || 2 ||
antarikṣe gandharvādibhiḥ sevite madhyame loke yat nṛmṇaṃ dhanamasti |
divi dyuloke ca yat asti |
pañca pañcasaṃkhyākān mānuṣān manuṣyān anu |
lakṣaṇe'noḥ karmapravacanīyatvam |
' karmapravacanīyayukte ' iti dvitīyā |
pañcavidhā manuṣyā niṣādapañcamāścatvāro varṇā yatra vartante tatra ceha loke yat nṛmṇaṃ dhanamasti |
he aśvinau tat trividhaṃ nṛmṇaṃ dhanaṃ dhattam asmabhyaṃ pratyacchatam ||
ye vāṃ daṃsāṃsyaśvinā viprāsaḥ parimamṛśuḥ | evetkāṇvasya bodhatam || 3 || ye | vām | daṃsāṃsi | aśvinā | viprāsaḥ | pari | mamṛśuḥ | eva | ita | kāṇvasya | bodhatam || 3 ||
he aśvinau vāṃ yuvayoḥ sambandhīni daṃsāṃsi karmāṇi paricaraṇātmakāni ye viprāsaḥ viprā medhāvino yajamānāḥ parimamṛśuḥ parimṛśanti punaḥpunaḥ spṛśanti |
anutiṣṭhantītyarthaḥ |
yathā tadīyāni paricaraṇāni yuvāṃ jānīthaḥ evet evameva kāṇvasya kaṇvaputrasya mama paricaraṇaṃ bodhatam avagacchatam ||
1. kha-bha- tadānīṃtanāni; jha- tadādīnāni; ta- tadīnāni; na- tadānāni |
ayaṃ vāṃ gharmo aśvinā stomena pariṃ sicyate | ayaṃ somo madhumānvājinīvasū yenaṃ vṛtraṃ ciketathaḥ ||4|| ayama | vām | gharmaḥ | aśvinā | stomena | pariṃ | sicyate | ayama | somaḥ | madhu'mān | vājinī'vasū iti vājinī'vasū | yena | vṛtram | ciketathaḥ ||4||
he aśvinau vāṃ yuvayoḥ sambandhī ayaṃ gharmaḥ pravargya stomena stotreṇa ṛksāmarūpeṇa pari sicyate ārdrīkriyate |
yathā yuvayostṛptikaro bhavati tathā kriyata ityarthaḥ |
yadvā |
gharmasya haviṣa ādhārabhūto mahāvīro gharmaḥ |
sa stomena stotratvena payasā pari sicyate āsicyate vāṃ yuvayortham |
api ca he vājinīvasū annavaddhanau ayaṃ somaḥ tātīyasavaniko madhumān mādhuryavān yuvābhyāṃ dīyate |
yena yuvāṃ vṛtram āvarakaṃ śatruṃ ciketathaḥ hantavyatayā jānīthaḥ |
ayaṃ gharmaḥ somaścetyubhayānvayaḥ ||
yadapsu yadvanaspatau yadoṣadhīṣu purudaṃsā kṛtam | tena māviṣṭamaśvinā ||5|| yat | apsu | yat | vanaspatau | yat | oṣadhīṣu | puru'daṃsā | kṛtam | tena | mā | aviṣṭam | aśvinā ||5||
he purudaṃsā bahukarmāṇavaśvinau apsu udakeṣu yat bheṣajaṃ kṛtaṃ yuvāmakāraṣṭam |
karoterluṅi 'mantre ghasa' ityādinā caleluk |
tathā vanaspatau vanānāṃ patirvanaspatiḥ |
pāraskarāditvāt suṭ |
'ubhe vanaspatyādiṣu' iti pūrvottarapadayoryugapatprakṛtisvaratvam |
jātau ceda- mekavacanam |

oṣadhīṣu oṣaḥ pāka āsu dhīyate ityoṣadhayo vrīhādayaḥ ||
'karmaṇyadhikaraṇe ca' iti dadhāteradhikaraṇe kipratyayaḥ |

tvama̭gne॒ vrata॒pā a̭si॒ deva॒ ā ma॒rtyḙṣvā | tvaṃ ya॒jñeṣvīḍya̭ḥ || 1 || tvam | agne | vrata॒pāḥ | a॒si॒ | devaḥ | ā | ma॒rtyḙṣu | ā | tvam | ya॒jñeṣu | īḍyaḥ || 1 || he agne deva dyotamānaḥ tvaṃ martyeṣu manuṣyeṣu ca deveṣu ca madhye vratapāḥ asi | vratānāṃ karmaṇāṃ rakṣitā bhavasi | ataḥ kāraṇāt yajñeṣu tvam īḍyaḥ stutyo'si ||

yadindrāṃha' iti pañcadaśarcaṃ dvitīyaṃ sūktaṃ gopūkyaśvasūkinoḥ kāṇvagotrayorārṣaṃ gāyatra- maindram |

vaheriti yajāditvāt saṃprasāraṇam ||
vibhūtarātiṃ vipra citraśauciṣamagmīḻiṣva yanturam |
asya medhasya somyasya sobhare premadhvarāya pūrvyam ||
2 ||
vibhūta'rātim | vipra | citra'śauciṣam | agnim | īḻiṣva | yanturam | asya | medhasya | somyasya | sobhare | pra | īm | adhvarāya | pūrvyam || 2 ||
ṛṣirātmānaṃ saṃbodhya stutau prerayati |
he ᵥvipra medhāvin ᵥsobhare etatsaṃjña ṛṣe ᵥadhvarāya yāgāya ᵥīm imam ᵥagniṃ ᵥpra ᵥīḻiṣva prastuhi |
kīdṛśam |
ᵥvibhūtarātiṃ vyāptadhanaṃ prabhūtadānaṃ vā ᵥcitraśauciṣaṃ cāyanīyatejaskaṃ vicitradīsikaṃ vā ᵥsomyasya somasādhyasya ᵥasya ᵥmedhasya yajñasya ᵥyanturaṃ niyantāraṃ ᵥpūrvyaṃ ciraṃtanam ||
ābhiplāvikeṣvāgneyeṣu tṛtīyasavane praśāstuḥ śāstre 'yajiṣṭhaṃ tvā' ityādikau pragāthau vaikalpiko stotriyānurūpau |
sūtritaṃ ca—'yajiṣṭhaṃ tvā vavamahe yaḥ samidhā ya āhutī' (ādha. śrau. 7. 8) iti ||
yajiṣṭhaṃ tvā vavamahe devaṃ devatrā hotāramamartyam |
asya yajñasya sukratum ||
3 ||
yajiṣṭham | tvā | vavamahe | devam | deva'trā | hotāram | amartyam | asya | yajñasya | su'kratum || 3 ||
he agne ᵥyajiṣṭhaṃ yaṣṭatamaṃ ᵥtvā ᵥvavamahe vṛṇīmahe saṃbhajāmahe |
kīdṛśaṃ tvām |
ᵥdevatrā deveṣu madhye ᵥdevam atiśayena dānādiguṇayuktaṃ ᵥhotāraṃ devānāmāhvātāram ᵥamartyam avināśam ᵥasya ᵥyajñasya yāgasya ᵥsukratuṃ suṣṭhu kartāram ||
ūrjo napātaṃ subhagaṃ sudīditimagniṃ śreṣṭhaśociṣam |
sa nau mitrasya varuṇasya so apāmā sumnaṃ yakṣate divi ||
4 ||
ūrjaḥ | napātam | su'bhagam | su'dīditim | agnim | śreṣṭha'śociṣam | saḥ | naḥ | mitrasya | varuṇasya | saḥ | apām | ā | sumnam | yakṣate | divi || 4 ||
ᵥūrjaḥ annasya ᵥnapātaṃ na pātayitāram |
yadvā |
nasāraṃ caturthyam |
havilakṣaṇenānnenāpo jāyante agniśśauciṣavanaspatebhya eṣa jāta iti caturthyam |
'annānnapāt' iti nañaḥ prakṛtibhāvaḥ |
ᵥsubhagaṃ śobhanadhanaṃ ᵥsudīditiṃ suṣṭhu dīpayitāraṃ ᵥśreṣṭhaśociṣaṃ praśastamatejaskam ᵥagniṃ staumīti śeṣaḥ |
ᵥsaḥ tādṛśo'gniḥ ᵥnaḥ asmadarthaṃ ᵥdivi dyotamāne devajane dhuloke vā ᵥmitrasya devasya ᵥvaruṇasya ca ᵥsumnaṃ sukham ᵥā abhilakṣya ᵥyakṣate yajatu |
tathā ᵥsaḥ agniḥ ᵥapām adevatānāṃ sumnamabhiyajatu ||
yaḥ samidhā ya āhutī yo vedena dadāśa matau agnaye |
yo namasā svadhvaraḥ ||
5 ||
yaḥ | sam'idhā | yaḥ | ā'hutī | yaḥ | vedena | dadāśa | matau | agnaye | yaḥ | namasā | su'adhvaraḥ || 5 ||
1. ga-na-ma-tvāṃ; gha-tvā tvā | 2. gha-na-ma-avināśinaṃ | 3. bha-jāyata | 4. ga-gha-na-ma-dīpayaṃta |
iyaṃ pākyajñapraśaṃsāpareti bhagavatāśvalāyanena brāhmaṇāntarapradarśanena vyākhyātā (āśva. gṛ. 1. 1.4-5) |
yaḥ mataḥ manuṣyaḥ samidhā pālāśādinedhmena apraye adhyarthaṃ dadāśa paricarati |
yaḥ ca āhutī āhutyā ājyādisādhyayā paricarati |
yaḥ ca vedena vedādhyayanena brahmayajñena paricarati |
yaḥ ca svadhvaraḥ śobhanenādhvareṇa jyotiṣṭomādinā yuktaḥ san namasā agnena carupuroḍāśādinābhaye dadāśa agnyartha paricarati |
tasyedvanta ityuttaratra saṃbandhaḥ ||
29 ||
tasyedvanto raṃhayanta āśvastasya ghubhritaṃ yaśaḥ | na tamaṃho devakṛtaṃ kutaścana na martyakṛtaṃ naśat || 6 || tasya | it | arventaḥ | raṃhayante | āśvaḥ | tasya | ghubhrita'tamam | yaśaḥ | na | tam | ahaḥ | deva'kṛtam | kutaḥ | cana | na | martya'kṛtam | naśat || 6 || yaḥ pūrvoktaḥ tasyeta tasyaiva āśvaḥ vyāpanaśīlāḥ arventaḥ aśvāḥ raṃhayante vegaṃ kurvanti | śatrūn prasahanta ityarthaḥ | ghubhritamaṃ dīptimattamaṃ yaśaḥ kīrtiśca tasya eva bhavati | yadvā | ghubhrimiti dhananāma | dhanavattamaṃ yaśo'nnaṃ ca tasya bhavati | api ca devakṛtam devaiḥ kṛtam ahaḥ pāpaṃ kutaścana kasmādapi hetoḥ taṃ na naśat na prāpnoti | na martyakṛtaṃ manuṣyaiḥ kṛtam ||
svamaryo vo agnibhiḥ syārma sūno sahasa urjāṃ pate | suvīrastvamasmayuḥ || 7 || su'agnayaḥ | vaḥ | agni'bhiḥ | syārma | sūno iti | sahasaḥ | ūrjām | pate | su'vīraḥ | tvam | asma'yuḥ || 7 || he sahasaḥ sūno balasya putra | agnibhiḥ balena madhyamāno jāyate | he ūrjāṃ pate annānāṃ havirlakṣaṇānāṃ svāminnagne vaḥ | vacanavyatyayaḥ | tavāvayavabhūtaiḥ agnibhiḥ gārhapatyādibhirvayaṃ svaprayaḥ śobhanāprikāḥ | syāma bhavema | suvīraḥ śobhanairvīrairupetaḥ tvaṃ ca asmayuḥ asmān kāmayamāno bhava ||
praśaṃsamāno atithirna mitriyo'bhī ratho na vedyaḥ | tve kṣemāso api santi sādhavastvaṃ rājā rayīṇām || 8 || pra'śaṃsamānaḥ | atithiḥ | na | mitriyaḥ | agniḥ | rathaḥ | na | vedyaḥ | tve iti | kṣemāsaḥ | api | santi | sādhavaḥ | tvam | rājā | rayīṇām || 8 || praśaṃsamānaḥ stuvan atithirna atithiriva | yadvā | vyatyayena karmaṇi kartṛpratyayaḥ | praśasya- mānaḥ saḥ agniḥ mitriyaḥ mitrāṇāṃ stotṝṇāṃ hito bhavati | tathā ratho na ratha iva vedyaḥ labhanīyo'bhīṣṭaphalasādhanatvena jñātavyo vā | uttaro'rdharcaḥ pratyakṣakṛtaḥ | he agne tve tvayi sādhavaḥ sādhakāḥ samīcīnāḥ kṣemāsaḥ dhāraṇāni api santi bhavanti | tathā rayīṇāṃ dhanānāṃ tvam eva rājā īśvaro bhavasi |

1. gha-na-sthirā sthirāṇi
yo havyānairayat manurhito deva āsā sugandhinā | vivāsate vāryāṇi svadhvaro hotā devo amartyaḥ || 24 || yaḥ | havyāni | airayat | manuḥ'hitaḥ | devaḥ | āsā | su'gandhinā | vivāsate | vāryāṇi | su'adhvaraḥ | hotā | devaḥ | amartyaḥ || 24 || yaḥ | manurhitaḥ manunā prajāpatināhitaḥ | devaḥ dyotamāno'gniḥ | sugandhinā śobhanagandha- yukena | āsā āsyena | havyāni asmadīyāni havīṃṣi | airayat devānprati prerayati | svadhvaraḥ śobhana- yajñaḥ | hotā devānāmāhvātā | devaḥ dīpyamānaḥ | amartyaḥ maraṇarahitaḥ so'gniḥ | vāryāṇi varaṇīyāni dhanāni | vivāsate paricarate | yajamānāya prayacchatīti śeṣaḥ ||

| su'havā | rudravartanī iti rudra'vartanī | ā | sūryāyai | tasthathuḥ || 1 || he aśvinau ᐯdaṃsiṣṭham atyantadarśanīyaṃ yadvātiśayena śatrūṇāmupakṣapayitāraṃ ᐯtyaṃ taṃ yuvayoḥ ᐯratham ᐯūtaye rakṣaṇāya yadadhi̭gā॒vo a॒dhi̭gū i॒dā ci॒dahaṷ a॒śvinā̭ ha॒vāma̭he | va॒yaṃ gī॒rbhirvvi̭panya॒vaḥ ||11|| yat | a॒dhi̭'gā॒vaḥ | a॒dhi̭gū i॒tyadhi̭'gū | i॒dā | ci॒t | a॒haḥ | a॒śvinā̭ | ha॒vāma̭he | va॒yam | gīḥ'bhiḥ | vi॒pa॒nya॒vaḥ || 11 ||
˅adhigāvaḥ aśatagamanāḥ karmasu tvaramāṇāḥ ˅vipanyavaḥ medhāvinaḥ ˅vayam ˅adhigū aśata- gamanau saṃgrāme śatrūvadhārthaṃ tvarayā gacchantau ˅aśvinā aśvinau yuvām ˅ahaḥ divasasya ˅idā ˅cit idānīmeva prātaḥkāle ˅gīrbhiḥ stutilakṣaṇābhirvāgbhiḥ ˅yat yadā ˅havāmahe yuvāmāhvayāmastadā tābhirūtibhirasmānāgacchatamityuttaratra saṃbandhaḥ ||
tābhi̭ryā॒taṃ vṛ॒ṣaṇo̭rpa me॒ havai̭ vi॒śvapsṷ̄ vi॒śvavā̭ryam | i॒ṣā maṃhi̭ṣṭhā puru॒bhūta̭mā na॒rā yā॒bhiḥ krivi̭ṃ vā॒vadhṷstābhi̭rā ga̭tam ||12|| tābhiḥ | ā | yā॒tam | vṛ॒ṣaṇaṷ | u॒pa | me॒ | hava̭m | vi॒śva'psṷ̄m | vi॒śva'vā̭ryam | i॒ṣā | maṃhi̭ṣṭhā | pu॒ru॒'bhṷ̄tamā | na॒rā | yā॒bhiḥ | krivi̭m | va॒vadhṷḥ | tābhi̭ḥ | ā | ga॒tam ||12||
˅vṛṣaṇā vṛṣaṇau varṣaṇaśīlau he aśvinau ˅viśvapsum |
psuriti rūpanāma |
stotraśastrātmakatvena nānārūpaṃ ˅viśvavāryaṃ sarvairdevairvvaraṇīyaṃ ˅me madīyaṃ ˅havaṃ yuṣmadviṣayamāhvānam ˅ā abhimukhīkṛtya ˅tābhiḥ ūtibhiḥ ˅upa ˅yātaṃ yuvāmāgacchatam |
yābhiḥ ˅narā narau sarvasya netārauvaśvinau ˅iṣā |
‘iṣa icchāyām’ |
havīṃṣīcchantau ˅maṃhiṣṭhā maṃhiṣṭhāvatiśayena dhanānāṃ dātārau ˅purubhūtamā purubhūtabhau yuddheṣvatyantaṃ purudhā bhavantau bahudhātiśayena bahūnāṃ śatrūṇāmabhibhavitārau bhavantau ˅kriviṃ kūpaṃ prati ˅yābhiḥ ūtibhirudakāni ˅vāvadhuḥ avardhayata |

tatra ^vaḥ : sarveṣābheva yuṣmākamarthāya ^nṛtamāya sarveṣāṃ netṛtamāya yadvā saṃgrāmeṣvā- yudhādīnāṃ netṛtamāya ^dhṛṣṇave śatrūṇāṃ gharṣaṇaśīlāya tasmā indrāyahāmeva ^su suṣṭhu ^stuṣe staumi ||

anu pūrvāṇyokyā sāmrājyasya saścima | mitrasya vratā varuṇasya dīrghaśrut ||17|| anu | pūrvāṇi | okyā | sām'rājyasya | saścima | mitrasya | vratā | varuṇasya | dīrgha'śrut || 17 ||
ᵥsāmrājyasya | samrājo bhāva: sāmrājyam | sāmrājyamasyāstīti sāmrājya: | ‘arśa- ādibhya: ’ ityacpratyaya: | sāmrājyavato varuṇasya ᵥpūrvāṇi purātanāni ᵥokyā | oko gṛham | tasmai hitāni karmāṇi vayaṃ ᵥ
tatsūryaṃ rodasī ubhe doṣā vastorupa bruve | bhojeṣvā abhyucara sadā || 21 || tat | sūryam | rodasī iti | ubhe iti | doṣā | vastauḥ | upa | bruve | bhojeṣu | asmān | abhi | ut | cara | sadā || 21 || ^sūrya suvīrya ^tat vāruṇaṃ maitraṃ ca teja: ^ubhe ^rodasī ubhe dyāvāpṛthivyau ca ^doṣā'| 'supāṃ suluk' iti dvitīyāyā luk | 'kālādhvanoḥ' iti dvitīyā | rātrau ^vastauḥ^2 ahani cāham ^upa ^bruve upastaumi | tvaṃ stūyamāno varuṇa: ^bhojeṣu dātṛṣu ^asmān ^sadā ^abhyucara sarvadābhimukhaṃ preraya | dātṛṣveteṣāṃ dāneṣvasmān purobhāvina: kurvityartha: ||
ṛjramukṣaṇyāyane rajataṃ harayāṇe | rathaṃ yuktamasanama suṣāmaṇi || 22 || ṛjram | ukṣaṇyāyane | rajatam | harayāṇe | ratham | yuktam | asanāma | su'sāmani || 22 || suṣāmaṇa: putro varuṇāna rājā | sa yahānaṃ prādāt viśvamanase tadasau viśvamanā ṛviranayā bhācaṣṭe | ^ukṣaṇyāyane | ukṣanāmā kaścidraro: pūrvaja: | tasya gotrāpatya ukṣaṇaśabdo'patya: | tadantāt phakpratyaya: | 'gau chāndasau | vṛddhibhāvo'pi 'saṃjñāpūrvako vidhiranitya: ' iti na bhavati | tasya gotrāpatye ^harayāṇe śatrujīvitaiśvaryādiharaṇaśīlathāne etādṛśe ^suṣāmaṇi | bahuvapitṛśabdena putro'bhidhīyate | suṣāmaṇa: putre varau rājani dadati sati kimabhūta | ^ṛjram ṛjugāminaṃ ^rajataṃ rajatamatyaṃ rajatasadṛśaṃ vā ^yuktam aśvābhyāṃ yuktaṃ ^ratham ^asanama | eteṣāṃ mitrādīnāṃ3 prasādāt vayaṃ saṃbhaktavanto labdhavanto'bhūma ||

1. gha-ta1-na-bha2-3-purovartinaṃ; jha-ta1-2-yāgapurovartinaṃ; ta4-bha1-gapurovartinaṃ|

1. gha-varṣatārā | 2. gha-vadhūriva | 3. bha-navaveṇa | 4. ta-bha-yajñaṃ samāpti |
he narā narau sarvasya netārauśvinau havānāṃ stotṛṇāṃ stotrāṇāṃ madhye stomaḥ bāhiṣṭhaḥ yuvāmatiśayena vyāpanuvan madīyaḥ stomaḥ dūtaḥ dūtabhūtaḥ san huvat āhvayatu |
so'yaṃ madīyaḥ stomaḥ yuvābhyāṃ priyakaraḥ bhūtu bhavatu ||

'sarvapratipadikebhyo lālasāyāṃ suk' (kā. 7.1.51.4) iti sugāgamaḥ |
tvaṣṭurjāmātaḥ brahmaṇo jāmātaḥ |
eṣā kathetihāsādibhiravagantavyā |
tādṛśa adbhuta mahan vicitrakarman he vāyo tava tvadīyāni avāṃrsi pālanāni ā vṛṇīmahe vayamasmin paśūyāge saṃbhajāmahe ||
tvaṣṭurjāmātaṃraṃ vayamīśānaṃ rāya īṃmahe | sutārvanto vāyuṃ yumnā janāsaḥ || 22 || tvaṣṭuḥ | jāmātaram | vayam | īśānam | rāyaḥ | īṃmahe | suta'vantaḥ | vāyum | yumnā | janāsaḥ || 22 ||
ime janāsaḥ janāḥ vayaṃ tvaṣṭuḥ brahmaṇaḥ jāmātaram īśānaṃ sarveśvarametādṛśaṃ vāyuṃ sutavantaḥ abhiṣutasomāḥ rāyaḥ dhanam īṃmahe yācāmahe |

vāyo yāhi śivā divo vahastvā su svaśvaryam | vahasva mahaḥ pṛthupakṣā rathe || 23 || vāyo iti | yāhi | śiva | ā | divaḥ | vahasva | su | su'aśvaryam | vahasva | mahaḥ | pṛthu'pakṣasā | rathe || 23 ||

tatastvaṃ svaśvaryam |
aśvānāṃ saṃgho'śvyaḥ |

idānīṃ tebhyo'pi samarthāvaśvāvāhaha |
mahaḥ mahāstvaṃ pṛthupakṣā pṛthupārśvadvayayuktāvaśvāṃ rathe svakīye vahasva śatruhananārthaṃ saṃyojaya ||
vāyavye paśau puroḍāśahavidhaḥ 'tvāṃ hi supparastamam' iti dve anuvākye |
sūtritaṃ ca-'tvāṃ hi supparastamamiti dve kuvidaṅga namasā ye vṛdhāsaḥ' (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti ||
tvāṃ hi supparastamaṃ nuṣardaneṣu humhe | grāvāṇaṃ nāśvaṃpṛṣṭhaṃ maṃhanā || 24 || tvām | hi | supparaḥ'tamam | nu'sardaneṣu | humhe | grāvāṇam | na | aśvaṃ'pṛṣṭham | maṃhanā || 24 ||

ᵛyaḥ yajamānaḥ sakṛt ᵛyajāti yāgaṃ karoti havirbhirdevān pūjayati sa devebhyo labdhadhanā- dikaḥ san ᵛyajāta ᵛit punarabhīṣṭaprāptaye devebhyo havīṃṣi prayacchatyeva |

na nūnaṃ brahmaṇārpaṇaṃ prāśūnāmasti sunvatām | na somo̭ a॒pratā॒ papḙ || 16 || na | nūna̭m | bra॒hmaṇa̭ḥ'a॒rpam | prā॒śūnā̭m | a॒sti | su॒nvatā̭m | na | soma̭: | a॒pratā̭ | papḙ || 16 || ^prāśūnām | ye somaṃ prāpnuvanti te prāśavaḥ | teṣāṃ somaṃ ^sunvatāṃ ^brahmaṇaṃ brāhmaṇānām ^ṛṇaṃ devarṇaṃ ^na ^nūnam ^asti na khalu vidyate | tathā ca śrūyate— 'eṣa vā aṇuṇo yaḥ putrī yajvā brahmacārīvāsī' (tai. saṃ. 6. 3. 10. 5) iti | kiṃca ^apratā avistīrṇadhanena ^somaḥ ^na ^pape na pīyate | prabhūtadhanenaiva somaḥ pīyata ityarthaḥ ||
pa॒nya i̭du॒rpa gā̭ya॒ta pa॒nya u॒kthāni̭ śaṃsata | bra॒hmā kṛ̭ṇota॒ pa॒nya i̭t || 17 || pa॒nyaḥ | it | u॒rpa | gā॒ya॒ta | pa॒nyaḥ | u॒kthāni̭ | śa॒ṃsa॒ta | bra॒hmā | kṛ॒ṇo॒ta | pa॒nyaḥ | it || 17 || he upagātāraḥ ^panya ^it stutya evendre ^upa ^gāyata upagānaṃ kuruta | kiṃca ^panye^2 evendre ^ukthāni stotrāṇi ^śaṃsata | he stotāra iti śeṣaḥ | ^panye ^it stutya evendre brahmaṇyanyāni stotrāṇi ^kṛṇota kuruta ||
pa॒nya ā da̭di॒raccha॒tā sa॒hasrā̭ vā॒jyarva̭taḥ | i॒ndro yo ya॒rva̭no vṛ॒dhaḥ || 18 || pa॒nyaḥ | ā | da॒di॒rat | śa॒tā | sa॒hasrā̭ | vā॒jī | arva̭taḥ | i॒ndraḥ | yaḥ | ya॒rva̭naḥ | vṛ॒dhaḥ || 18 || ^yaḥ ^vājī balavān ^śatā vīrāṇāṃ śatāni ^sahasrā sahasrāṇi ca ^ā ^dadi॒rat ābhi- mukhyena dārayati so'yam ^indraḥ śatrubhiḥ ^arvataḥ ^panyaḥ stutyo bhavati | ^yajvanaḥ vidhiniṣṭavato yajamānasya ^vṛdhaḥ vardhayitā ca bhavati ||
vi pū॒ cara̭ sva॒dhā anṷ kṛ॒ṣṭīnā̭ma॒nvāhṷvaḥ | i॒ndra pi̭ba su॒tānā̭m || 19 || vi | su | cara̭ | sva॒dhā | anṷ | kṛ॒ṣṭīnā̭m | anṷ | ā॒'hu॒vaḥ | i॒ndra | pi॒ba | su॒tānā̭m || 19 ||
1. kha-ta-bha-ābhimukhyeta netāraṃ |
yacchaṃtamityarthaḥ; ga-āyāmavantaṃ; gha-sarvato niyāmakaṃ |
2. jha- ta1-2-evendre tasmin sāmāni |
3. kha-ta-bha-panye stutye |
4. gha-pustake'sya mantrasya bhāṣyameva vidyate-he upagātāraḥ panya it stutya eva indrastasmin sāmāni upagāyata |
he hotāraḥ panye tasminnindre ukthāni stotrāṇi śaṃsata |
he adhvaryavaḥ panye it tasminnevendre brahmā ajñāni kṛṇota kuruta juhuta |
5. ga-ta-na-bha-āvṛttaḥ |
he ᵥindra ᵥāhuva: āhvātavyastvaṃ ᵥkṛṣṭīnāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ ᵥsvadhā: havīṃṣi ᵥanu ᵥsu suṣṭhu ᵥvi ᵥcara|
dvitīya: ᵥanu pūraṇa:|
ᵥsutānām abhiputān somāṃśca ᵥpiba ||
piba svadhainavāmṛta yastudhye sarcā| utāyamindra yastarva || 20 || piba| svaᵥdhainavāṇām| uta| ya:| tudhye| sarcā| uta| ayam| indra| ya:| tarva ||20|| he ᵥindra ᵥsvadhainavānāṃ svadhainavān svabhūtapayaso dheno: saṃbandhana: somān| dhenvā krītāni- sthyartha:| tathā ca śrūyate—‘dhenvā krīṇāti’ (tai. saṃ. 6. 1. 10. 2) iti| ᵥuta api ca ᵥya soma: ᵥtudhye udake| ‘bulaṃ tubhyam’ ityudakanāmasu pāṭhāt| ᵥsacā saṃsṛṣṭa: tamapi somaṃ ᵥpiba| ᵥuta api ca ᵥya: soma: ᵥtava tvadīya: tvāmuddiśya gṛhīta: sa: ᵥayaṃ tvayā pātavya iti śeṣa: || 4 ||
atīhi manyuṣāviṇaṃ suṣuvāṃsamupāraṇa| imaṃ rātaṃ sutaṃ piba || 21 || ati| ihi| manyuᵥsāvinam| susuᵥvāṃsam| upaᵥaraṇe| imam| rātam| sutam| piba ||21|| he indra ᵥmanyuṣāviṇaṃ krodhena somaṃ sunvantam ᵥatīhi atigaccha| tathā ᵥupāraṇe| brāhmaṇā upetya yasmin deśe na ramante sa upāraṇa:| tasmin deśe ᵥsupuvāṃsaṃ sunvantam atīhi| ᵥimaṃ ᵥrātaṃ brāhmaṇopadravarahite deśe asmābhirdattamitaṃ ᵥsutaṃ somaṃ ᵥpiba ||
ihi tisra: parāvata ihi pañca janā~ ati| dhenā indrāvacārkṣat || 22 || ihi| tisra:| parāᵥvata:| ihi| pañca| janān| ati| dhenā:| indra| avaᵥcārkṣat ||22|| he ᵥindra ᵥdhenā: asmadīyā: stutī: ᵥavaccukṛśat yo'paśyat sa tvaṃ ᵥparāvata: dūrāt| ‘āre parāvata:’ iti dūranāmasu pāṭhāt| ᵥtiṣṭha: agrapṛṣṭhapārśvadiśa: ᵥihi gaccha| anena agrata: pṛṣṭhata: pārśvataśca indrasyāgamanamāśāste| api ca ᵥpañca ᵥjanān manuṣyān ᵥati ᵥihi atigaccha| yadvā| gandharvā: pitaro devā asurā rakṣāṃsi ca pañcajanā:| tānatīhītyartha:| tathā ca yāska:—‘gandharvā: pitaro devā asurā rakṣāṃsītyeke catvāro varṇā niṣāda: pañcama ityaupamanyava:’ (niru. 3. 8) iti ||
sūryo raśmi yathā sujā tvā yacchantu me gira:| nimnamāpo na sadhryak || 23 || sūrya:| raśmim| yathā| suja| ā| tvā| yacchantu| me| gira:|| nimnam| āpa:| na| sadhryak || 23 || he indra ᵥsūrya: ᵥyathā ᵥraīima raśmīn kiraṇānaścapragrahān vā visṛjati tathā mahyaṃ dhanaṃ visṛja| api ca ᵥme madīyā: ᵥgira: stutaya: ᵥsadhyak saha tvām ᵥā ᵥyacchantu| ᵥnimnamāpo ᵥna| yathā nimnaṃ deśamāpa: saha parigṛhṇanti tadvadityartha: ||
adhvaryuvā tu hi viśva somaṃ vīrārya śipriṇe| bharā sutasya pītaye || 24 || adhvaryo iti| ā| tu| hi| siñca| somam| vīrārya| śipriṇe| bhara| sutasya| pītaye ||24|| he ᵥadhvaryo ᵥśipriṇe hanūmate ᵥvīrāya śūrāyendrāya ᵥsomaṃ ᵥtu ᵥhi kṣiprameva ᵥā ᵥsiñca| ᵥsutasya sutaṃ somaṃ ᵥpītaye pānāya ca ᵥbhara āhara ca ||
1. gara-ta-na-bha- ‘pañca’ nāsti|
2. ga- ‘ca’ nāsti|
ya u॒graḥ phali̭gaṃ bhina॒tsi॒na॒dhrvāsṛ̭jat | yo go॒ṣu॒ pa॒kaṃ dhā॒raya̭t ||25|| yaḥ | u॒graḥ | phali̭gam | bhina॒t | nya̭k | si॒ndhūn | ava̭'asṛjat | yaḥ | go॒ṣṷ | pa॒kam | dhā॒raya̭t || 25 ||
payaḥ : 'ugraḥ udakārthaṃ 'phaligaṃ megham | 'raivataḥ phaligaḥ ' iti meghanāmasu pāṭhāt | 'bhinat abhinat | 'sindhūn apaśca antarikṣāt 'nyak arvāk 'avāsṛjat | 'yaḥ ca 'goṣu 'pakaṃ payaḥ 'dhārayat adhārayat | sa indra ityarthaḥ || 5 ||
a॒hanvṛ॒tramṛ̭cī॒ṣama॒rṇavā̭bhama॒hīśṷrvam | hi॒menā̭vidhya॒darbṷdam ||26|| a॒han | vṛ॒tram | ṛ॒cī॒ṣama̭ḥ | au॒rṇa'vā̭bham | a॒hī'śṷrvam | hi॒mena̭
‘ vayaṃ gha tvā ’ ityekonaviṃśatyṛcaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktaṃ kāṇvasya medhyātitherārṣaṃ bṛhatīcchandaskam |
ṣoḍaśyādyāstisro gāyatrya ekonaviṃśatyanuṣṭup |
indro devatā |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘ vayaṃ ghaikonā medhyātithirbārhataṃ trigāyatryanuṣṭubantam ’ iti |
mahāvrate niṣkevalye bārhatatvāśītāvāditaḥ pañcadaśarcaḥ |
tathaiva pañcamāraṇyake śaunakena sūyate—‘ vayaṃ gha tvā sutāvanta iti pañcadaśa mo ṣu tvā vāghataścane- tyetasya dvipadāṃ coddharati ’ ( ai. ā. 5. 2. 4 ) iti |
cāturviṃśike'hani mādhyandinasavane brāhmaṇācchaṃsiśastre ‘ vayaṃ gha tvā ’ iti tṛco vaikalpikaḥ stotriyaḥ |
tathā ca sūtraṃ—‘ vayaṃ gha tvā sutāvantaḥ ka īṃ veda sute sacā ’ ( āśva. śrau. 7. 4 ) iti |
svarasāmyameva tṛco'nurūpaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ vayaṃ gha tvā sutāvanta iti tisro bṛhatyaḥ ’ ( āśva. śrau. 8. 5 ) iti |
tasminneva śastre ‘ ka īṃ veda ’ iti vaikalpiko'nurūpaḥ |
sūtramuktameva ||
vayaṃ gha tvā sutāvanta āpo na vṛktabarhiṣaḥ |
pavitrasya prasravaṇeṣu vṛtrahanpari stotāra āsate ||
1 ||
vayam | gha | tvā | suta'vantaḥ | āpaḥ | na | vṛkta'barhiṣaḥ | pavitrasya | pra'sravaṇeṣu | vṛtra'han | pari | stotāraḥ | āsate || 1 ||
he vṛtrahan indra tvā tvāṃ vayaṃ gha vayaṃ khalu sutāvantaḥ somamabhiputavantaḥ āpo na āpa iva pravaṇamabhigacchāmaḥ |
pavitrasya somānāṃ prasravaṇeṣu vṛktabarhiṣaḥ stīrṇabarhiṣaḥ stotāraḥ ca tvāṃ paryupāsate ||
svaranti tvā sute naro vasī nireka ukthinaḥ |
kadā sutaṃ tṛṣāṇa oka ā gama indra svandīva vaṃsagaḥ ||
2 ||
svaranti | tvā | sute | naraḥ | vaso iti | nireke | ukthinaḥ | kadā | sutam | tṛṣāṇaḥ | okaḥ | ā | gamaḥ | indra | svandīva | vaṃsagaḥ || 2 ||
he vaso vāsayitarindra tvā tvāṃ sute abhipute some nireke nirgamane ukthinaḥ naraḥ netāraḥ svaranti śabdayante |
api cendraḥ sutaṃ somaṃ prati tṛṣāṇaḥ tṛṣyan svandīva svabhūtaśabda iva vaṃsagaḥ vanarnāyagamano vṛṣabhaḥ śabdaṃ kurvan kadā okaḥ sthānam ā gamata ||
kaṇvaibhirṛṣṇivā dhṛṣṭājaṃ darṣi sahasriṇam |
piśaṅgarūpaṃ maghavanvicarṣaṇe makṣū gomantamīmahe ||
3 ||
kaṇvaibhiḥ | vṛṣṇe iti | ā | dhṛṣat | vājam | darṣi | sahasriṇam | piśaṅga'rūpam | maghavan | vi'carṣaṇe | makṣu | go'mantam | īmahe || 3 ||
he vṛṣṇe dharṣakendra kaṇvaibhiḥ kaṇvānudiśya |
vibhaktivyatyayaḥ |
sahasriṇaṃ sahasrasaṃkhyākaṃ vājam ā darṣi dehi |
he maghavan dhanavan vicarṣaṇe vidvaṣṭarindra dhṛṣat dhṛṣṭaṃ piśaṅgarūpaṃ gomantaṃ ca vājaṃ makṣu śīghram īmahe yācāmahe |
vāmiti śeṣaḥ ||
1. ga-gha-ta-na-bha-medhyātithe | 2. ga-ta-na-bha-‘ indra ’ nāsti | 3. gha-paryāyate upāsate | 4. ga-ta-na-‘ ukthinaḥ ’ nāsti | 5. ga-ta-na-bha-‘ dehi ’ nāsti |
pāhi gayānrdhaso mada indrāya meṣyātithe | yaḥ saṃrmiślo haryoryaḥ sute sarcā vajrī rathau hiraṇyayaḥ || 4 || pāhi | gāya | andhasaḥ | made | indrāya | medhya'atithe | yaḥ | sam'miślaḥ | haryoḥ | yaḥ | sute | sarcā | vajrī | rathaḥ | hiraṇyayaḥ || 4 || he ᵥmeṣyātithe ᵥpāhi somaṃ piba | ᵥandhasaḥ pītasya somasya ᵥmade tasmai ᵥindrāya ᵥgāya stotraṃ paṭha' ca' | ᵥyaḥ indraḥ ᵥharyoḥ aśvayoḥ ᵥsaṃmiślaḥ svarathe saṃmiśrayitā | ᵥyaḥ ca ᵥsute some ᵥsaccā sahāyaḥ | ya indraḥ ᵥvajrī | yasya ᵥrathaḥ ᵥhiraṇyayaḥ hiraṇmayaḥ ||
yaḥ suṣpavyaḥ sudakṣiṇa ino yaḥ sukraturguṇe |
ya okuraḥ
^sute abhiṣute some ^saccā ṛtvijā saha somaṃ ^pibantam ^īm enamindraṃ ^ka: ^veda vesi | na ko'pi vettītyartha: | ^kat kiṃ vā ^vaca: akṣaṃ ^dadhe dhārayati | ^ya: ^ayam indra: ^śimrī hanūmān ^andhasa: somena ^mandāna: mandamāna: ^ojasā balena ^pura: ^vibhinatti || dānā mṛgo na vāraṇa: purutrā carathaṃ dadhe | nakiṣṭā ni yamadā sute gamo mahāṃścarasyojasā || 8 || dānā | mṛga: | na | vāraṇa: | puru'trā | caratham | dadhe | naki: | tvā | ni | yamat | ā | sute | gama: | mahān | carasi | ojasā || 8 || ^mṛga: śatrūṇāmanveṣaka: ^vāraṇa: gaja: ^dānā madajalānīva ^purutrā bahuṣu yajñeṣu ^carathaṃ caraṇaśīlaṃ madaṃ ^dadhe indro dhārayati | atha pratyakṣastuti: | he indra ^tvā ^tvāṃ ^naki: ^ni ^yamat na kaścidyacchati | ^sute some ^ā ^
he ˅maghavan ˅te tava ˅abhīśavaḥ raśmayo'śvaraśanāḥ ˅vṛṣaṇaḥ varṣitāraḥ |
˅hiraṇyayī hiraṇmayī ˅kakṣā api ˅vṛṣā |
˅rathaḥ api ˅vṛṣā varṣitā |
˅harī aśvāvapi ˅vṛṣaṇā vṛṣaṇau varṣitārau |
he ˅śatakrato bahuprajñendra ˅tvaṃ ca ˅vṛṣā varṣitā ||
vṛṣā sortā sunotu te vṛṣaṛjīpinā bhara |
vṛṣā dadhanve vṛṣaṇaṃ nadīṣvā tubhyaṃ sthātaharīṇām ||
12 ||
vṛṣā | sortā | sunotu | te | vṛṣan | ṛjīpin | ā | bhara | vṛṣā | dadhanve | vṛṣaṇam | nadīṣu | ā | tubhyam | sthātaḥ | harīṇām || 12 ||
he ˅vṛṣan varṣitarindra ˅te tava ˅sotā abhivyakartā ˅vṛṣā varṣitā san ˅sunotu somama- bhiyuṇotu |
he ˅ṛjīpin ṛjugamanendra ˅ā ˅bhara dhanamasmabhyamāhara |
˅harīṇām aśvānām ˅ā ābhimukhyena he ˅sthātaḥ indra ˅tubhyaṃ ˅nadīṣu udakeṣu ˅vṛṣaṇaṃ varṣitāraṃ somaṃ ˅vṛṣā varṣitā ˅dadhanve dhāritavānabhivyartham ||
endraṃ yāhi pītaye madhu śaviṣṭha somyam |
nāyamacchā maghavā śṛṇvadido brahvokthā ca sukratuḥ ||
13 ||
ā | indra | yāhi | pītaye | madhu | śaviṣṭha | somyam | na | ayam | acche | maghavā | śṛṇvat | giraḥ | brahma | ukthā | ca | sukratuḥ || 13 ||
he ˅śaviṣṭha balavattama ˅indra ˅somyaṃ somātmakaṃ ˅madhu amṛtaṃ ˅pītaye pānāya ˅ā ˅yāhi āgaccha |
kimarthamāgamanamityata āha |
yata āgamanamantareṇa ˅maghavā dhanavān ˅sukratuḥ sukarmā śobhanaprajño vā ˅ayam indraḥ ˅giraḥ stutīḥ ˅brahma stotrānyukthāni ˅ca ˅na ˅acche³ ˅śṛṇvat nābhiśṛṇoti |
ata āgamanamityarthaḥ ||
vahantu tvā ratheṣṭhāmā harayo rathayujaḥ |

14 ||
vahantu | tvā | rathe'sthām | ā | harayaḥ | ratha'yujaḥ | tiraḥ | cit | ayam | sarvanāni | vṛtra'han | anyeṣām | yā | śatakrato || 14 ||
he ˅vṛtrahan ˅śatakrato bahuprajña ˅ratheṣṭhāṃ rathastham ˅artham īśvaraṃ ˅tvā tvāṃ ˅rathayujaḥ rathe yuktāḥ ˅harayaḥ aśvāḥ ˅anyeṣām ˅yā yāni savanāni santi tāni ˅tiraḥ⁴ tiraskurvantaḥ ˅savanāni asmadīyāni savanāni ˅ā ˅vahantu ||
asmākamadyāntamaṃ stomaṃ dhiṣva mahāmaha |
asmākaṃ te sarvanā santu śaṃtamā madāya dyukṣa somapāḥ ||
15 ||
1. kha-ta-na-bha-śa-ṛjīpin | 2. gha-śastrāṇi ca | 3. ga-gha-ta-na-bha-accha | 4. gha-jña- ta1-2-tiraścit |
asmākam | adya | antatam | stomam | dhiṣtra | mahā'maha | asmākam | te | sarvanā | santu | śam'tamā | madāya | dhukṣa | soma'pāḥ || 15 ||
he mahāmaha mahatāmapi mahan mahāpūja vā adya antatam antikatamam asmākaṃ medhyāti- thīnāṃ stomaṃ dhiṣtra dhāraya |
he dhukṣa dīpta somapāḥ somasya pātarindra te tava madāya madāya savanā savanāni asmākaṃ śaṃtamā śaṃtamāni sukhatamāni santu bhavantu ||
9 ||
nahi ṣatava no marma śāstra anyasya raṇyati | yo asmānvīra ānayat ||16|| nahi | saḥ | tava | no iti | marma | śāstra | anyasya | raṇyati | yaḥ | asmān | vīraḥ | ā | anayat || 16 ||
yaḥ vīraḥ śūraḥ asmān ānayat saḥ indraḥ tava śāstre śāsane nahi raṇyati na ramate |
mama api śāstre śāsane no raṇyati |
anyasya api śāsane na raṇyati |
kiṃtu rakṣaṇa eva ramata ityarthaḥ ||
indraścid tadabravītstriyā aśāsyaṃ manaḥ | uto aha ṛtuṃ raghum || 17 || indraḥ | cit | gha | tat | abravīt | striyāḥ | aśāsyam | manaḥ | uto iti | aha | ṛtum | raghum || 17 ||

mitrāvaruṇavantā uta dharmavantā marutvantā jariturgacchatho havam | sajoṣasā upasā sūryeṇa cādityairyātamaśvinā || 13 || mitrāvaruṇa'vantau | uta | dharma'vantā | marutvantā | jarituḥ | gacchathaḥ | havam | sa'joṣasau | upasā | sūryeṇa | ca | ādityaiḥ | yātam | aśvinā || 13 || vauta api ca he aśvinau yuvāṃ vamitrāvaruṇavantā mitrāvaruṇayuktau vadharmavantā dharmayuktau ca vamarutvantā marunmiyuktau ca vajarituḥ sto
he aśvinau yuvāṃ vbrahma brāhmaṇaṃ vjinvataṃ prīṇayatam |
vuta api ca vdhiyaḥ karmāṇi vjinvatam |
vhataṃ ca vrakṣāṃsi |
vamīvāḥ rākṣasāṃśca vsedhatam |
vupasā vsūryeṇa vca saṃgatau vsunvataḥ yajamānasya vsomaṃ pibatamityarthaḥ |
kṣetraṃ jinvatamuta jinvataṃ nṛnhataṃ rakṣāṃsi sedhatamamīvāḥ |
sajoṣasā upasā sūryeṇa ca somaṃ sunvato aśvinā |
17 |
kṣetram | jinvatama | uta | jinvatama | nṛn | hatam | rakṣāṃsi | sedhatam | amīvāḥ | su'joṣasau | upasā | sūryeṇa | ca | somam | sunvataḥ | aśvinā || 17 ||
he aśvinau yuvāṃ vkṣatra kṣatriyaṃ vjinvataṃ | vuta api ca vnṝn yoddhṝn vjinvataṃ | siddhamanyat ||
dhenurjinvatamuta jinvataṃ viśoṃ hataṃ rakṣāṃsi sedhatamamīvāḥ |
sajoṣasā upasā sūryeṇa ca somaṃ sunvato aśvinā |
18 |
dhenuḥ |
jinva
raśmī̭riva yacchatamadhva॒ra~ upa̭ śyā॒vāśva̭sya sunva॒to mada̭cyutā | sajo̭ṣasā u॒ṣasa॒ḥ sūryḙṇa cāśvinā tiro̭ahyam || 21 || raśmīn'i̭va | yacchatam | a॒dhva॒rān | upa̭ | śyā॒va'a̭śvasya | sunva̭taḥ | mada̭cyutā | sa'jo̭ṣasau | u॒ṣasā̭ | sūryḙṇa | ca | a॒śvinā̭ | tira̭ḥ'a̭hyam || 21 || he aśvinau' śyāvāśvasya mama 'adhvarān' raśmīniva yathāpragrahāṃstadvat 'upa' 'yacchatam' upagacchatam | siddhamanyat ||
a॒rvāgratha॒ṃ ni ya̭cchataṃ piba̭taṃ somya॒ṃ madhṷ | ā yā̭tamaśvinā gaṃtamava॒suvī̭ma॒haṃ hṷve dhatta॒ṃ ratnā̭ni dā॒śuṣḙ || 22 || a॒rvāk | ratha̭m | ni | ya̭cchatam | piba̭tam | so॒myam | madhṷ | ā | yā̭tam | a॒śvinā̭ | ā | ga̭tam | a॒va॒suḥ | vā॒m | a॒ham | hu॒ve | dha॒ttam | ratnā̭ni | dā॒śuṣḙ || 22 || he aśvinau vāṃ svīyaṃ 'ratham' 'arvāk' asmadabhimukhaṃ 'ni' 'yacchatam' | 'somyaṃ' somamayaṃ 'madhu' amṛtaṃ ca 'pibatam' | 'yajñam' 'ā' 'yātaṃ ca' | somaṃ prati 'ā' 'gatam' āgacchataṃ ca | 'avasuḥ' rakṣaṇakāmaḥ 'ahaṃ' śyāvāśvaḥ 'vāṃ' 'huve' hvayāmi | 'dāśuṣe' havīṃṣi prayacchate mahyaṃ 'ratnāni' 'dhattaṃ' dhārayatam ||
namo̭vāke prasthite a॒dhva॒re na̭rā viva̭kṣaṇasya pī॒tayḙ | ā yā̭tamaśvinā gaṃtamava॒suvī̭ma॒haṃ hṷve dhatta॒ṃ ratnā̭ni dā॒śuṣḙ || 23 || namo̭'vāke | pra'sthi̭te | a॒dhva॒re | na॒rā | viva̭kṣaṇasya | pī॒tayḙ | ā | yā̭tam | a॒śvinā̭ | ā | ga̭tam | a॒va॒suḥ | vā॒m | a॒ham | hu॒ve | dha॒ttam | ratnā̭ni | dā॒śuṣḙ || 23 || he aśvinau 'narā' netārau yuvāṃ 'vivakṣaṇasya' havanaśīlasya' mama 'prasthite' 'namovāke' | namaskārāya procyate sa namovākaḥ | tasmin 'adhvare' yajñe | tathā ca brāhmaṇam—'ubhayaṃ saha vā etadyajña eva yassūktavākaśca namovākaśca' ( śa. brā. 1. 9. 1. 4 ) iti | 'pītaye' somapānāya 'ā' 'yātam' | siddhamanyat ||
svāhā̭kṛtasya tṛmpataṃ su॒tasya̭ de॒vāva̭ndhasaḥ | ā yā̭tamaśvinā gaṃtamava॒suvī̭ma॒haṃ hṷve dhatta॒ṃ ratnā̭ni dā॒śuṣḙ || 24 || svāhā̭'kṛtasya | tṛ॒mpa॒tam | su॒tasya̭ | de॒vau | a॒ndhasa̭ḥ | ā | yā̭tam | a॒śvinā̭ | ā | ga̭tam | a॒va॒suḥ | vā॒m | a॒ham | hu॒ve | dha॒ttam | ratnā̭ni | dā॒śuṣḙ || 24 || he aśvinau 'devau' yuvāṃ 'sutasya' abhiṣutasya 'svāhākṛtasya' hutasya 'andhasaḥ' somasya 'tṛmpataṃ' tṛpyatam | siddhamanyat || || 17 ||
1. gha-aśvinau madacyutā śatrūṇāṃ madasya cyāvayitārau yuvāṃ sunvataḥ |
2. bha-adhvarān yajñān |
3. ga-bha3-vahanaśīlasya; śa-darśanaśīlasya |
janitā divo janitā pṛthivyāḥ piba somaṃ madāya kaṃ śatakrato | yaṃ te bhāgamadhārayanviśvāḥ sehānaḥ pṛtanā uru jayaḥ samapsujinamarutvā~ indra satvate ||4|| janitā | divaḥ | janitā | pṛthivyāḥ | piba | somam | madāya | kam | śatakrato iti śata'krato | yam | te | bhāgam | adhārayan | viśvāḥ | sehānaḥ | pṛtanāḥ | uru | jayaḥ | sam | apsu'jit | marutvān | indra | satva'pate || 4 || he indra tvaṃ divaḥ dyulokasya janitā janako'si | pṛthivyāḥ ca janitā asi | siddhamanyat ||
janitāśvānāṃ janitā gavāmasi piba somaṃ madāya kaṃ śatakrato | yaṃ te bhāgamadhārayanviśvāḥ sehānaḥ pṛtanā uru jayaḥ samapsujinamarutvā~ indra satvate ||5|| janitā | aśvānām | janitā | gavām | asi | piba | somam | madāya | kam | śatakrato iti śata'krato | yam | te | bhāgam | adhārayan | viśvāḥ | sehānaḥ | pṛtanāḥ | uru | jayaḥ | sam | apsu'jit | marutvān | indra | satva'pate || 5 || he indra tvam aśvānāṃ janitā janakaḥ asi | gavāṃ ca janitā asi | siddhamanyat ||
atrīṇāṃ stomamadrivo mahaskṛdhi piba somaṃ madāya kaṃ śatakrato | yaṃ te bhāgamadhārayanviśvāḥ sehānaḥ pṛtanā uru jayaḥ samapsujinamarutvā~ indra satvate ||6|| atrīṇām | stomam | adrivaḥ | mahaḥ | kṛdhi | piba | somam | madāya | kam | śatakrato iti śata'krato | yam | te | bhāgam | adhārayan | viśvāḥ | sehānaḥ | pṛtanāḥ | uru | jayaḥ | sam | apsu'jit | marutvān | indra | satva'pate || 6 || he adrivaḥ adriman atrīṇāṃ stomaṃ mahaskṛdhi pūjitaṃ kuru | siddhamanyat ||
śyāvāśvasya sunvattathā śṛṇu yathāśrṛṇoratreḥ karmāṇi kṛṇvataḥ | pra trasadasyumāvitha tvameka iṣupā indra brahmāṇi vardhayan ||7|| śyāva'aśvasya | sunvataḥ | tathā | śṛṇu | yathā | aśṛṇoḥ | atreḥ | karmāṇi | kṛṇvataḥ | pra | trasadasyum | āvidha | tvam | ekaḥ | it | nṛ'sahau | indra | brahmāṇi | vardhayan || 7 || he indra tvaṃ sunvataḥ somābhiṣavaṃ kurvataḥ śyāvāśvasya mama stutiṃ karmāṇi kṛṇvataḥ kurvataḥ atreḥ yathāśṛṇoḥ aśrauṣīḥ tathā śṛṇu | api ca tvameka it eka eva nṛṣāhe yuddhe brahmāṇi stotrāṇi kāmaiḥ vardhayan trasadasyuṃ pra āvidha ||
‘predaṃ brahma’ iti saptacaṃ saptamaṃ sūktamātrayasya śyāvāśvasyārṣam |
ādyā dvābhyādaśarāti- jagatī |
śiṣṭāḥ ṣaṭṭacaḥ mahāpaṅktayaḥ |
indro devatā |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ- ‘predaṃ mahāpāṅktamā-
ghātijagatī' iti |
mahāvrate niṣkevalya etassūktam' |
tathā ca sūtraṃ—'predaṃ brahmaṇvo madāya' (āśva. śrau. 7. 12) iti ||
predaṃ brahma̭ vṛtratūrye॒ṣvāvi̭tha pra su॒nva॒taḥ | śacī̭pata indra॒ viśvā̭bhirū॒tībhi̭ḥ || 1 || mādhya̭ndinasya | sa॒rva॒na॒sya | vṛ॒tra॒ha॒n | e॒dya॒ | piba̭ | so॒ma॒sya | va॒jri॒vaḥ || 1 ||
he śacīpate indra tvaṃ vṛtratūryeṣu saṃgrāmeṣu idaṃ brahma imān brāhmaṇān viśvābhiḥ sarvābhiḥ ūtibhiḥ2 rakṣābhiḥ pra āviya prarakṣa |
sunvataḥ somābhiṣavaṃ kurvato yajamānāṃśca pra āviya |
api ca he anedya aninya vajrivaḥ vajrin vṛtrahan indra mādhyandinasya savanasya saṃbandhanaṃ somasya somaṃ piba ||
sehānaṃ u॒graṃ pṛ̭ta॒nā a॒bhi dṷhaḥ śacī̭pata indra॒ viśvā̭bhirū॒tībhi̭ḥ ||
2 ||
kṣemasya ca prayujaśca tvamīśiṣe śacīpata indra viśvābhirūtibhiḥ | mādhyaṃdinasya sarvasya vṛtrahannanedha pibā somasya vajrivaḥ || 5 || kṣemasya | ca | pra'yujaḥ | | ca | tvam | īśiṣe | śacī'pate | indra | viśvābhiḥ | ūti'bhiḥ | mādhyaṃdinasya | sarvasya | vṛtra'han | anedha | piba | somasya | vajri'vaḥ || 5 ||
api ca he śacīpate indra sarvasya jagataḥ kṣemasya prayujaśca prayogasya ca |
yogakṣemayo- rityarthaḥ |
īśiṣe īśvaro bhavasi |
siddhamanyat ||
kṣatrāya tvamarvasi na tvamāvitha śacīpata indra viśvābhirūtibhiḥ | mādhyaṃdinasya sarvasya vṛtrahannanedha pibā somasya vajrivaḥ || 6 || kṣatrāya | tvam | avasi | na | tvam | āviṣa | śacī'pate | indra | viśvābhiḥ | ūti'bhiḥ | mādhyaṃdinasya | sarvasya | vṛtra'han | anedha | piba | somasya | vajri'vaḥ || 6 ||
he śacīpate indra tvaṃ kṣatrāya jagato balāya bhavasi |
avasi āśritān rakṣasi |
tvaṃ na āviya kenāpi na rakṣyase |
siddhamanyat ||
iyāvāśvasya rebhatastathā śṛṇu yathāśṛṇotreḥ karmāṇi kṛṇvataḥ | pra trasadasyumāvitha tvameka inupāhā indra kṣatrāṇi vardhayan || 7 || iyāva'aśvasya | rebhataḥ | tathā | śṛṇu | yathā | aśṛṇoḥ | atreḥ | karmāṇi | kṛṇvataḥ | pra | trasadasyum | āviṣa | tvam | ekaḥ | ita | nu'sahyai | indra | kṣatrāṇi | vardhayan ||7||
he indra rebhataḥ stuvataḥ iyāvāśvasya mama stutiṃ karmāṇi kṛṇvataḥ atreḥ yathā aśṛṇoḥ tathā śṛṇu |
api ca kṣatrāṇi balāni kāmaiḥ vardhayan yuddhe tvamekaḥ eva trasadasyuṃ pra āviya ||
19 ||
'yajñasya hi' iti daśarcamamaṃ sūktaṃ iyāvāśvasyārṣaṃ gāyatramindrāgni- devatākam |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—'yajñasya daśaindrābham' iti |
pṛṣṭhābhiplavaṣaḍahayoḥ prātaḥsavane'- cchāvākasya āvāpāthametatsūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca—'yajñasya hi stha ityacchāvākasya' (āśva. śrau. 7. 5) iti |
cāturviṃśike'hani prātaḥsavane 'yajñasya hi sthaḥ' iti ṣaḻahastomatriyasaṃjñakattvaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—'indrāgnī yuvāmimaṃ yajñasya hi stha ṛtvijatyacchāvākasya' (āśva. śrau. 7. 2) iti |
agniṣṭome prātaḥsavane'cchāvākasya 'prātaryāvabhiraḥ' iti prātaḥsavanīyasya prasthitayājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'prātaryāvabhiriti yajati' (āśva. śrau. 5. 7) iti |
cāturviṃśike prātaḥsavane'- cchāvākasya 'iyāvāśvasya' ityathaṃ paryāmastvacaḥ |
anyatrāpyahargaṇepu dvitīyādiṣṭavahaḥsu |
sūyate hi—'iyāvāśvasya sunvat iti tṛcāḥ paryāsāḥ' (āśva. śrau. 7. 2) iti ||
yajñasya hi stha ṛtvijā sastrī vājeṣu karmasu | indrāgnī tasya bodhatam || 1 || yajñasya | hi | sthaḥ | ṛtvijā | sastrī | vājeṣu | karmasu | indrāgnī iti | tasya | bodhatam ||1||
1. ga-ta-na-bha- 'kṣatrāṇi...vardhayan' nāsti |
he ᵥindrāgnī ᵥsakhī śuddhau yuvāṃ ᵥyajñasya ᵥṛtvijā ᵥṛtvijau ᵥsthaḥ bhavathaḥ ᵥhi| ᵥvājeṣu yuddheṣu ᵥkarmasu copatiṣṭhantāvindrāgnī ᵥtasya taṃ māṃ tasya mama stutiṃ vā ᵥbodhataṃ jānītam || tośāsā rthayāvānā vṛtrahaṇāparājitā | indrāgnī tasya bodhatam || 2 || tośāsā | ratha'yāvānāṃ | vṛtra'hanā | aparā'jitā | indrāgnī iti | tasya | bodhatam || 2 || he ᵥindrāgnī ᵥtośāsā śatrūna hiṃsantau ᵥrathayāvānā rathena gacchantau ᵥvṛtrahaṇā vṛtrasya hantārau ᵥaparājitā kenāpyaparājitau ᵥtasya taṃ māṃ ᵥbodhatam || idaṃ vāṃ madiraṃ madhvadhukṣannadribhinaraiḥ | indrāgnī tasya bodhatam || 3 || idam | vām | madiram | madhu | adhukṣan | adri'bhiḥ | naraiḥ | indrāgnī iti | tasya | bodhatam || 3 || he ᵥindrāgnī ᵥvāṃ yuvāmuddiśya ᵥnaraḥ yajñasya netāraḥ ᵥadribhiḥ grāvabhiḥ ᵥmadiraṃ madakaraṃ ᵥmadhu somātmakamatṛtam ᵥadhukṣan apūrayan | siddhamantyat || juṣethāṃ yajñamiṣṭayai sutaṃ somaṃ sadhastutī | indrāgnī ā gataṃ narā || 4 || juṣethām | yajñam | iṣṭayai | sutam | somam | sadhastutī iti sadha'stutī | indrāgnī iti | ā | gatam | narā || 4 || he ᵥsadhastutī sahabhūtastutī ᵥnarā netārau ᵥindrāgnī ᵥyajñaṃ ᵥjuṣethāṃ sevethām | ᵥiṣṭaye yāgāya ᵥsutam abhiputaṃ ᵥsomaṃ ca ᵥā ᵥgatam āgacchatam || imā juṣethāṃ sarvanā yebhirhavyān yūhathuḥ | indrāgnī ā gataṃ narā || 5 || imā | juṣethām | sarvanā | yebhiḥ | havyāni | ūhathuḥ | indrāgnī iti | ā | gatam | narā || 5 || he ᵥindrāgnī ᵥnarā netārau yuvāṃ ᵥyebhiḥ yeḥ savanaiḥ ᵥhavyāni ᵥūhathuḥ vahathaḥ tāni ᵥimā imāni ᵥsavanā savanāni ᵥjuṣethāṃ sevethām | ᵥā ᵥgataṃ ca || imāṃ gāyatravartaniṃ juṣethāṃ suṣṭutiṃ mama | indrāgnī ā gataṃ narā || 6 || imām | gāyatra'vartanim | juṣethām | su'stutim | mama | indrāgnī iti | ā | gatam | narā || 6 || he ᵥindrāgnī narau yuvāṃ ᵥmama ᵥgāyatravartaniṃ gāyatramārgān ᵥimāṃ ᵥsuṣṭutiṃ śobhanāṃ stutiṃ ᵥjuṣethāṃ sevethām | ᵥā ᵥgataṃ ca || || 20 || prātaryāvabhirgātaṃ devebhirjenyāvasū | indrāgnī somapītaye || 7 || prātaryāva'bhiḥ | ā | gatam | devebhiḥ | jenyāvasū iti | indrāgnī iti | soma'pītaye || 7 || he ᵥjenyāvasū jetavyaśatrudhanau ᵥindrāgnī ᵥprātaryāvabhiḥ devaiḥ saha ᵥsomapītaye somasya pānāya ᵥā ᵥgatam āgacchatam || śyāvāśvasya sunvato'strīṇāṃ śṛṇutaṃ havam | indrāgnī somapītaye || 8 || 1. ga-ta-na-bha- 'yebhiḥ' nāsti|
śyāva'aśvasya | sunvataḥ | atrīṇām | śṛṇutam | havam | indrāgnī iti | soma'pītaye || 8 || he indrāgnī yuvāṃ sunvataḥ somābhiṣavaṃ kurvato yajamānasya śyāvāśvasya mama atrīṇām ṛtvijāṃ havam āhvānaṃ somasya pānāya śṛṇutam ||
evā vāmaha ūtaye yathāhuvanta medhirāḥ | indrāgnī somapītaye || 9 || eva | vām | ahe | ūtaye | yathā | ahuvanta | medhirāḥ | indrāgnī iti | soma'pītaye || 9 || he indrāgnī vāṃ yuvāṃ yathā medhirāḥ prājñāḥ ahuvanta āhūtavantaḥ evamaham ūtaye rakṣaṇāya somasya pītaye ca ahe hvayāmi ||
āhaṃ sarasvatītorendrāmyoravau vṛṇe | yābhyāṃ gāyatramucyate || 10 || ā | aham | sarasvatī'vatoḥ | indrāgnyoḥ | avaḥ | vṛṇe | yābhyām | gāyatram | ṛcyate || 10 || yābhyāṃ yayorindrāgnyorartha gāyatraṃ sāma ṛcyate stūyate tayoḥ sarasvatīvatoḥ stutimatoḥ indrāgnyoḥ saṃbandha ava rakṣaṇam aham ā vṛṇe || || 21 || ‘abhimstoṣi’ iti daśacaṃ navamaṃ sūkaṃ kāṇvasya nābhākasyārṣam | ṣaḍṛcā mahāpaṅkti- śchandaḥ | agnirdevatā | anukrāntaṃ ca—‘abhimstoṣi nābhāka ātreyaṃ mahāpāṅktaṃ hi’ iti | viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
agnimastoṣyugmiyamimīḻā yajadhyai | agnidevā a̭naktu na ubhe hi vidathai kavirantaścarati dūtya̭ṃ | nabhantāmanyake same ||11|| agnim | astoṣi | ṛgmiyam | agnim | īḻā | yajadhyai | agniḥ | devān | anaktu | naḥ | ubhe iti | hi | vidathe iti | kaviḥ | antariti | carati | dūtyam | nabhantām | anyake | same || 1 || ṛgmiyam arham agnim astoṣi staumi | api ca agni yajadhyai yaṣṭum īḻā stutyā staumītyarthaḥ | api ca agniḥ naḥ asmākaṃ vidathe yajñe devān havirbhiḥ anaktu | kaviḥ krāntadarśabhiḥ ubhe dyāvāpṛthivyau antaḥ dūtyaṃ havirvahanādilakṣaṇaṃ dūtakarma carati | anyake śatravo'pi same sarve nabhantām | nabhatihiṃsākarmā | agninā hiṃsyantām ||

'indrāgnī yuvam' iti dvādaśarcaṃ daśamaṃ sūktaṃ nābhākasyārṣam |

ᵥuto ᵥnu ᵥcit api ca ᵥya: indra: ᵥojasā balena ᵥśuṣṇasya śuṣṇanāma kasyāsurasya ᵥaṇḍāni aṇḍajātānyapatyāni ᵥbhedati abhinat sa: ᵥsvarvatī: divyāni ᵥapa: salilāni ᵥjepat jayatu |
siddhamanyat |
taṃ śiśītā svaśvvaraṃ satyaṃ sattvānamṛviyam |
uto nu cidya ohaṃta aṇḍā śuṣṇasya bhedatyai: svarvataripo nabhantāmanyake same|
11|
tam | śiśīt | su'adhvaram | satyam | sattvānam | ṛviyam | uto iti | nu | cit | ya: | ohate | aṇḍā | śuṣṇasya | bhedati | ajai: | sva:'vatī: | apa: | nabhantām | anyake | same || 11 ||
he stotāra: ᵥsvadhvaraṃ suyajñaṃ ᵥsatyam adhināśaṃ ᵥsattvānaṃ saṃbhaktāram ᵥṛviyam ṛtau yaṣṭavyaṃ ᵥtam indraṃ ᵥśiśīta stutibhi: saṃskṛtata |
atha pratyakṣastuti: |
ᵥuto ᵥnu ᵥcit api ca ᵥya: indra: ᵥohate yajñaṃ prati gacchati ᵥśuṣṇasya ᵥaṇḍā aṇḍānyaṇḍajātāni ca ᵥbhedati³ bhinatti³ sa tvaṃ ᵥsvarvatī: divyāni ᵥapa: salilāni ᵥajai: ajaipī: |
siddhamanyat |
evendrāgnibhyāṃ pitṛvannavīyo mandhātuvadaṅgirasvadavāci |
tridhātunā śarmaṇā pātamusmānvayaṃ syāma patayo rayīṇām |
12 |
etra | indrāgni'bhyām | pitṛ'vat | navīya: | mandhātu'vat | aṅgirasa'vat | avāci | tri'dhātunā | śarmaṇā | pātam | asmān | vayam | syāma | pataya: | rayīṇām || 12 ||
ᵥeva evaṃ yābhyām ᵥindrāgnibhyāṃ ᵥpitṛvat nabhākavat ᵥmandhātuvat yauvanāśvamandhātuvacca ᵥaṅgirasvat aṅgirovacca ᵥnavīya: navataram ᵥavāci nābhākena mayā pāṭhitāvindrāgnī ᵥtridhātunā triparvaṇā ᵥśarmaṇā gṛheṇa ᵥna: ᵥasmān nābhākān⁴ ᵥpātaṃ rakṣatam |
ᵥvayaṃ ᵥrayīṇāṃ dhanānāṃ ᵥpataya: svāmina: ᵥsyāma bhavema |
25 |
1. ga-ta-na-bha- 'sūno' nāsti |
2. ta-na-bhara-vasvo vā |
3. kha-ga-ta-na-bha-śa- 'bhedati bhinatti' nāsti; gha-bhedati abhinat |
4. ga-gṛhe tu nāsmājñāḥ; ta-na-bha-gṛhe nāsmājñāḥ |
‘asmā ū ṣu’ iti daśarcemekādaśaṃ sūktam|
atreyamankramaṇikā—‘asmā ū ṣu daśa vāruṇaṃ tu’ iti|
nābhāka ṛṣiranuṣṭuttvāt|
‘mahāpāṅkaṃ hi’ ityuktatvādadapi mahāpāṅkam|
idamā- dike dve sūkte varuṇadevatye|
viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ|
cāturviṃśike'hani mādhyandinasavane maitrāvaruṇaśastra ārambhaṇīyāyā ūrdhvaṃ ‘sa kṣapaḥ’ ityayaṃ nābhākatvaḥ|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘sa kṣapaḥ pari ṣasvaja iti maitrāvaruṇaḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 2) iti|
‘yaḥ kakubhaḥ’ ityetatprabhṛtiko vā nābhākatvaḥ|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘yaḥ kakubho nidhāraya iti’ vā’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 2) iti||
asmā ū ṣu prarbhūtaye varuṇāya marudbhyo'rcā viduṣṭarebhyaḥ|
yo dhītā mānuṣāṇāṃ paśvo gāīva rakṣati narbhantāmanyake saṃme ||
1 ||
asmai| ū~ iti| su| pra'bhūtaye| varuṇāya| marut'bhyaḥ| arca| viduḥ'tarebhyaḥ| yaḥ| dhītā| mānuṣāṇām| paśvaḥ| gāḥ'iva| rakṣati| narbhantām| anyake| saṃme || 1 ||
he stotaḥ ᵥsu ᵥprabhūtaye prakṛṣṭanāya ᵥasmai ᵥvaruṇāya ᵥviduṣṭarebhyaḥ viduttarebhyaḥ ᵥmarudbhyaḥ ca ᵥarca stuhi|
ᵥyaḥ varuṇaḥ ᵥdhītā karmaṇā ᵥmānuṣāṇāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ ᵥpaśvaḥ paśūn ᵥgāiva ᵥrakṣati|
sindvamanyat||
tamu ṣu samanā girā pitṝṇāṃ ca manmabhiḥ|
nābhākasya praśastibhiḥ sindhūnāmupodaya saptsvasā sa madhyamo narbhantāmanyake saṃme ||
2 ||
tam| ū~ iti| su| samanā| girā| pitṝṇām| ca| manma'bhiḥ| nābhākasya| praśasti'bhiḥ| yaḥ| sindhūnām| upa| ut'aye| sapta'svasā| saḥ| madhyam| narbhantām| anyake| saṃme || 2 ||
ᵥtamu tameva varuṇa ᵥsamanā samānayā ᵥgirā stutyā ᵥsu abhiṣṭaumi|
ᵥpitṝṇāṃ ᵥmanmabhiḥ stomaḥ ᵥca abhiṣṭaumi|
ᵥnābhākasya ṛṣeḥ ᵥpraśastibhiḥ stotraiścābhiṣṭaumi|
ᵥsindhūnāṃ syanda- mānānāṃ nadīnām ᵥupa samīpe ᵥyaḥ ᵥudaye udgacchati yaśca ᵥsaptasvasā ᵥsa ᵥmadhyamaḥ iti vāgbhi- rnirucyate|
ᵥanyake durdhiyaḥ śatravaḥ ᵥsame sarve ᵥnarbhantāṃ mā bhūvan|
tathā ca yāskaḥ—‘taṃ svabhi- ṣṭaumi samānayā girā gītvā stutyā pitṝṇāṃ ca mananīyaiḥ stomairnābhākasya praśastibhiḥ|
ṛṣināṃ- bhāko babhūva|
yaḥ syandamānāmāsāmupodaye saptasvasāramenamāha vāgbhiḥ sa madhyama iti nirucyate'thaiṣa eva bhavati|
narbhantāmanyake saṃme mā bhūvandhanyake sarve ye no dviṣanti durdhiyaḥ pāpa- dhiyaḥ pāpasaṃkalpāḥ’ (niru. 10. 5) iti||
sa kṣapaḥ pari ṣasvaje nyuso māyayā dadhe sa viśvaṃ pari darśataḥ|
tasya venīrnu vratamupastisro avardhayannarbhantāmanyake saṃme ||
3 ||
saḥ| kṣapaḥ| pari| ṣasvaje| ni| ut| saḥ| māyayā| dadhe| saḥ| viśvam| pari| darśataḥ| tasya| venīḥ| anu| vratam| ut| tisraḥ| avardhayan| narbhantām| anyake| saṃme||3||
1. kha-ta-iti ca; ga-iti ceti; na-bha-iveti; śa-iti|
2. ga-ta-na-bhara-gacchati iva rakṣati; gha-rakṣati yathā gopālo gā rakṣati tadvat; bha1-gacchati|
ka. 1-47
vsaḥ varuṇaḥ vkṣapaḥ rātrīḥ vpari vpasvaje pariṣvajate |
api ca vdarśataḥ darśanīyaḥ vsaḥ varuṇaḥ vutsaḥ utsaraṇaśīlaḥ san vviśvaṃ vmāyayā karmaṇā vpari paritaḥ vni vdadhe nidadhāti |
kiṃca vtasya varuṇasya vvrataṃ karma vvenīḥ kāmayamānāḥ prajāḥ vtisraḥ vuṣaḥ triṣu prātarmādhyaṃdinaṃ sāyaṃ ca vanu vavadhayan anuvardhayanti |
siddhamanyat ||
yaḥ kakubhau nidhārayaḥ pṛthivyāmadhari darśataḥ |
sa mātā pūryaṃ padaṃ tadruṇasya saptyaṃ sa hi gopāḥveyoṃ nabhantāmanyake saṃme ||
4||
yaḥ | kakubhaḥ | ni'dhārayaḥ | pṛthivyām | adhi | darśataḥ | saḥ | mātā | pūryam | padam | tat | varuṇasya | sapt‌yam | saḥ | hi | gopāḥ'iva | iryaḥ | nabhantām | anyake | saṃme || 4 ||
vyaḥ varuṇaḥ vpṛthivyāmadha ipṛthivyā upari vdarśataḥ darśanīyaḥ san vkakubhaḥ diśaḥ vnidhā- rayaḥ nidhārayati vsaḥ varuṇaḥ vmātā nirmātā |
vpūūryyaṃ pratnaṃ vpadaṃ svargakhyaṃ sthānaṃ vsapt‌yam asmā- bhiśca sarpaṇīyaṃ vtat vvaruṇasya svakṛtam |
api ca vsa vhi sa eva viryaḥ īśvaraḥ san vgopāḥiva gopāla iva paśūnāmasnākaṃ rakṣitā |
siddhamanyat ||
yo dhartā bhuvanānāṃ ya utsrāṇāmapīcyā veda nāmāni guhyā |
sa kaviḥ kāvyā puru rūpaṃ dyauriva puṣyati nabhantāmanyake saṃme ||
5||
yaḥ | dhartā | bhuvanānām | yaḥ | utsrāṇām | apīcyā | veda | nāmāni | guhyā | saḥ | kaviḥ | kāvyā | puru | rūpam | dyauḥ'iva | puṣyati | nabhantām | anyake | saṃme ||5||
vyaḥ varuṇaḥ vbhuvanānāṃ vdhatāṃ dhārayitā vyaḥ ca vutsrāṇāṃ devādhiṣṭhānabhūtānāṃ raśmīnām vapīcyā apīcyānyantarhitāni vguhyā guhyāni guhāyāṃ nihitāni vnāmāni vveda jānāti vsaḥ varuṇaḥ vkaviḥ prājñaḥ san vkāvyā kāvyāni kavikarmāṇi vpuru bahūni vrūpaṃ vdyauriva vpuṣyati |
siddhamanyat ||

7|
yaḥ | āsu | atkaṃḥ | ā'śaye | viśvā | jātāni | eṣām | pari | dhāmāni | marmṛśat | varuṇasya | puraḥ | gaye | viśve | devāḥ | anu | vratam | nabhantām | anyake | seme || 7 ||

yaḥ | śvetān | ardhi'nirnijaḥ | cakre | kṛṣṇān | arju | vratā | saḥ | dhāma | pūrvyam | mame | yaḥ | skambhena | vi | rodasī iti | ajaḥ | na | dyām | adhārayat | narbhantām | anyake | same || 10 ||
ᵥthaḥ varuṇaḥ ᵥnirnijaḥ ātmīyān raśmīna divā ᵥśvetān ᵥadhi ᵥcakraṃ adhikaroti tathā rātrau ᵥkṛṣṇān cakre ᵥsaḥ varuṇaḥ ᵥanu ᵥvratā karmāṇi lakṣmīrūpobhayavidhakamāṃnuguṇaṃ ᵥpūrvya ᵥdhāma antarikṣaṃ divaṃ vā ᵥmame nirmame |
api ca ᵥthaḥ ᵥskambhena antarikṣeṇa ᵥajo ᵥna yathā ādityaḥ ᵥdyāṃ dhārayati tathā ᵥrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥvi ᵥadhārayat vidhāyati sa varuṇa ityarthaḥ |
siddhamanyat ||

‘astabhnāt’ iti ṣaṭ‌caṃ dvādaśaṃ sūktam |
arcanānā ṛṣiḥ kāṇvo nābhāko vā ṛṣiḥ |
uttare tvarca nānāḥ |
ādyastṛcaiṣṭubho varuṇadevatayo dvitīyastṛca ānuṣṭubho'śvidevatākaḥ |
tathā cānukrāntam—‘astabhnāt ṣarca nānā vā traiṣṭubhamanthaṃ vā tṛcamāśvinamānuṣṭubhamapaśyat’ iti |
sūktaviniyogo laiṅgikaḥ |
vāruṇe paśau haviṣo yājyā ‘astabhnāt’ iti |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘asta- bhnādyāmasuro viśvavedā ityekādaśināḥ |
’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 7) iti |
agnīṣomapraṇayane'pyepottarā paridhānīyā |
tathā sūtritam—‘astabhnādyāmasuro viśvavedā iti paridadhyāduttarayā vā’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 10) iti |
‘evā vandasva varuṇa̭ bṛhantaṃ tvā yāmi brahmaṇā vandamāna iti dve’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 7) |
somapravahaṇe ‘imāṃ dhiyam’ ityeṣā paridhānīyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘imāṃ dhiyaṃ śikṣamāṇasya deveti nihite paridadhyāt’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 4) iti ||
astabhnād dyāmasuro viśvavedā armīmīta varimāṇaṃ pṛthivyāḥ | āsīddiśvā bhuvanāni samrāḍ viśvettāni varuṇasya vratāni || 1 || astabhnāt | dyām | asuraḥ | viśva'vedāḥ | armīmīta | varimāṇam | pṛthivyāḥ | ā | āsīdat | viśvā | bhuvanāni | samrāṭ | viśvā | ita | tāni | varuṇasya | vratāni ||1||
ᵥviśvavedāḥ viśvadhanaḥ ᵥasuraḥ balavān varuṇaḥ ᵥdyām ᵥastabhnāt |
tathā ᵥpṛthivyāḥ ca ᵥvarimāṇaṃ parimāṇam ᵥamimīta cakre |
evaṃ nirmitāni ᵥviśvā sarvāṇi ᵥbhuvanāni ᵥsamrāṭ bhūtvā ᵥāsīdat ca adhyatiṣṭhacca |
ᵥvaruṇasya ᵥtāni etāni ᵥvratāni karmāṇi ᵥvidhet viśvanyeva ᵥ |
ato varṇayitumaśakyānītyarthaḥ 4 ||
evā vandasva varuṇa̭ bṛhantaṃ namasyā dhīramṛtasya gopām | sa naḥ śarma trivarūthaṃ vi yaṃsatpātaṃ nau dyāvāpṛthivī upasthe || 2 || eva | vandasva | varuṇam | bṛhantam | namasya | dhīram | amṛtasya | gopām | saḥ | naḥ | śarma | tri'varūtham | vi | yaṃsat | pātam | naḥ | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | upasthe ||2||
he stotaḥ ᵥbṛhantaṃ mahāntaṃ ᵥvaruṇam ᵥeva evaṃ ᵥvandasva stuhi |
ᵥamṛtasya ᵥgopāṃ gopā- yitāraṃ ᵥdhīraṃ prājñaṃ varuṇa ᵥnamasya namaskuru ca |
ᵥsaḥ varuṇaḥ ᵥnaḥ asmabhyaṃ ᵥtrivarūthaṃ tristhānaṃ 1. kha-gha-ta-na-śa- ᵥpṛṣṭho viśvadevatākaḥ |
2. gha-etādṛśasya varuṇasya |
3. ta-na-bha- ᵥvratāni ’ nāsti |
4. gha-bahūni saṃti |
vśarma gṛhaṃ vvi vyaṃsat prayacchatu | vupasthe upasthāne vartamānān vna: asmān vdyāvāpṛthivī dyāvāpṛthivyau vpātaṃ rakṣatam || imāṃ dhiyaṃ śikṣamāṇasya deva ṛtuṃ dakṣaṃ varuṇa saṃ śiṃśādhi | yayāti viśvā duritā taraima sutarmāṇamadhi nāvaṃ ruhema || 3 || imām | dhiyam | śikṣamāṇasya | deva | ṛtum | dakṣam | varuṇa | sam | śiṃśādhi | yayā | ati | viśvā | duḥ'itā | taraima | sutarmāṇam | adhi | nāvam | ruhema || 3 || he vdeva dyotamāna vvaruṇa vimāṃ vdhiyam idaṃ karma vśikṣamāṇasya anutiṣṭhato mama vṛtuṃ prajñānaṃ vdakṣaṃ balaṃ ca vsaṃ vśiṃśādhi tīkṣṇīkuru | vyayā nāvā yajñarūpayā vviśvā sarvāṇi vduritā duritāni vati vtarema tāṃ vsutarmāṇaṃ suṣṭhu tārayitrīṃ yajñarūpāṃ vnāvam vadhi vruhema vayamāruhema | duḥkhāsāgarataraṇe hetutvādyajño nau rityatra vyapadiśyate || ā vāṃ grāvāṇo aśvinā dhībhirviprā acyucyavu: | nāsatyā somapītaye narbhantāmanyake saṃme || 4 || ā | vām | grāvāṇaḥ | aśvinā | dhībhiḥ | viprāḥ | acyucyavuḥ | nāsatyā | soma'pītaye | narbhantām | anyake | same || 4 || he vnāsatyā satyau¹ satyapraṇetārau vā | tathā ca yāska:—‘satyāveva nāsatyāvitivarṇavābhaḥ satyasya praṇetārau’ (niru. 6. 13) iti | vaśvinā aśvinau vvāṃ yuvāṃ vsomapītaye somasya pānāya vviprāḥ prājñā ṛtvijaḥ vgrāvāṇaḥ somābhiṣavapāṣāṇāśca vdhībhiḥ karmabhiḥ svasvavyāpāraiḥ vacyucyavuḥ | abhigacchanti | siddhamanyat || yathā vāmatriraśvinā gīrbhirvipro ajauhavīt | nāsatyā somapītaye narbhantāmanyake saṃme || 5 || yathā | vām | atriḥ | aśvinā | gīḥ'bhiḥ | vipraḥ | ajauhavīt | nāsatyā | soma'pītaye | narbhantām | anyake | same || 5 || he nāsatyāśvinau vvāṃ yuvāṃ vvipraḥ prājñaḥ vatriḥ vyathā vgīrbhiḥ stutibhiḥ vsomapītaye vajauhavīt tathāhamapi johavīmi || evā vāmaha ūtaye yathāhuvanta medhirāḥ | nāsatyā somapītaye narbhantāmanyake saṃme || 6 || eva | vām | ahe | ūtaye | yathā | ahuvanta | medhirāḥ | nāsatyā | soma'pītaye | narbhantām | anyake | same || 6 || iyaṃ vyākhyātacarā || || 28 || || 5 ||
1. jha-bha-nāsatyau |
ṣaṣṭhe'nuvāke ṣaṭ sūktāni |
tatra 'ime viprasya' iti trayastriṃśadṛcaṃ prathamaṃ sūktamāṅgirasasya virūpasyārṣaṃ gāyatramagnidevatākam |
tathā cānukrāntam—'ime trayastriṃśadvirūpa āṅgirasa āgneyaṃ tu' iti |
prātaranuvāka āgneye kratau gāyatre chandasyāśvinaśastre caitadādike dve sūkte |
sūtritaṃ ca— 'ime viprasyeti sūkte' (āśva. śrau. 4. 13) iti |
kārīryavabhṛtheṣṭagoḥ prathamājyābhāgasya 'apsavatre' ityanuvākyā |
tathā ca sūtritam—'apsumantāvājyabhāgāvapsavatre sadhiṣṭava' (āśva. śrau. 2. 13) iti |
āgnīdhrasya prātaḥsavane prasthitayājyā |

anvāhitāgneḥ prayāṇe samāropaṇapakṣe'nayaikāhutiḥ kartavyā |

ime viprasya vedhaso'gnerastṛtayajvanaḥ | giraḥ stomāsa īrate || 1 || ime | viprasya | vedhasaḥ | agne | astṛtayajvanaḥ | giraḥ | stomāsaḥ | īrate || 1 ||

trayodaśinormadhye'ṣṭakaḥ pipīlikamadhyā’ (anu. 7. 7) ityuktatvāt |

‘viśeṣām’ iti ṣoḍaśī virāḍ |
‘mahaḥ su vaḥ’ iti saptadaśī jagatī |
‘ye pātayante’ ityaṣṭādaśyupariṣṭādbṛhatī caturthapādasya dvādaśākṣaratvāt |
‘prabhaṅkam’ ityekonaviṃśī bṛhatī |
‘sanitaḥ susanitaḥ’ iti viṃśī viṣamapadā bṛhatī ‘navakāṣṭyekādaśyaṣṭino viṣamapadā’ (anu. 7. 8) ityuktatvāt |
‘ā sa etu’ ‘ṣaṣṭi sahasrā’ ityekaviṃśīdvāviṃśayau paṅktī |
‘daśa śyāvāḥ’ iti trayoviṃśī gāyatrī |
‘dānāsaḥ’ iti caturviṃśī paṅktiḥ |
pañcaviṃśī- sasaviṃśayau bṛhatyau |
ṣaḍviṃśyāviṃśayau satobṛhatyau |
ekonatriṃśī gāyatrī |
‘gāvo na yūtham’ iti triṃśī viṃśatyakṣarā dvipadā virāḍ |
ekatriṃśyuṣṇip |
dvātriṃśī paṅktiḥ |
trayastriṃśī gāyatrī |
‘ā sa etu’ ityādibhiścatasṛbhiḥ kanītaputrasya pṛthuśravaso dānaṃ stūyate |
atastaddevatākāḥ |
‘ā no vāyo’ ityādīnāṃ catasṛṇāṃ dvātriṃśacca vāyudevatā |
śiṣṭā anādeśaparibhāṣayendradevatākāḥ |
etatsarvamanukramaṇyāmuktaṃ- ‘tvāvatastrayastriṃśo'ṣṭya ā sa ādi kānītasya pṛthuśravaso dānastutirādyā pādanicṛt pañcamyādi kakuggāyatrī bṛhatyanuṣṭup satobṛhatī gāyatrī viparītottaraḥ pragātho dvipadā caturviṃśikā bṛhatī pipī- likamadhyā kakubhyaṅkuśirā virāḍjagatyupariṣṭādbṛhatībṛhatvaṃ viṣamapadottare paṅktī gāyatrī paṅktiḥ pragāthau ca vāyavyau gāyatrī dvipadoṣiṇap paṅktirvāyavyā gāyatrī’ iti |
mahāvrate niṣkevalye ‘sanitaḥ susanitaḥ’ ityetameva sūktam |
tathā ca pañcamāraṇyake śaunakena sūtritaṃ—‘tvāvataḥ puruvasiviti vaśaḥ sanitaḥ susanirityetadantaḥ’ (ai. ā. 5. 2. 5) iti ||
tvāvataḥ puruvaso va॒yami̭ndra praṇe॒taḥ | sa॒masi̭ sthā॒tarhrī̭ṇām || 1 || tvā̭va̭taḥ | pu॒ru॒va॒so॒ iti̭ pu॒ru॒'va॒so॒ | va॒yam | indra̭ | pra॒ṇe॒taḥ iti̭ pra॒'ne॒taḥ | sa॒masi̭ | sthā॒taḥ | hrī̭ṇām || 1 ||
he puruvaso bahudhanendra praṇetaḥ karmaṇāṃ pāraṃ prakarṣeṇa prāpayitaḥ indra tvāvataḥ tvatsadṛśasya |
indrasamānasthānasyābhāvāttavetyarthaḥ |
nava svabhūtāḥ vayaṃ smasi smaḥ |
he hrīṇām etatsaṃjñakā- nāmnāṃ sthātaḥ adhiṣṭhātaḥ ||
tvāṃ hi sa॒tyama̭drivo vi॒śva dā॒tāra̭mi॒ṣām | vi॒śva dā॒tāra̭ṃ ra॒yī॒ṇām || 2 || tvām | hi | sa॒tyam | a॒dri'vaḥ | vi॒śva | dā॒tāra̭m | i॒ṣām | vi॒śva | dā॒tāra̭m | ra॒yī॒ṇām || 2 ||
he adrivaḥ asti śatrumityadrivajraḥ |
tadvacchindra tvāṃ satyaṃ niścayama iṣām annānāṃ dātāraṃ viśva jānīmaḥ |
tathā rayīṇāṃ dhanānāṃ dātāraṃ viśva ||
ā yasya̭ te ma॒hi॒māna̭ṃ śa॒tambhṷ̄te śa॒takra̭to |
gī॒rbhirgṛ̭ṇanti॒ kāra̭vaḥ ||
3 ||
1. ga-ta-bha-śa-bṛhatī | 2. kha-ga-ta-bha-uṣṇip | 3. kha-gha-ta-na-bha-śa-kanīna | 4. ga-ityantametat | 5. kha-gha-jha-ta-2-bha-śa-pranetaḥ; ga-prāṇetaḥ | 6. na-jānīma |
ā | yasya̭ | tḙ | ma॒hi॒māna̭m | śata̭m'ūte | śata̭krato iti śata̭'krato | gīḥ'bhiḥ | gṛ॒ṇanti̭ | kā॒rava̭ḥ || 3 || he ᵥśatam‌ūte aparimitarakṣaṇa he ᵥśatakrato bahukarmayuktendra ᵥyasya ᵥte ᵥmahimānaṃ māhātmyaṃ ᵥkāravaḥ stotāraḥ ᵥgīrbhiḥ stutibhiḥ ᵥgṛṇanti stuvanti svāmīṣṭāya ||
su॒nī॒tho gha॒ sa ma॒rtyo yaṃ ma॒ruto̭ yama̭rya॒mā | mi॒traḥ | pā॒ntya॒druha̭ḥ || 4 || su॒nī॒thaḥ | gha॒ | saḥ | ma॒rtyaḥ | yam | ma॒ruta̭ḥ | yam | a॒rya॒mā | mi॒traḥ | pā॒nti̭ | a॒druha̭ḥ ||4|| ᵥsa ᵥmartyaḥ manuṣyo yajamānaḥ ᵥsunīthaḥ suyajñaḥ sunayano vā bhavati | ᵥgha iti prasiddhau | sa ityuktaṃ kamityāha | ᵥyaṃ yajamānaṃ ᵥmarutaḥ devāḥ ᵥpānti rakṣanti ᵥadruhaḥ adrohakartāraḥ | tathā ᵥyamaryamā pāti | yaṃ ca ᵥmitraḥ pāti | sa evaṃ bhavatīti ||
da॒rdhāno̭ goma̭daśva̭tsu॒vīrya̭mādi॒tyarja̭ta edhate | sa॒dā rā॒yā pṷru॒spṛhā || 5 || da॒rdhāna̭ḥ | go'ma̭t | a॒śva̭'vat | su॒'vīrya̭m | ā॒di॒tya'rja̭taḥ | e॒dha॒te | sa॒dā | rā॒yā | pu॒ru॒spṛhā̭ || 5 || ᵥādityarjutaḥ ādityaprerita ādityānugṛhīto' yajamānaḥ ᵥgomat gobhirupetam ᵥaśvavat aśvairupetaṃ ᵥsuvīryaṃ śobhanavīryopetaṃ putraṃ ᵥdadhānaḥ dhārayan ᵥedhate vardhate ᵥsadā sarvadā | kiṃca ᵥpuruspṛhā bahubhiḥ spṛhaṇīyena ᵥrāyā dhanena sadaivadhate || || 5 ||
tamindraṃ dā॒rnamī̭mahe śa॒vasā̭nama॒bhīrva̭m | ī॒śāna̭ṃ rā॒ya ī̭mahe || 6 || tam | indra̭m | dā॒rnam | ī॒mahḙ | śa॒vasā̭nam | a॒bhīrva̭m | ī॒śāna̭m | rā॒yaḥ | ī॒mahḙ || 6 || ᵥtaṃ prasiddham ᵥindraṃ ᵥdānaṃ deyaṃ ᵥrāyaḥ dhanam ᵥīmahe yācāmahe | kīdṛśamindram | ᵥśavasānaṃ balamācarantam ᵥabhīrvam abhīrum ᵥīśānaṃ sarvasya svāminam ||
tasmi̭nhi sa॒ntyu॒tayo॒ viśvā̭ a॒bhīrnuḥ sa॒cā | tamā va̭hantu sa॒rmaya̭ḥ pu॒ruva̭suṃ ma॒dāya̭ ha॒raya̭ḥ su॒tam || 7 || tasmi̭n | hi | sa॒nti | ū॒taya̭ḥ | viśvā̭ḥ | a॒bhīrva̭ḥ | sa॒cā | tam | ā | va॒ha॒ntu | sa॒rmaya̭ḥ | pu॒ru'va̭sum | ma॒dāya̭ | ha॒raya̭ḥ | su॒tam || 7 || ᵥtasmin indre ᵥūtayaḥ gantavyaḥ ᵥviśvāḥ sarvāḥ ᵥabhīravaḥ akātarāḥ ᵥsacā sahāyabhūtā marudrūpāḥ senāḥ ᵥsanti bhavanti | athavā tasmin sarvāṃ rakṣaṇāḥ saha saṃbhavanti | ᵥtam indraṃ ᵥsasayaḥ sarpaṇaśīlāḥ ᵥharayaḥ aśvāḥ ᵥpuruvasuṃ bahudhanam | bahudhanapradamityarthaḥ | taṃ ᵥmadāya ᵥsutam abhiṣutaṃ somaṃ prati ᵥā ᵥvahantu āgamayantu ||
yastḙ ma॒do va̭re॒ṇyo ya indra̭ vṛ॒traha̭ntamaḥ |
ya ā̭da॒diḥ sva̭rni॒bhiḥ pṛ̭ta॒nāsṷ du॒ṣṭara̭ḥ ||
8 ||
1. kha-ga-ta-ādityānugato; na-ādityānugato |
2. kha-ga-ta-na-bha-śa- 'rāyaḥ' nāsti |
yaḥ | te | madaḥ | vareṇyaḥ | yaḥ | indra | vṛtrahan'tamaḥ | yaḥ | ā'dadiḥ | svaḥ | nṛ'bhiḥ | yaḥ | pṛtanāsu | dustaraḥ || 8 ||
pūrvamantre madāya haraya ityuktaṃ sa madaḥ stūyate |
he indra te tava yaḥ madaḥ vareṇyaḥ varaṇīyaḥ |
yaḥ ca madaḥ saṃgrāme vṛtrahantamaḥ śatrūṇāmatiśayena hantā |
yaḥ ca ādadiḥ ādātā svaḥ svaraṇaṃ dhanaṃ nṛbhiḥ nṛbhyaḥ śatrubhyaḥ |
yaḥ ca pṛtanāsu saṃgrāmeṣu dustaraḥ anabhibhāvyaḥ |
tasmai madāya harayo vahnivati ||
yo duṣṭarau viśvavāra śravāyyo vājeṣvasti tarutā |
sa naḥ śaviṣṭha savanā vaso gahi gumem gormati vraje ||
9 ||
yaḥ | dustaraḥ | viśva'vāra | śravāyyaḥ | vājeṣu | asti | tarutā | saḥ | naḥ | śaviṣṭha | savanā | ā | vaso iti | gahi | gumem | go'rmati | vraje || 9 ||
he viśvavāra viśvairvarṇīyendra vājeṣu yuddheṣu yaḥ tava duṣṭaraḥ duḥkhena tarītuṃ śakyaḥ tarutā śatrūṇāṃ tārakaḥ asti bhavati he vaso vāsaka he śaviṣṭha atiśayena balavannindra saḥ tvaṃ naḥ savanā asmākaṃ savanāni ā gahi āgaccha |
vayaṃ ca gomati vraje gomantaṃ vrajaṃ gamema gacchema ||
gumyo ṣu ṇo yathā purāśvayota rathyā |
varivasya mahāmaha ||
10 ||
gumyo iti | su | naḥ | yathā | purā | aśva'yā | uta | ratha'yā | varivasya | mahā'maha || 10 ||
he mahāmaha mahābhenendra gamyā |
gamyā u iti nipātānipātadvayasamudāyasyādivadbhāvena nipātatvāt prakṛtibhāvaḥ |
asmākaṃ gāmicchayā asmākaṃ gā dātuṃ yathā purā pūrvaṃ yathāsmākaṃ gavādidānāya varivasyasi tadvadadyāpi su suṣṭhu varivasya paricara |
āgacchetyarthaḥ |
na kevalaṃ gavecchayā kintu aśvayā aśvapradānecchayā |
uta api ca rathayā rathecchayā ca varivasyeti ||
2 ||
nahi tai śūra rādhaso'ntaṃ vindāmi satrā |
daśasyā nau maghavan cidadriva dhiyo vājebhirāvitha ||
11 ||
nahi | te | śūra | rādhasaḥ | antam | vindāmi | satrā | daśasya | naḥ | maghavan | nu | cit | adri'vaḥ | dhiyaḥ | vājebhiḥ | āviya || 11 ||
he śūra vikrāntendra te tava rādhasaḥ dhanasya antam ittāṃ satrā satyaṃ nahi vindāmi na labhe |
yasmādevaṃ tasmāt he maghavan dhanavan he adrivaḥ vajravannindra naḥ asmākaṃ nū cit kṣipra- meva daśasya dehi taddhanam |
