ya ṛṣvaḥ śrāvyutsarkhā viśvetsa veda janimā puruhūtaḥ |
taṃ viśve mānuṣā yugendraṃ havante taviṣaṃ yatasrucaḥ ||
12 ||
1. kha-ga-ta-na-bha-śa- 'tava' nāsti | 2. gha-tārakaḥ śravāyyaḥ śravaṇīyaḥ dayaḥ | 3. gha-naḥ asmākaṃ | 4. kha-gha-ta-na-bha-śa-yathāsmākaṃ yāgaṃ | 5. gha-labhe khalu | 6. ga-rakṣita |
yaḥ ! ṛṣvaḥ | śravayat'sakhā | viśvā | it | saḥ | veda | janima | puru'stutaḥ | tam | viśvai | mānuṣā | yugā | indram | havante | taviṣam | yata'ścucaḥ || 12 ||
yaḥ indraḥ ṛṣvaḥ darśanīyaḥ śravayat'sakhā |
śrāvayantaḥ sakhāya ṛtvijo yasya sa tādṛśaḥ śrāvayassakhā |
puruhūtaḥ bahubhiryajamānaiḥ stuto ya indraḥ saḥ viśvat sarvāṇyapi janimā janmāni prāṇināṃ veda jānāti |
taṃ taviṣaṃ balavantam indraṃ viśve sarve'pyadhvarvādayaḥ yataścucaḥ svīkṛtahaviṣkāḥ santaḥ mānuṣā manuṣyasambandhinaḥ yugā yugāni kālān sarveṣu kāleṣu havante āhva- yanti stuvanti ||
sa no vājeṣvavitā puruvasuḥ puraḥsthātā maghavā vṛtrahā bhuvat ||13|| saḥ | naḥ | vājeṣu | avitā | puru'vasuḥ | puraḥ'sthātā | magha'vā | vṛtra'hā | bhuvat ||1
he indra tvāṃ viśveṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ vasūnāṃ dhanānām harayantam īśānam asya varṣasaḥ vārakasya kṛpayataḥ yuddhaṃ kalpayataḥ śatroḥ sāsahāṃsam abhivitāraṃ stuvata iti śeṣaḥ |
sa tvaṃ nūnaṃ kṣipamadyādīnāmīpi dhanaṃ prayacchetyarthaḥ ||
mahaḥ su vo aramiṣe stavāmahe mīḻahuṣe araṃgamāya jagmaye |
yajñebhirgīrbhirviśvamanuṣāṃ marutāmiyakṣasi gāye tvā narmasā girā ||
17||
mahaḥ | su | vaḥ | aram | ise | stavāmahe | mīḻahuṣe | aram'gamaya | jagmaye | yajñebhiḥ | gīḥ'bhiḥ | viśva'manuṣām | marutām | iyakṣasi | gāye | tvā | narmasā | girā ||17||
he indra mahaḥ mahataḥ vaḥ |
tavetyarthaḥ |
vyatyayena bahuvacanam |
araṃ gamanamasmadiṣaya- micchāmi |
aṃtaramiti rūpam |
tadartha mīḻahuṣe sektre araṃgamāya saṃpūrṇagamanāya jagmaye gamana- śīlāya yajñaṃ prati evaṃbhūtāya devāya stavāmahe |
tvāṃ stuma ityarthaḥ |
kena sādhaneneti taducyate |
yajñebhiḥ yajanasādhanairhavirbhiryajñairvā gīrbhiḥ stutibhiḥ |
he deva viśvamanuṣāṃ viśveṣāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ yaṣṭṛṇām iyakṣasi etairyajyase |
marutāṃ saṃbandhī tvam |
kiṃca tvā tvāṃ namasā namaskāreṇa girā stutyā ca gāye stuve ||
ye pātayante ajmabhirgirīṇāṃ snubhireṣām |
yajñaṃ mahiṣvarṇīnāṃ sumnaṃ tuvisvarṇīnāṃ prādhvare ||
18 ||
ye | pātayante | ajma'bhiḥ | girīṇām | snu'bhiḥ | eṣām | yajñam | mahi'svarṇīnām | sumnam | tuvi'svarṇīnām | prādhvare || 18 ||
ye mahataḥ girīṇāṃ meghānāṃ snubhiḥ prasnavadbhiḥ ajmabhiḥ balaiḥ balakarairudakaiḥ saha pātayante patanti gacchanti eṣāṃ marutāṃ mahiṣvarṇīnāṃ prabhūtadhvanīnāṃ yajñaṃ kurma iti śeṣaḥ |
kṛtvā ca tuvisvarṇīnāṃ bahudhvanīnāṃ sumnaṃ sukhaṃ taiḥ kṛtam adhvare yajñe pra prāpnuyāma |
athavā caturthyarthe ṣaṣṭhī |
prabhūtasvanebhyaḥ sumnamuktalakṣaṇaṃ haviḥ pra prāpnuyāmādhvare yajñe ||
prabhajaṃ durmatīnāmindra śaviṣṭhā bhara |
rayimasmabhyaṃ yujyaṃ codayanmate jyeṣṭhaṃ codayanmate ||
19 ||
pra'bhajam | duḥ'matīnām | indra | śaviṣṭha | ā | bhara | rayim | asmabhyam | yujyam | codayat'mate | jyeṣṭham | codayat'mate || 19 ||

he indra śaviṣṭha atiśayena balavan stutastvam asmabhyaṃ rayi dhanaṃ yujyaṃ yogyamasmākamucitaṃ

dānāsaḥ pṛthuśravasaḥ kānītasya surādhasaḥ | rathaṃ hiraṇyayaṃ dadanmaṃhiṣṭhaḥ sūrirabhūdvarṣiṣṭhakṛta śravaḥ || 24 || dānāsaḥ | pṛthu'śravasaḥ | kānītasya | su'rādhasaḥ | ratham | hiraṇyayam | dadat | maṃhiṣṭhaḥ | sūriḥ | abhūt | varṣiṣṭham | akuruta | śravaḥ || 24 || pūrvemantraiḥ pratipāditāni dhanāni' bandhūnāṃ piturvā purastānnirdiṣṭāśrāste | 'pṛthuśravasaḥ 'kānītasya 'surādhasaḥ śobhanadhanasya | yatastasya dhanaṃ kalpatamataḥ sa surādhāḥ | tasya 'dānāsaḥ dānāni dhanānīmāni | sa ca pṛthuśravāḥ pūrvamuktāni 'hiraṇyayaṃ hiraṇmayaṃ 'rathaṃ ca 'dadat prayacchan 'maṃhiṣṭhaḥ atiśayena dātā 'sūriḥ sarvasya prerakaḥ prājño vā 'abhūt bhavati bhavatu vā | 'varṣiṣṭham atiśayena pravṛddhāṃ 'śravaḥ kīrtim 'akuruta karoti karotu vā || 'ā no vāyo' ityeṣā pañcame'hani praugaśastre vāyavyatṛce tṛtīyā | 'ā no vāyo mahe tana ityekā rathena pṛthupājasā' (āśva. śrau. 7. 12) iti hi sūtritam ||

ᐁyasmai manuṣyāya ᐁkṣayaṃ nivāsaṃ ᐁjīvātuṃ jīvanasādhanamacaṃ ᐁpracetasaḥ prakṛṣṭamatayaḥ ᐁarāsata prayacchanti tasmai yajamānāya tadartham ᐁime ᐁādityāḥ ᐁviśvasya ᐁghet sarvasyāpyadhuḥ ᐁmanoḥ’ manuṣyasya dhanikasya ᐁrāyaḥ dhanasya ᐁīśate svāmino bhavantyapahatya yajamānāya pradātum ||
pari̭ ṇo॒ vṛṇa̭ja॒nghā du॒rgāṇi̭ ra॒thyo yathā̭ | syāme॒dindra̭sya॒ śarma̭ṇyādi॒tyānā̭mu॒tā'va̭syane॒haso̭ va ū॒taya̭ḥ su॒ūta̭yo va ū॒taya̭ḥ ||5|| pari̭ | naḥ | vṛṇa̭ja॒n | a॒ghā | duḥ'gāni̭ | ra॒thyaḥ | yathā̭ | syāma̭ | it | i॒ndra̭sya | śarma̭ṇi | ā॒dityā̭nām | u॒ta | a॒va̭si | anḙhasaḥ | vaḥ | ū॒taya̭ḥ | su'ṷ̄tayaḥ | vaḥ | ū॒taya̭ḥ || 5 ||

na taṃ ti॒gmaṃ ca̭na॒ tyajo̭ na drā॒sadṷbhi॒ taṃ gu॒ru | yasmā̭ u॒ śama̭ sa॒pratha॒ ārdityā̭so a॒rāgha̭va॒manḙhaso va ū॒taya̭ḥ su॒ūta̭yo va ū॒taya̭ḥ ||7|| na | tam | ti॒gmam | ca॒na | tyaja̭ḥ | na | drā॒sat | a॒bhi | tam | gu॒ru | yasmai̭ | ū~ iti̭ | śama̭ | sa॒'pratha̭ḥ | ā॒dityā̭saḥ | a॒rāgha̭vam | anḙhasaḥ | vaḥ | ū॒taya̭ḥ | su'ṷ̄tayaḥ | vaḥ | ū॒taya̭ḥ || 7 ||
ᐁtaṃ manuṣyaṃ ᐁtigmaṃ ᐁcana tīkṣṇameva santaṃ ᐁtyajaḥ | krodhanāmaitat | atra krodhāt prayujyamāna- māyudhamucyate | ᐁna ᐁdrāsat | ‘drā kutsāyāṃ gatau’ | kutsitaṃ nācchati 1 | na hinastītyarthaḥ | tathā ᐁtaṃ janaṃ ᐁguru pravṛddhamaparihārahaṃ duḥkhaṃ ᐁna drāsat na gacchati | he ᐁādityāḥ : ādityāḥ : ᐁsaprathaḥ :

samānaprathanā: sarvataḥ pṛthubhūtā vā yūyaṃ yasmā ḍau yasmai yajamānāya | uśaśabda: pūraṇa: | śarma sukham 'arādhyam adatta taṃ ma’ drāsaditi || yuṣme devā arpi mpasi yudhyantaiva varmasu | yuyaṃ maho na enaso yuyamabhīdurupyatānehasau va ūtayaḥ sukṛtayau va ūtayaḥ ||8|| yuṣme iti | devāḥ | arpi | mpasi | yudhyantaḥ'iva | varma'su | yuyam | mahaḥ | naḥ | enasaḥ | yuyam | abhīt | urupyata | anehasaḥ | vaḥ | ūtayaḥ | su'ūtayaḥ | vaḥ | ūtayaḥ || 8 || he devāḥ ādityāḥ yuṣme yuṣmāsu vayam api smasi api bhavema | yuṣmābhirapahitāḥ smetyarthaḥ | tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ | yudhyantaḥ śūrāḥ varmasu kavaceṣu yathā bhavanti tadvat | yuyaṃ naḥ asmān mahaḥ mahataḥ enasaḥ pāpāt urupyata rakṣata | tathā yūyam asmān abhīt alpādyenasaḥ urupyata || arditinaṃ urupyatarditiḥ śarma yacchatu | mātā mitrasya revatau'ryammo varuṇasya cānehasau va ūtayaḥ sukṛtayau va ūtayaḥ ||9|| aditiḥ | naḥ | urupyatu | aditiḥ | śarma | yacchatu | mātā | mitrasya | revataḥ | aryamṇaḥ | varuṇasya | ca | anehasaḥ | vaḥ | ūtayaḥ | su'ūtayaḥ | vaḥ | ūtayaḥ || 9 || naḥ asmān aditiḥ akhaṇḍanīyā devamātā urupyatu rakṣatu | tathā aditiḥ śarma sukhaṃ yacchatu | aditirviśeṣyate | yā mātā nirmātrī | kasya | mitrasya revataḥ dhanavataḥ aryamṇaḥ varuṇasya ca | sā na urupyatviti || yadaivāḥ śarma śaraṇaṃ yadbhadraṃ yadanāturam | tridhātu yadvarūthyaṃ tadasmāsu vi yantanānehasau va ūtayaḥ sukṛtayau va ūtayaḥ ||10|| yat | devāḥ | śarma | śaraṇam | yat | bhadram | yat | anāturam | tri'dhātu | yat | varūthyam | tat | asmāsu | vi | yantana | anehasaḥ | vaḥ | ūtayaḥ | su'ūtayaḥ | vaḥ | ūtayaḥ || 10 || he devāḥ ādityāḥ yat śarma sukhaṃ śaraṇaṃ śaraṇīyam | yat bhadraṃ sarvabhaṃjanīyam | yat anāturaṃ rogarahitam | yat tridhātu triguṇam | yadvarūthyam | varūthaṃ gṛham | tadarham | tat ūphaguṇaṃ śarma asmāsu vi yantana viyacchata || 8 || ādityā ava hi khyatādhi kūlādiva sparśaḥ | sutīrthamarvato yāthānu no neṣathā sugarmanehasau va ūtayaḥ sukṛtayau va ūtayaḥ ||11|| 1. ta-na-tana; bha-nata; śa-tena | 2. ta-śa-pāpa; na-bha1-2- pāpa |
ādityāḥ | ava̭ | hi | ryata | adhi̭ | kū॒lāt'iva | sparśaḥ | su'tīrtham | arva̭taḥ | yathā̭ | anṷ | naḥ | nepatha | su'gam | anehasa̭ḥ | vaḥ | ūtaya̭ḥ | su'ūtaya̭ḥ | vaḥ | ūtaya̭ḥ || 11 ||

yathā kūlasthaḥ puruṣo'dhogatamudakaṃ jijñāsustatasthaṃ manuṣyāṃ vā vilokayitumavāpakṣyati tadvat |
tathā kṛtvā sutīrtha śobhanāvatāra- pradeśam arvataḥ aśvān yathā prāpayantyaśvarakṣakāstadvat naḥ asmān sugaṃ supanthānam anu nepatha anunayatha ||
neha bhadraṃ rakṣasvine nāvayai nopayā uta | garve ca bhadraṃ dhenavai vīrāya ca śravasyatai'nehasau va ūtayaḥ suūtayau va ūtayaḥ||12|| na | iha | bhadram | rakṣasvinḙ | na | atra'yai | na | upa'yai | uta | garve | ca | bhadram | dhenavai̭ | vīrāya̭ | ca | śravasyatḙ | anehasa̭ḥ | vaḥ | ūtaya̭ḥ | mu'ūtaya̭ḥ | vaḥ | ūtaya̭ḥ || 12 ||
he ādityāḥ iha bhūmau bhadraṃ kalyāṇaṃ sukhaṃ rakṣasvine rakṣo balam |
balavate'smadreṣṭre na bhavatviti śeṣaḥ |
atrayai asmān hiṃsitumagacchate na bhavatu bhadram |
tathā upayai upagacchate na bhavatu |
tarhi kasya bhavatvityucyate |
gave ca bhadraṃ yuṣmadīyaṃ2 bhavatu |
caśabdo vakṣyamāṇa- dhenvādyapekṣaḥ |
kiṃca dhenave navaprasūtikāyai bhadraṃ bhavatu |
vīrāya asmatputrādikāya bhadraṃ bhavatu |
kīdṛśāya vīrāya |
śravasyate annamicchate |
athottarārdhe'pi netyanuvartate |
asmadvirodhino gavādi- kāya bhadraṃ na bhavatviti tasyārthaḥ ||
yadāviryadapīcya̭ṃ devāso asti duṣkṛtam | tritai tadviśvamāpatya āre asmaddhātanānehasau va ūtayaḥ suūtayau va ūtayaḥ||13|| yat | āvi̭ḥ | yat | apīcya̭m | devāsa̭ḥ | asti | duḥ'kṛtam | tritai | tat | viśva̭m | āptyḙ | ārḙ | asmat | dadhātana | anehasa̭ḥ | vaḥ | ūtaya̭ḥ | su'ūtaya̭ḥ | vaḥ | ūtaya̭ḥ || 13 ||
he devāsaḥ devā ādityāḥ yadāviḥ yat pāpmāvirbhūtam asti 1 duṣkṛtaṃ yat ca apīcyam antarhitamasti |

tadviśvaṃ tadubhayam āptye tritai mayi mā bhūt |
kiṃtu asmat āre dūre dadhātana sthāpayata ||
‘yacca goṣu’ ityādisūktaśeṣeṇa duḥsvamaṃ dṛṣṭādityamupatiṣṭheta |
tathā ca ‘svamamanojñaṃ dṛṣṭvā’ ityupakramya ‘yacca goṣu duḥsvapnyamiti pañcabhirādityamupatiṣṭheta’ (āśva. gṛ. 3. 6. 6) iti sūtritam ||
1. kha-ta-bha-śa-śobhanāvatara°; gha-śobhana° |
2. kha-ta-bha-anunayatha ānukūlyena prāpayatha |
3. gha- asmadīyaṃ |

svārthiko yat |

gopīḍānimintakasmākaṃ pīḍānimintakaṃ ca yahuḥsvapnaṃ paśyāma ityarthaḥ |
tat sarvaṃ he vibhāvari |
uponāmainat |
he vyucchana- vati devi āpyāya tritāya parā vaha dūre parihara ||
niṣkaṃ vā ghā kṛṇavate srajaṃ vā duhitardivaḥ | trite duṣapnyaṃ sarvamāptye pari dadhasyanehasau va ūtayaḥ ||15|| niṣkam | vā | gha | kṛṇavate | srajam | vā | duhitaḥ | divaḥ | trite | duḥsvapnyam | sarvam | āptye | pari | dadhasi | anehasaḥ | vaḥ | ūtayaḥ | su'ūtayaḥ | vaḥ | ūtayaḥ | || 15 ||
he divaḥ duhitaḥ uṣaḥ niṣkaṃ vā gha ābharaṇaviśeṣaṃ vā kṛṇavate kurvate svarṇakārāya yat duṣvapnyaṃ dṛṣṭam |
svarṇakāreṇa nirmāṇasamaye dṛṣṭamityarthaḥ |
veti pūraṇaḥ |
vāśabdaścārthe |

kurvāṇa ityarthaḥ |

vayaṃ tritāḥ parityajāmetyarthaḥ |

tadannāya |

tathā tadapase |

1. kha-nā-bha1-3-duḥsvapnaṃ; ta-na-bhara-duḥsvapnaṃ; śa-duḥṣvapnyaṃ |
2. kha-ta-bha-paridadhasyupari; gha-na-śa- paridadhasi pari |
3. ga-niṣkṛṣṭayoḥ; tara-2-śa-niṣkṛṣṭayoḥ; na-niṣkṛṣyetayoḥ |
4. kha-gha-ta-bha- devāha; na-tadevā |
syāṃśam ᵥupaseduṣe prāpnuvate ᵥtritāya ᵥdvitāya ᵥca he ᵥuṣaḥ devi ᵥduṣṣvapnyam añjhakarmaviṣayaṃ ᵥvaha anyatra prāpaya |

yathā kalāṃ yathā śaphaṃ yatha ṛṇaṃ saṃnayāmasi |
evā duṣṣvapnyaṃ sarvamāptye saṃ nayāmasyanehasau va ūtayaḥ sukṛtayau va ūtayaḥ|

yathā | kalām | yathā | śapham | yathā | ṛṇam | sam'nayāmasi | eva | duḥ'svapnyam | sarvam | āptye | sam | nayāmasi | anehasaḥ | vaḥ | ūtayaḥ | su'ūtayaḥ | vaḥ | ūtayaḥ || 17 ||
saṃjñapitaṃ paśuṃ dānārthaṃ saṃskṛtavantaḥ ᵥyathā yena prakāreṇa ᵥkalāṃ ᵥśapham iti saṃdāyānyatra saṃnayanti |
athāparo yathāśabdaḥ pūraṇaḥ |
athavā |
yathā kalāṃ hṛdayādyavayavamavadānāhaṃ saṃnayanti yathā ca śaphaṃ śaphopalakṣitamanavadānāhaṃ śaphāsthyādikaṃ saṃnayanti ᵥyathā vā ᵥṛṇaṃ śanaiḥ saṃna- yanti ᵥeva evaṃ ᵥduṣṣvapnyaṃ ᵥsarvamāptye vartamānaṃ ᵥsaṃ ᵥnayāmasi saṃnayāmaḥ apasārayāmaḥ |

‘svādorabhakṣi’ iti pañcadaśarcaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ sūktaṃ kāṇvasya pragāthasyārṣa somadevatākaṃ traiṣṭubham |
pañcamī jagata |
somo devatā |

sūktaviniyogo laiṅgikaḥ |
svādorabhakṣi vayasaḥ sumedhāḥ svādhyo varivovittasya |
viśve yaṃ devā uta martyāso madhu bruvanto abhi saṃcaranti |
1 |
svādoḥ | abhakṣi | vayasaḥ | su'medhāḥ | su'adhyaḥ | varivovit'tasya | viśve | yam | devāḥ | uta | martyāsaḥ | madhu | bruvantaḥ | abhi | sam'caranti || 1 ||
ahaṃ pragāthaḥ ᵥsumedhāḥ śobhanaprajñaḥ ᵥsvādhyaḥ svādhyayanaḥ sukarmā ᵥvarivovittasya atiśayena pūjāṃ labhamānasya ᵥsvādoḥ suṣṭudanīyasya svādubhūtasya ᵥvayasaḥ annasya |
etāḥ karmāṇi ṣaṣṭhyaḥ |
1. kha-ta-saṃstuvanto; ga-stuvanto; na-saṃstuvanto |
2. kha-ga-ta-na-śa-‘ ca ’ nāsti |
3. kha-ga- ta-na-bha-śa-apāgacchatu |
uphalakṣaṇaṃ vayo'nnaṃ somākhyam 'abhakṣi-akṣayeya'|
yaṃ padaṃ 'viśve 'devāḥ sarve'pīndrādayaḥ 'uta api ca 'martyāsaḥ martyā manuṣyāḥ 'madhu 'bhuvantaḥ manoharametaditi śabdayantaḥ 'abhi 'saṃcaranti abhisaṃgacchante prāpnuvanti tadannamabhakṣīti |
aṣobhapraṇayane 'antaśca 'ityeṣā |

antaśca prāgā aditirbhāsavayātā haraso daivyasya |
indvindrasya sakhyaṃ juṣāṇaḥ śrauṣṭhīva dhuramanu rāya ṛdhyāḥ |
2 |
antariti | ca | pra | argāḥ | aditiḥ | bhavāsi | ava'yātā | harasaḥ | daivyasya | indo iti | indrasya | sakhyam | juṣāṇaḥ | śrauṣṭhī'iva | dhuram | anu | rāye | ṛdhyāḥ ||2||

hṛdayasya yāgāgārasya vāntargacchasi |

hara iti krodhanāma |

śruṣṭīti kṣipranāma |
tatsaṃbandhī śrauṣṭī |
kṣiprābhyaśca 'dhuram 'iva 'rāye asmākaṃ dhanalābhāya 'anu 'ṛdhyāḥ anugacchasi |
athavāśvo yathā dhuraṃ tvvābhimatedeśaṃ prāpayati tadvadasmān prāpaya |
anupūrvaṃ ṛdhigaṃtyarthaḥ |
'apāma somam' ityādike dve somapānottarakālīnāsthābhimarśane hṛdayābhimarśane ca krameṇa viniyukte |
tathā ca sūtritam—'apāma somamamṛtā abhūma śaṃ no bhava hṛda ā pīta indviti mukhahṛdaye abhimṛśeran' (āśva. śrau 5. 6) iti |
apāma somamamṛtā abhūma jyotirvidāma devān |
kiṃ nūnamasmānkṛṇavadarātiḥ kimu dhūrtiramṛta martyasya |

kurmaḥ |

yatra yasmin some pīte āyuḥ āyuṣyaṃ pratirante vardhayanti manuṣyāstaṃ somamagama iti ||
yo na induḥ pitaro hatsu pīto'martyo martya ā viveśa |
tasmai somāya haviṣā vidhema maḻīke asya sumatau syāma ||
12 ||
yaḥ | naḥ | induḥ | pitaraḥ | hatsu | pītaḥ | amartyaḥ | matirahitaḥ | martyān | ā | viveśa | tasmai | somāya | haviṣā | vidhema | maḻīke | asya | su'matau | syāma || 12 ||
he pitaraḥ yaḥ induḥ hatsu pītaḥ san amartyaḥ matirahitaḥ san āviveśa martyān naḥ asmān tasmai somāya haviṣā vidhema paricarema |
asya somasya maḻīke sukhe sumatau cānugrahabuddhau ca syāma bhavema ||
mahāpitṛyajñe 'somāya pitṛmate puroḍāśam' ityatra 'tvaṃ soma' iti yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca— 'tvaṃ soma pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidāno barhiṣadaḥ pitara ūrvvān' (āśva. śrau. 2. 19) iti |
tṛtīyasavane saumyasya carorapīyaṃ yājyā |
'tvaṃ soma pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidāna iti saumyasya' (āśva. śrau. 5. 19) iti hi sūtritam ||
tvaṃ soma pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidāno'nu dyāvāpṛthivī ā tatantha |
tasmai ta indo haviṣā vidhema vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīṇām ||
13 ||
tvam | soma | pitṛ'bhiḥ | sam'vidānaḥ | anu | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | ā | tatantha | tasmai | te | indo iti | haviṣā | vidhema | vayam | syāma | patayaḥ | rayīṇām || 13 ||
he soma tvaṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha saṃvidānaḥ saṃgacchamānaḥ dyāvāpṛthivī dyāvāpṛthivyau anu ā tatantha krameṇa vistārayasi |
tasmai somāya haviṣā vidhema paricarema |
vayaṃ rayīṇāṃ dhanānāṃ patayaḥ syāma bhavema ||
trātāro devā adhi vocatā no mā no nidrā īśata mota jalpiḥ |
vayaṃ somasya viśvahe priyārsaḥ suvīrāso vidathamā vadema ||
14 ||
trātāraḥ | devāḥ | adhi | vocata | naḥ | mā | naḥ | nidrā | īśata | mā | uta | jalpiḥ | vayam | somasya | viśvahe | priyārsaḥ | su'vīrāsaḥ | vidatham | ā | vadema || 14 ||
he trātāraḥ rakṣitāro he devāḥ naḥ asmān adhi vocata adhivacanaṃ kuruta |
kiṃca naḥ asmān nidrāḥ svamāḥ mā īśata īśvarā mā bhūvan bādhitum |
uta api ca jalpiḥ |
1. khata-na-bha-śa-'pīḍāḥ' nāsti | 2. khata-na-bha-1-2-śa-aparāme; gha-tyajaṃtu aparādhe | 3. ta-na-bha-śa-martau | 4. kha-ta-bha-'pīyameva |
nindakaḥ asmān mā nindatu |
vayaṃ somasya priyāsaḥ priyāḥ syāma viśvaha sarveṣvapyahaḥsu |
sarvadetyarthaḥ |
subīrāsaḥ śobhanaputrāḥ santaḥ vidathaṃ stotram ā vadema ābhimukhyena vadema |
athavā suputrā vidathaṃ gṛhamā vadema |
āvadan putrapautrāṇāṃ dhanenopacchandanm ||
'tvaṃ naḥ soma viśvataḥ' iti saumye paśau haviṣo'nuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'tvaṃ naḥ soma viśvato vayodhā yā te dhāmāni divi yā pṛthivyām' (āśva. śrau. 3. 7) iti ||
tvaṃ naḥ soma viśvatau vayodhāstvaṃ svarvadā viśa nṛcakṣāḥ |
tvaṃ na inda ūtibhiḥ sajoṣaḥ pāhi paścātāduta vā purastāt ||
15 ||
tvam | naḥ | soma | viśvataḥ | vayaḥdhāḥ | tvam | svaḥ'vit | ā | viśa | nṛ'cakṣāḥ | tvam | naḥ | indo iti | ūtibhiḥ | sa'joṣaḥ | pāhi | paścātāt | uta | vā | purastāt ||15||
he soma tvaṃ naḥ asmākaṃ viśvataḥ sarvābhyo digbhyaḥ vayodhāḥ annadātā |
tathā tvaṃ svarvit svargalabhakaḥ nṛcakṣāḥ sarvamanuṣyadraṣṭā tvam ā viśa |
he indo tvaṃ sajoṣaḥ saha prīyamāṇaḥ san ūtibhiḥ saha |
athavotayo gantāro marutaḥ |
taiḥ sahitaḥ san paścātāt paścāt uta vā purastāt ca pāhi ||

śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ | śrutikārthalakṣmī yo vikāsayati hṛdratam(? ॰tām) | sa pāyātścindānandaḥ sūryamaṇḍalago hariḥ || 'abhi pra vaḥ' iti daśarce prathamaṃ sūktaṃ praskaṇvasyārṣamaindram | anukrāntaṃ ca—'abhi pra daśa praskaṇvaḥ prāgāthaṃ tat' iti | ayujāṃ bṛhatī chandaḥ | yujāṃ satobṛhatī chandaḥ | viniyogaḥ sūtrādavagantavyaḥ || (1) ṛṣirātmānaṃ brūte | he praskaṇva vaḥ yuṣmākam | bahuvacanaṃ putrapautrābhiprāyeṇa | yuṣmatsvāmibhūtamindramabhilakṣya prācya prakarṣeṇa pūjaya | tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ | yathā vide vidvāṃsaṃ svaguruṃ pūjayasi tadvat | kīdṛśam | surādhasam | śobhanaṃ rādho dhanaṃ yasya tam | ko'sāvindra ityucyate | ya indraḥ maghavā dhana-

paraṃ vitareṣu sarveṣvapi pustakeṣvanupalambhān vyākhyāśailyā vibhinnatvānna naitatsāyaṇācāryakṛtamiti vyaktaṃ pratīyate |
tathāpyetadviduṣāmupayogāya syāditi matvātra dīyate |

tasya lekhanaṃ puṇyapattana evābhūditi śrūyate |

na kenāpi vārāṇasīsthena paṇḍitenaitadviracitamiti niścitameva' |

( 1 ) ṛṣirātmānaṃ brūte |
he puṣṭigo tvam abhiṣṭaye abhilaṣitasiddhaye taṃ śakraṃ sarvakarmasu śaktamindraṃ su suṣṭhu yathā tathā pra arca prakarṣeṇa pūjaya |

taṃ kam |
ya indraḥ munvate somābhiṣavaṃ kurvate stuvate stutiṃ kurvate yajamānāya kāmyaṃ kāminaṃ( ! kamanīyaṃ ) vasu dhanaṃ sahasreṇaiva asaṃkhyātameva maṃhate dadāti tam |
maṃhatidānārthaḥ |
dānakarmasu tatyāṭhāt ||
( 2 ) sutā abhiṣutāḥ somāḥ yadā īmenamindram amandiṣuḥ amādayan tadā asyendrasya hetayaḥ inasādhanānyāyudhāni maghavatsu havirlakṣaṇadhanavatsu yajamāneṣu pinvate |

( 3 ) ṛṣirātmānaṃ brūte |

vidhatiḥ paricaraṇakarmā |

( 4 ) yasmai indrāya parameśvarāya tadartham uttame pade utkṛṣṭatame sthāne dyulokākhye sthitam arkam arcanīyamādityamānucuḥ |
arcanti sarve |
‘ atha ya epo'ntarāditye hiraṇmayaḥ puruṣo dṛśyate ’ (chā. u. 1. 6. 6) ityādidhiyuktaṃ yadīyaṃ svarūpamupalavdham |
sarve sūryamārādhayantītyarthaḥ |
kīhaśamarkam |
saptaśīrmāṇam |
saptasaṃkhyākāḥ śīrṣavat pradhānabhūtā raśmayo yasya tādṛśam |
tridhātuṃ trayāṇāṃ bhūrādilokānāṃ poṣakam |
sūryasya trilokīpoṣakatvaṃ tu vṛṣṭādyāvyāpāreṇa prasiddham |
yatsvarūpalāmāya arkamarcanti sa tu parameśvaraḥ imā imāni dṛśyamānāni viśvā sarvāṇi bhuvanāni bhūtajātāni devā suramanuṣyarūpāṇi uddiśya acikradat ākrandati |
mādhyamikā vācā dadaśabdargama(? rdama) dayādānavācakaiḥ |
ādit anantarameva pauṃsyaṃ balaṃ śikṣālakṣaṇaṃ janiṣṭa janayati āvirbhāvayati |
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ |
parameśvara ādau meghastanitalakṣaṇayā svavāṇyā bhūkṣebhyo (? bhūsthebhyo) hitamupadiśati |
anākarṇite ca tasmin paścāddhāriyādilakṣaṇāṃ śikṣāmaviṣkarotīti |
atrābhihito'rtho bṛhadāraṇyake khilakāṇḍe ‘ trayā ha vai prājāpatyāḥ ’ ityādinā granthena prapañcitaḥ (bṛ. u. 5. 2) ||
( 5 ) ya indraḥ no'smākaṃ vasūnāṃ vāsahetūnāṃ dhanānāṃ dātā bhavati tamindraṃ parameśvaraṃ hūmahe āhvayāmo vayaṃ stotāraḥ |

yasmai tvaṃ vaṃso dānāya śikṣasi sa rāyaspoṣamaśrute | taṃ tvā vayaṃ maghavannindra girvaṇaḥ sutāyanto havāmahe || 6 || yasmai | tvam | vaso iti | dānāya | śikṣasi | saḥ | rāyaḥ | poṣam | aśnute | tam | tvā | vayam | maghavan | indra | girvaṇaḥ | sutāyantaḥ | havāmahe || 6 ||

( 6 ) he vaso vāsayitarindra tvaṃ yasmai yajamānāya dānāya dhanadānārthe śikṣasi ājñāpayasi sa yajamānaḥ rāyaḥ dhanasya poṣaṃ puṣṭimaśnute vyāpnoti |
yaṃ yajamānaṃ dhanaṃ dātuṃ pravartayasi sa tvadāśayā dhanalābhena vasusamṛddho bhavatītyāścaryam |
ato he girvaṇaḥ gīrbhirvandanīya maghavan dhanavannindra taṃ tādṛśaṃ tvā tvāṃ sutāyantaḥ abhiṣutasomā vayaṃ havāmahe āhvayāmaḥ ||
( 7 ) he maghavan dhanavannindra nu kṣipraṃ dānaṃ haviḥsamarpaṇaṃ nu niścitaṃ bhūya it pracuraṃ sadaiva te tava devasya dyotamānasya parameśvarasya upopet samīpameva pṛcyate tvayā saṃpṛktaṃ bhavati |
svalpamapi śīghraṃ satyātre samarpitaṃ bahulībhūya īśvare saṃgata bhavatītyabhiprāyaḥ |
ataḥ he indra parameśvara dāśuṣe havirdattavato yajamānasya starīḥ hiṃsakaḥ kadācana kadāpi nāsi na bhavasi |
api tu saścasi tena saṃgacchase |
tadīyaṃ sakhyaṃ saṃpādayasītyarthaḥ |

dhanasthāpahartā svadhanasya ca rakṣitelyarthaḥ |
tenendreṇa ruśame akalyāṇe arye arigaṇe tasmin tiraścit antarhitameva pavīravi |
pavī rathemistadntoḍarāḥ pavīrāḥ |
tadupetaṃ cakraṃ pavīravi |
atra 'pavī rathemirbhavati' (niru. 5. 5) ityādi niruktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
tādṛśaṃ cakramatyate prakṣipyate |
caturthapāde pratyakṣīkṛtya ucyate |
he indra tubhyameva tvadarthameva saḥ asuragaṇe vidyamānaḥ rayiḥ nikhilalakṣaṇo dhanarāśiḥ ajyate prakṣipyate'rigaṇena |
asuragaṇe guptaṃ cakradānamapi indrāya nidhipradamabhavat tadā saptātre guptaṃ dhanadānaṃ mahāphaladaṃ syāditi kimu vaktavyamityāśayaḥ ||

kīdṛśam |

kenābhiprāyeṇarcantītyucyate |
asme asmāsu rayiḥ dhanarāśiḥ praprathe prathito bhavatu |

kiñcāsme asmāsu suvānāsaḥ abhiṣutā indavaḥ somāḥ santu |
indrānu- graheṇaiva tatsarvamasmāsu saṃpadyatāmityāśayenetyarthaḥ ||

'yathā manau vivasvati' iti daśarce caturthe sūktam āyavamaindram | anukrāntaṃ ca—'yathā manāvāyuḥ' iti | kāṇva āyurṛṣiḥ | indro devatā | ayujāṃ bṛhatī chandaḥ | yujāṃ satobṛhatī chandaḥ | viniyogaḥ sūtrādavagantavyaḥ ||
(1) he śakra samarthendra yathā yena prakāreṇa vivasvati vivasvataḥ putre vaivasvate manau vartamānaṃ sutamabhiṣutaṃ somam apibaḥ pītavānasi |
he indra parameśvara yathā ca trita etannāmake ṛṣau vartamānaṃ chandaḥ vedajātaṃ jujoṣasi bhṛśaṃ sevase |
tathā āyau etannāmake mayi sacā saha vartamānaṃ somaṃ chandolakṣaṇaṃ tadubhayaṃ saṃsevya mādayase ānandayasi māmātmānaṃ ca ||
pṛṣre medhyai mātiśvanīndraṃ suvāne amandathāḥ | yathā somaṃ darśiśre darśoṇye syūmaraśmārjūnasi || 2 || pṛṣre | medhyai | mātiśvani | indra | suvāne | amandathāḥ | yathā | somam | darśiśre | darśoṇye | syūmaraśmau | arjūnasi || 2 ||
ya ukthā kevalā dudhe yaḥ somaṃ dhṛṣitāpibat | yasmai viṣṇustrīṇi padā vicakrama upa mitrasya dharmabhiḥ || 3 || yaḥ | ukthā | kevalā | dudhe | yaḥ | somam | dhṛṣitā | apibat | yasmai | viṣṇuḥ | trīṇi | padā | vi'cakre | upa | mitrasya | dharmabhiḥ || 3 ||
yasya tvamindra stomeṣu cākano vājeṃ vājiñchatakrato | taṃ tvā vayaṃ sududhāmiva goduhau juhumasi śravasyavaḥ || 4 || yasya | tvam | indra | stomeṣu | cākanaḥ | vājeṃ | vājin | śatakrato iti śata'krato | tam | tvā | vayam | sududhām'iva | go'duhaḥ | juhumasi | śravasyavaḥ || 4 ||
yo nau dātā sa naḥ pitā mahā~ ugra īśānakṛt |
ayāmabhyugro maghavā puruvasurgorśvasye pra dātu naḥ ||

( 2 ) he indra suvāne somābhiṣavaṃ kurvāṇe pṛṣre etannāmake ṛṣau vartamānaṃ somaṃ pītvā yathā amandathāḥ hṛṣṭavānasi |
yathā ca medhye mātariśvani darśiśre darśoṇye syūmaraśmau ṛjūnasi ca eteṣu sarveṣvṛṣiṣu vartamānaṃ somaṃ pītvā amandathāḥ |

( 4 ) he vājin annavan he śatakrato bahukarmavannindra tvaṃ yasya yajamānasya stomeṣu stotreṣu nimitteṣu vāje havirlakṣaṇe anne ca nimittabhūte sati cākanaḥ |
tṛpyasi taṃ tādṛśaṃ tvā tvāmindra śravasyavaḥ |

yasmai tvaṃ vaṃso dānāya maṃhese sa rāyaspoṣaminvati | vasuyavo vasupatiṃ śatakratuṃ stomairindraṃ havāmahe || 6 || yasmai | tvam | vaso iti | dānāya | maṃhese | saḥ | rāyaḥ | poṣam | invati | vasu'yavaḥ | vasu'patim | śata'kratum | stomaiḥ | indram | havāmahe || 6 ||
kudā cana pra yucchasyume ni pāsi janmanī | turīyāditya harvataṃ ta indriyamā tasthāvamṛtaṃ divi || 7 || kudā | cana | pra | yucchasi | ume iti | ni | pāsi | janmanī iti | turīya | āditya | harvatam | te | indriyam | ā | tasthau | amṛtam | divi || 7 ||
yasmai tvaṃ maghavannindra girvaṇaḥ śikṣo śikṣasi dāśuṣe |
asmā
yasmai | tvam | magha'van | indra | | girvaṇaḥ | | śikṣo iti | | śikṣasi | | dāśuṣe | | asmākam | giraḥ | | uta | | su'stutim | | vaso iti | | kṛṇva'vata | | śṛṇudhi | | havam || 8 ||
astāvi manma pūrvyaṃ brahvendrāya vocata | pūrvīṛtasya bṛhatīranuṣata stoturmedhā asṛkṣata || 9 || astāvi | manma | | pūrvyam | | brahma | | indrāya | | vocata | | pūrvīḥ | | ṛtasya | | bṛhatīḥ | | anuṣata | | stotuḥ | | medhāḥ | | asṛkṣata || 9 ||
samindro rāyo bṛhatīradhanuta saṃ kṣoṇī samu sūryam | saṃ śukrāsaḥ śurcayaḥ saṃ gavāśiraḥ somā indramamandiṣuḥ || 10 || sam | indraḥ | | rāyaḥ | | bṛhatīḥ | | adhunuta | | sam | kṣoṇī iti | | sam | ū~ iti | | sūryam | | sam | śukrāsaḥ | | śurcayaḥ | | sam | go'āśiraḥ | | somāḥ | | indram | | amandiṣuḥ || 10 || || 21 ||
atha pañcamaṃ sūktam ||
upamaṃ tvā maghonāṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ ca vṛṣabhāṇām |
pūrbhittaṃ maghavannindra govidamīśānaṃ rāya īmahe ||
1 ||
śṛṇu |
utāpi ca he vaso vāsayitarindra suṣṭutiṃ śobhanāṃ stutiṃ śṛṇudhi |
kiṃca kaṇbavat yathā kaṇbasya havamāhānamaśṛṇoḥ evamasmākaṃ havaṃ śṛṇu ||
(9) he ṛtvijaḥ manma mananīyaṃ pūrvyaṃ purātanaṃ yat brahma stotram asāvi(astāvi) stutiviṣayī- kṛtaṃ bhavadbhistat brahma stotram indrāya parameśvarāya tadarthe vocata brūta |
yato bhavantaḥ ṛtasya satyasyendrasya sambandhinaḥ pūrvīḥ vahīḥ bṛhatīḥ mahatīḥ |
sāmarthyāt mūrtīḥ indrasya pracurān mahato guṇāvatārā- nityarthaḥ |
anuṣata stutiviṣayīkṛtavantaḥ |
kiṃca stoturmama āyoḥ medhāḥ dhāraṇāvatīḥ buddhīḥ asṛkṣata sṛṣṭavantaḥ |
ataḥ prārthaye brahvendrāya vocateti ||
(10) ya indraḥ bṛhatīḥ mahānti rāyaḥ hiraṇyādidhanāni samadhunuta samyakarot |
yaścendraḥ kṣoṇī dyāvāpṛthivyau samadhunuta samyak racyāṃcakre |
kṣoṇī iti dyāvāpṛthivyonamidheyam |
'rodasī kṣoṇī andhasī ' iti tannāmasu pāṭhāt |
kiṃca ya indraḥ sūrye maṅgalātmānaṃ samadhunuta samajanayat |
tamindraṃ śukrāsaḥ śukrāḥ dīptāḥ śurcayaḥ pūtāḥ āpaḥ vasatīvaryekadhanālakṣaṇāḥ samamandiṣuḥ samyagmā- dayan |
kiṃca gavāśiraḥ godugdhaṃ somāḥ tadrasāśca tamindraṃ samamandiṣuḥ |
itthaṃ mahānubhāva indro'sīti tvadarthe brahma vocata iti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ ||
21 ||
'upamaṃ tvā ' ityṛrce pañcamaṃ sūktaṃ kāṇvasya medhyasyārṣam |
anukrāntaṃ ca—'upamaṃ tvādyai medhyaḥ ' iti |
ayujāṃ bṛhatī chandaḥ |
yujāṃ satobṛhatī chandaḥ |
viniyogaḥ sūtrādavagantavyaḥ ||
(1) he maghavan dhanavannindra maghonāṃ dhanavatāṃ madhye upamaṃ upamābhūtaṃ vṛṣabhāṇāṃ kāmavarṣitṛṇāṃ madhye jyeṣṭhamatiśayena praśastaṃ ca tvā tvāṃ rāyaḥ gavāśvādidhanāni īmahe yācāmahe vayam |
kīdṛśaṃ tvām |
upa'mam | tvā | maghonām | jyeṣṭham | ca | vṛṣabhāṇām | purbhit'tamam | magha'van | indra | go'vidam | īśānam | rāyaḥ | īmahe || 1 || ya āyuṃ kutsamatiyigvamardayo vāvṛdhāno divedive | taṃ tvā vayaṃ haryaśvaṃ śatakratuṃ vājayantau havāmahe || 2 || yaḥ | āyum | kutsam | atithi'gvam | ardayaḥ | vavṛdhānaḥ | dive'dive | tam | tvā | vayam | hari'aśvam | śata'kratum | vāja'yantaḥ | havāmahe || 2 || ā no viśveṣāṃ rasaṃ madhvaḥ siścantvadrayaḥ | ye parāvati sunvire janeṣvā ye arvāvatīndavaḥ || 3 || ā | naḥ | viśveṣām | rasam | madhvaḥ | siścantu | adrayaḥ | ye | parā'vati | sunvire | janeṣu | ā | ye | arvā'vati | indavaḥ || 3 || viśvā dveṣāṃsi jahi cāva cā kṛdhi viśve sanvantvā vasu | śīrṣepu citte madirāsau aṃśavo yatrā somasya tumpasi || 4 || viśvā | dveṣāṃsi | jahi | ca | ava | ca | kṛdhi | viśve | sanvantu | ā | vasu | śīrṣepu | cit | te | madirāsaḥ | aṃśavaḥ | yatra | somasya | tumpasi || 4 || || 22 ||
purbhittamam atiśayena śatrupurāṃ bhettāramata eva govidaṃ gavāṃ lambhayitāram īśānaṃ sarvavidyānāṃ niyantā- ram |
' īśānaḥ sarvavidyānām ' (tai. ā. 10.47) iti śruteḥ ||
( 2 ) he indra yastvaṃ divedive pratidinaṃ vāvṛdhānaḥ stutyaḥ atiśayena vardhamānaḥ san āyum etannāmānamṛṣim uttamam etatsaṃjamṛṣim atithigvam etatsaṃjamṛṣiṃ ca ardayaḥ varapradāne (1°pradānena) pīḍa- yasi |
te ātmānam alaṃ manyamānāḥ vārayanti |
tvaṃ tu haṭhāt abhīṣṭavaraṃ yācate (yācata ) ityuktvā tān kleśayasītyarthaḥ |
taṃ tādṛśaṃ haryaśvaṃ harisaṃsthau aśvau yasya tādṛśaṃ śatakratuṃ bahukarmāṇaṃ tvā tvām vāja- vantaḥ (1°vantaḥ) annakāmā vayaṃ yajamānāḥ havāmahe āhvayāmaḥ ||
( 3 ) viśveṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ no'smākaṃ saṃbandhinaḥ adrayaḥ |
tāsthyāttācchabdayam |
adriprabhavāste somāḥ madhvaḥ madhuram |
karmāṇi ṣaṣṭhī |
rasamātmīyaṃ dravamāsiñcantu kātsnryena kṣarantu |
indrārthamiti śeṣaḥ |
te ke |
ye indavaḥ somāḥ parāvati dūradeśe vartamānāḥ |
janeṣu yajamānalakṣaṇeṣu nimitteṣu ā sunvire ābhimukhyena abhiṣutā ṛtvigbhiḥ |
ye ca indavaḥ arvāvati samīpadeśe vartamānāḥ sunvire abhiṣutā- ste sarve'pi militvā indrārthe svarasaṃ siñcantvityarthaḥ ||
( 4 ) he indra viśvā viśvāni sarvāṇi dveṣāṃsi asmākamamitrāṇi jahi nāśaya |
kiṃcāsmān ava rakṣa |
ā ca kṛdhi ā samantāt praśastān kuru ca |
kiṃca viśve sarve janāḥ vasu dhanam ā sanvantu ābhimukhyena dadatu |
asammyamiti śeṣaḥ |
atha ca yatra yeṣvasmāsu sthitasya somasya tumpasi pānena tṛpto bhavasi teṣu śīrṣepu viśiṣṭeṣvasmāsu vartamānāḥ aṃśavaḥ somāḥ te cit tavaiva , madirāsaḥ mādakāḥ santu |
netarasya kasyacit ||
22 ||
indra nedīya edihi mitamedhābhirūtibhiḥ | ā śaṃtam śaṃtamābhirūtibhiḥ svāpe svāpibhiḥ || 5 || indra | nedīyaḥ | ā | it | ihi | mita'medhābhiḥ | ūtibhiḥ | ā | śam'tama | śam'tamābhiḥ | abhiṣṭi'bhiḥ | ā | su'āpe | svāpibhiḥ || 5 || ājituraṃ satpatiṃ viśvacarṣaṇi kṛdhi prajāsvābhagam | pra sū tirā śacībhirye ta ukthinaḥ ṛtaṃ punata ānuṣak || 6 || āji'turam | sat'patim | viśva'carṣaṇim | kṛdhi | pra'jāsu | ā'bhagam | pra | su | tira | śacībhiḥ | ye | te | ukthinaḥ | kartum | punate | ānuṣak || 6 || yaste sādhiṣṭho'vase te syāma bhareṣu te | vayaṃ hotrābhirūta devahūtibhiḥ sasavāṃso manāmahe || 7 || yaḥ | te | sādhiṣṭhaḥ | avase | te | syāma | bhareṣu | te | vayam | hotrābhiḥ | uta
aham | hi | te | hari'vaḥ | brahma | vāja'yuḥ | ājim | yāmi | sadā | ūti'bhiḥ | tvām | it | eva | tam | ame | sam | aṣṭa'yuḥ | gavyuḥ | agne | mathīnām ||8||23||
atha ṣaṣṭhaṃ sūktam ||
etatta indra vīryaṃ gīrbhirgṛṇanti kāravaḥ |
te stobhanta ūrjamāvanghṛtaśrutaṃ paurāso nakṣandhītibhiḥ ||
1 ||
etat | te | indra | vīryam | gīḥ'bhiḥ | gṛṇanti | kāravaḥ | te | stobhantaḥ | ūrjam | āvan | ghṛta'śrutam | paurāsaḥ | nakṣan | dhīti'bhiḥ || 1 ||
nakṣanta indramavase sukṛtyayā yeṣāṃ suteṣu mandase |
yathā saṃvarte amado yathā kṛśa evāsme indra matsva ||
2 ||
nakṣante | indram | avase | su'kṛtyayā | yeṣām | suteṣu | mandase | yathā | sam'varte | amadaḥ | yathā | kṛśe | eva | asme iti | indra | matsva || 2 ||
ā no viśve sajoṣaso devāso gantunorpa naḥ |
vasavo rudrā avase na ā gamacchṛṇvantu maruto havam ||
3 ||
prasiddham | tvāmeva sam ame saṃgato bhavāmi | saṃgrāme tvāmevāśrayāmi | netaraṃ kaṃcanetyarthaḥ | evetyanarthako nipātaḥ || || 23 ||
‘etatte’ iti aṣṭarce ṣaṣṭhaṃ sūktaṃ kāṇvasya mātāriśvanasyārṣamaindram ( mātāriśvana ārṣamaindram ) |
anukramyate ca- ‘etatte mātāriśvā no viśva iti vaiśvadevaḥ pragāthaḥ ’ iti |
tṛtīyācaturthyau viśve- devadevatāke |
anyāsāṃ ṣaṇṇāmindro devatā |
ayujāṃ bṛhatī chandaḥ yujāṃ satobṛhatī chandaḥ |
viniyogaḥ sūtrādavagantavyaḥ ||
( 1 ) he indra ye kāravaḥ stotāraḥ te tava saṃbandhi etat dṛśyamānaṃ pṛṣṭhādilakṣaṇasāmarthyaṃ gīrbhiḥ stutirūpābhirvāgbhiḥ gṛṇanti stuvanti te stobhantaḥ stotāraḥ ūrjam annam āvan prāpnuvanti |
kiṃca te paurāsaḥ pure bhavāḥ stotāraḥ dhītibhiraṅgulībhiḥ ghṛtaśrutaṃ ghṛtasrāviṇaṃ gavādikaṇaṃ nakṣan vyāpnuvanti |
tvadanugrahāt gavādipaśūn aṅgulībhiḥ sarvadā duhantītyarthaḥ ||
( 2 ) he indra yeṣāṃ yajamānānāṃ saṃbandheṣu suteṣvabhiṣuteṣu someṣu nibhāteṣu ( ! nipīteṣu ) tvaṃ mandase hṛṣyasi te yajamānāḥ avase annāya rakṣaṇāya vā sukṛtyayā śobhanena somābhivakarmaṇā indraṃ parameśvaraṃ tvāṃ nakṣante vyāpnuvanti |
nakṣatirvyāptikarmā ‘invati nakṣati’ iti vyāptikarmasu pāṭhāt |
annāyarthe tvāmāśrayanta ityarthaḥ |
ato he indra yathā yena prakāreṇa saṃvarte etannāmake ṛṣau yajamāne sati amadaḥ hṛṣṭavānasi yathā ca kṛśe etannāmni yajamāne amadaḥ eva evamanena prakāreṇa asme asmāsu kāṇveṣu matsva hṛṣṭo bhava somapānādineti śeṣaḥ ||
( 3 ) sajoṣasaḥ saha juṣamāṇāḥ viśve devāsaḥ sarve devāḥ no'smākaṃ yajñamuddiśyeti śeṣaḥ |
ā | naḥ | viśvai | su'jorṣasaḥ | devāsaḥ | gantaṃna | upa | naḥ | vasavaḥ | rudrāḥ | arbase | naḥ | ā | gaman | śṛṇvantu | marutaḥ | havam || 3 || pūṣā viṣṇurhavanaṃ me sarasvatyavantu sapta sindhavaḥ | āpo vātaḥ parvatāso vanaspatiḥ śṛṇotu pṛthivī havam || 4 || puṣā | viṣṇuḥ | havanam | me | sarasvatī | avantu | sapta | sindhavaḥ | āpaḥ | vātaḥ | parvatāsaḥ | vanaspatiḥ | śṛṇotu | pṛthivī | havam || 4 || || 24 || yadindra rādho asti te māghonaṃ maghavattama | tena no bodhi sadhamādhau vṛdhe bhago dānārya vṛtrahan || 5 || yat | indra | rādhaḥ | asti | te | māghonam | maghavattama | tena | naḥ | bodhi | sadha'mādhaḥ | vṛdhe | bhagaḥ | dānārya | vṛtra'han || 5 || ājipate nṛpate tvamiddhi no vāja ā vakṣi sukrato | vītī hotrābhiruta devavītibhiḥ sasvāṃso vi śṛṇvire || 6 ||
no'smākamupa samīpe ā ganṭana āgacchantu |
kiñca vasavo'thau rudrā ekādaśa ca no'smākamavase rakṣaṇāya ā gaman āgacchantu |
marutaḥ ekonapañcāśatsaṃkhyākā devāstu havamasmatkṛtaṃ stotraṃ śṛṇvantu |
(4) pūṣā poṣakaḥ devaḥ viṣṇurvyāpako devaḥ sarasvatī |
sara ityudakanāma |
tadvatī devī ca ete sarve me mama mātariśvanasya ( mātariśvanaḥ ) ṛṣeḥ havanamāhānam |
stotramiti yāvat |
avantu śṛṇvantu |
avatiratra śravaṇakarmā |
'ava rakṣaṇagati' ityasmin sūtre (1) tasya śravaṇābhidhanāt |
kiṃca sapta saṃkhyākāḥ sindhavo gaṅgādyā nadya āpaḥ jalābhimāninyo devatāḥ vāto vāyuḥ parvatāsaḥ parvatāṃ mervādīnāmabhimānino devāśca me havanam avantu śṛṇvantu |
atha ca vanaspatiḥ vanānāṃ pātāraḥ pālako ( pālakā ) devāḥ pṛthivī ca devī me havaṃ stotraṃ śṛṇotu |
24 |
(5) he maghavattama atiśayena dhanavannindra te tava svabhūtaṃ māghonaṃ maghavatsvāmikaṃ yat prasiddhaṃ rādhaḥ dhanamasti bhavati tena rādhasā dhanena he vṛtrahan vṛtrahannindra tvaṃ dānārtha vṛṣe vardhanārtha ca no'smān pātrabhūtān bodhi budhyasva |
taddanadānena asmān samṛddhān kartuṃ jānīhītyarthaḥ |
kīdṛśastvam |
sadhamādhaḥ sahamādanayogyaḥ bhago bhajanīyaśca |
(6) he ājipate saṃgrāmapālaka he netṛpate ( nṛpate ) netṛṇāṃ sainikānāṃ pālaka he sukrato śobhanakarmannindra tvamit tvameva no'smān vāje saṃgrāme āvakṣi āvahasi prāpayasi |
tvatsāhāyyena vayaṃ dhanārtha saṃgrāmaṃ gantumutsahāmaha ityarthaḥ |
kīdṛśastvam |
hotrābhiḥ stutilakṣaṇābhiḥ vāgbhiḥ saha |
vītī aśanavān stutibhiḥ protsahamāno havirityarthaḥ |
utāpi ca sasvāṃsaḥ havirlakṣaṇānnavanto yajamānāḥ
ārji'pate | nu'pate | tvam | it | hi | naḥ | vājai | ā | vakṣi | sukrato iti su'krato | vītī | hotrābhiḥ | uta | devavīti'bhiḥ | susa'dvāṃsaḥ | vi | śruṇire || 6 || santi ha॒rya āśiṣa indra āyurjanānām | asmārnakṣasva maghavanbhupāvase dhṛṣvasva pipyuṣīmiṣam || 7 || santi | hi | arye | ā'śiṣaḥ | indrai | āyuḥ | janānām | asmān | nakṣasva | magha'van | upa | arbase | dhṛṣvasva | pipyuṣīm | iṣam || 7 || vūrya te indra stomairvidhema tvamassākaṃ śatakrato | mahiṃ sthūraṃ śaśayaṃ rādho ahryaṃ praskaṇvāya ni tauśaya || 8 || vayam | te | indra | stomaibhiḥ | vidhema | tvam | asmākam | śatakrato iti śata'krato | mahiṃ | sthūram | śaśayam | rādhaḥ | ahryam | praskaṇvāya | ni | tauśaya || 8 || ||25|| || atha saptamaṃ sūktam || bhūrīdindrasya vīryaṃ vyakhyanmubhyāryati | rādhaste dasyave vṛka || 1 || devavītibhiḥ devakartṛkāśanaiḥ vi śruṇire viśeṣeṇa śrūyante | yadā hi tvādśā devāsteṣāṃ havirśvanuvanti tadā te vikhyātā bhavantītyarthaḥ || (7) he parameśvara hi yasmāt janānāṃ jāyamānānāṃ yajamānānāmasmākaṃ svabhūtamāyuḥ carupuroḍā- śādyannam āśiṣaḥ gavāśvādyaiśvaryāṇi ca arye jagatasvāmini indre parameśvare tvayi santi samarpitāni bhavanti | ato he maghavan dhanavannindra asmān yajamānān upa nakṣasva samīpe vyāpnuhi | ātmīyatvena svasamīpe kurvityarthaḥ | kiṃcāsmākamavase rakṣaṇāya pipyuṣīm atiśayena pālayitrīma iṣamannaṃ dhṛṣvasva prapūrya dehītyarthaḥ || (8) he indra vayaṃ yajamānāḥ te tvadīyāḥ smaḥ | tvaṃ cāsmākamasi | 'tvasmākaṃ tavasmasi' (ṛ. saṃ. 8. 92. 32) iti śruteḥ | ataḥ he śatakrato bahukarmavannindra stomebhiḥ stotraiḥ vidhema paricarema tvām | yasmāccadīyā vayaṃ tasmāttvaṃ praskaṇvāya kaṇvaputrāya mahyaṃ mātariśvanāya (? mātariśvane ) rādho dhanaṃ ni tośaya niyamena dehi | tadeva rādho viśinaṣṭi | mahi mahat pūjyaṃ sthūraṃ sthūlam | prauḍhamityarthaḥ | śaśayaṃ devabrāhmaṇādiśarīre śayānaṃ tairupabhogyamityarthaḥ | ahryaṃ lajjārahitam | pāpavartmanā labdhaṃ dhanaṃ hi lajjāsahitaṃ bhavati | idaṃ tu na tathā | īśvarānugrahalabdhatvāsyetyarthaḥ || ||25|| 'bhūrīt' iti pañcarcaṃ saptamaṃ sūktaṃ kāṇvasya kuśasyārṣaṃ praskaṇvadānadevatākaṃ gāyatram | anukāntaṃ ca—'bhūrīt pañca kuśaḥ praskaṇvasya dānastutirgāyatraṃ tu' tṛtīyāntye anuṣṭubhau' | iti | antyātṛtīyeyoranuṣṭup chandaḥ | anyāsāṃ gāyatrī chandaḥ | viniyogaḥ sūtrādavagantavyaḥ || (1) indrasya samarthasya praskaṇvasya vīryaṃ dānalakṣaṇasāmarthyaṃ bhūrīt pracurameva sat abhi abhitaḥ ā ayati ābhimukhyena prāpnotīti vyalyaṃ vyāśasipamaham | īśvareṇālpamapi kṛtaṃ karma bahu bhavat 1. anu.-'tṛtīyāpañcamyāvanuṣṭubhau' |
bhūri̭ | it | indra̭sya | vī॒rya̭m | vi | a॒hya̭m | a॒bhi | a॒yati̭ | rādha̭: | te | da॒syavḙ | vṛ॒ka॒ || 1 ||
śa॒taṃ śve॒tāsa̭ u॒kṣaṇo̭ di॒vi tā॒ro na raṷcante | ma॒hā di॒vaṃ na ta̭stabhuḥ ||2|| śa॒tam | śve॒tāsa̭: | u॒kṣaṇa̭: | di॒vi | tā॒ra̭: | na | ro॒ca॒nte | ma॒hā | di॒vam | na | ta॒sta॒bhuḥ ||2||
śa॒taṃ ve॒ṇūccha̭taṃ śu॒naḥ śa॒taṃ ca॒rmāṇi̭ mlā॒tāni̭ | śa॒taṃ mai ba॒lbaja̭stukā a॒ru॒ṣīṇā̭ṃ ca॒tuḥśa॒tam || 3 || śa॒tam | ve॒ṇūn | śa॒tam | śu॒naḥ | śa॒tam | ca॒rmāṇi̭ | mlā॒tāni̭ | śa॒tam | me | ba॒lbaja̭stukā: | a॒ru॒ṣīṇām | ca॒tuḥśa॒tam || 3 ||
su॒de॒vā: stha̭ kā॒ṇavā̭yanā॒ vaya̭ovayo vi॒cara̭ntaḥ | a॒śvāso̭ na ca॒ṅkrama̭ta ||4|| su॒de॒vā: | stha̭ | kā॒ṇavā̭yanā: | vaya̭: | traya̭: | vi॒'ca॒ra̭ntaḥ | a॒śvāsa̭: | na | ca॒ṅkrama̭ta || 4 ||
ādi॒tsāma̭sya ca॒rkirbhā॒nūnsya̭ ma॒hi śrava̭: |
i॒yāvī̭rati॒ṣvasa̭na॒pa॒thaśca̭rṣā॒ cana̭ saṃna॒śe ||
5 ||
prasiddhayatītyarthaḥ |
idānīmṛṣiḥ pratyakṣīkṛtyāha |
he vṛka śatruvikartana praskaṇva te tava saṃvandhi rādho dhanaṃ dasyave dasyuṃ hantuṃ samartha bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
( 2 ) he praskaṇva tvayā dattāḥ śatasaṃkhyākāḥ śvetāsaḥ śvetāḥ śuklavarṇāḥ ukṣaṇaḥ ukṣṇaḥ reta:- secanasamarthā balīvardā rocante dīpyante |
ka iva |
divi dyuloke tāro na tārā iva |
tā yathā divi rocante tadvadete bhuvītyarthaḥ |
kiṃca mahā tejaso mahattve ( mahattvena ) divaṃ na tārā yathā dyulokaṃ tastabhuḥ vyāpnuvanti tadvadete mallā yaśaso mahattvena bhuvaṃ vyāpnuvanti |
( 3 ) praskaṇvaḥ śataparimaptāna veṇūn maskarān |
prādāditi sarvatra śeṣaḥ |
śataṃ śatasaṃkhyā- kān śunaḥ sārameyān prādāt |
tathā śataṃ bahūni mlātāni mṛdukṛtāni carmāṇi ajināni prādāt |
kiṃca śataparimatāḥ balbajastukāḥ tṛṇapūlāḥ me mahyaṃ kṛśanātre ṛṣaye prādāt |
atha ca arupīṇā- mārocamānānāṃ gavāṃ catuḥśataṃ prādāt |
( 4 ) he kāṇavāyanāḥ kaṇagotrāḥ praskaṇvāḥ vayobayaḥ atiśayena pakṣiṇa iva |
luptopameyam |
vicarantaḥ vihāyasi carantaḥ yūyaṃ sudevāḥ stha kalyāṇadevāḥ bhavatha |
ato'smadāśiṣā aśvā iva caṅkramata viharata yūyam |
( 5 ) ādid dānānantarameva anūnasya anyūnasya |
sarvotkṛṣṭasyeti yāvat |
sāmasya evannāmnaḥ kaṇaputrasya saṃvandhi mahi mahat śravaḥ yaśaḥ na carkiran na kartumicchanti |
tadyaśaḥ saṃpādayituṃ kecit neśate ityarthaḥ |
kiṃca pathaḥ dharmamārgāt sakāśāt atidhvasan atiśayenādhaḥ patanaṃ kṛpaṇaḥ pumān syāvīḥ
āta | it | sāsatya | cakriran | na | anūnasya | mahi | śravaḥ | śyāvī | ati'dhvasan | pathaḥ | cakṣuṣā | cana | sum'naśai || 5 || || 26 || || athāṣṭamaṃ sūktam || prati te dasyave vṛka rādho adṛśyahyam | ghaurna prathinā śavaḥ || 1 || prati | te | dasyave | vṛka | rādhaḥ | adṛśi | ahyam | dhauḥ | na | prathinā | śavaḥ || 1 || daśa mahaṃ pautakṛtaḥ sahasrā dasyave vṛkaḥ | nityādvāyo amaṃhata || 2 || daśa | mahyam | pauta'kṛtaḥ | sahasrā | dasyave | vṛkaḥ | nityāt | rāyaḥ | amaṃhata || 2 || śataṃ me gardabhānāṃ śatamūrṇāvatīnām | śataṃ dāsā~ ati sarjaḥ || 3 || śatam | me | gardabhānām | śatam | ūrṇā'vatīnām | śatam | dāsān | ati | sarjaḥ ||3|| kapilā gāḥ cakṣuṣā cana netreṇāpi saṃśahe samyak vyāptuṃ saṃdraṣṭuṃ neṣṭe | kimuta vaktavyaṃ tadyaśaḥsaṃpādane neṣṭe iti || || 26 || ‘prati te ’ iti pañcarcamaṣṭamaṃ sūktaṃ kāṇvasya pṛṣadhrasyārṣaṃ gāyatram | anukrāntaṃ ca — prati te pṛṣadhro'ntyā ’ agnisūryadevatyā paṅkticchandaskā | śeṣā indradevatyā gāyatrīcchandaskāḥ | viniyogaḥ sūtrā- davagantavyaḥ || ( 1 ) he vṛka śatruvikartanendra te tava svabhūtaṃ rādho dhanaṃ dasyave upakṣayitre śatrave prati pratikūlam adarśī dṛṣṭam | śatrunāpahṛtaṃ san ( śatrunāpahṛtaṃ sat ) na tasya upakārāya bhavati pratyutāpakārāyaiva jāyata ityarthaḥ | kīdṛśaṃ rādhaḥ | ahyaṃ lajjārahitam | śatruhanane pragalbhamiti yāvat | kiṃca te śavaḥ balaṃ prathinā pṛthutvena ghaurna dyuloka ivāsti | ataḥ sarvotkṛṣṭaṃ tvāmahaṃ staumītyabhiprāyaḥ || ( 2 ) he pautakṛtaḥ pavitrakarmavindra mahaṃ madarthe daśa sahasrā ayutasaṃkhyākān dasyūna2 upakṣayi- tṛn śatrūn vṛkaḥ | vivṛttavān chinnavānasi | ata eva nityāt śāśvatāt tvattaḥ bhītāḥ santaste śatravaḥ rāyaḥ dhanāni amaṃhata aduḥ mahyam | maṃhatidānakarmā tatra tasya pāṭhāt || ( 3 ) kiṃca he indra tvatto bhītāste me mahyamṛṣe pṛṣadhrānne ( pṛṣadhra ) gardabhānāṃ rāsabhānāṃ śatam | amaṃhata aduḥ iti pūrvamantragatenānvayaḥ | sarvatra | atha ca ūrṇāvatīnāṃ meṣīnāṃ śatamduḥ | tathā śatāna śatasaṃkhyākān dāsān bhṛtyān ati atiśayitāḥ srajo mālāśca aduḥ | evaṃprabhāvastvāṃ ( 0prabhāvaṃ tvāṃ ) staumītyarthaḥ | yadvā ubhayormantrayorayamarthaḥ | pautakṛtaḥ | pūte pavitre sthāne kṛtaḥ pāda- vikṣepo yasyāḥ sā pūtakṛtā aditiḥ | pavitradeśe pādanyāsaḥ aditirucita eva | ‘dṛṣṭapūtaṃ nyaset pādam ’ iti vacanāt | tasyā apatyaṃ pautakṛta indraḥ | dasyave | dasyuḥ | karmopakṣayitā tamaḥsaṃghaḥ | taṃ hantuṃ vṛkaścandramāḥ sana ( san ) nityāt anupakṣīṇāt svakośāt sakāśāt daśa sahasrā ayutasaṃkhyākāni rāyaḥ dhanāni mahyaṃ pṛṣadhrānne amaṃhata prādāt | kiṃca gardabhameṣīdāsamālāśatānyapi me mahyaṃ sa eva prādāt iti | ‘vṛkaścandramā bhavati ’ ( niru. 5. 20 ) iti yāskaḥ || 1. ‘pṛṣadhro'ntyāmisaurī paṅktiḥ iti | antyā ’ iti bhāvyam | 2. ‘dasyave’ iti mantrapāṭhaḥ |
tatro api prāṇīyata pūtakratāyai vyaktā | aśvānamiṣa yūthyām || 4 || tatro iti | api | pra | anīyata | puta'kratāyai | vi'aktā | aśvānam | ita | na | yuthyām ||4||
acaityamiśriṃkitahyyavād sa sumadrathaḥ | agniḥ śukeṇa śociṣā bṛhatsūrau arocata divi sūryau arocata || 5 || acaiti | agniḥ | cikituḥ | havya'vāṭ | saḥ | sumata'rathaḥ | agniḥ | śukeṇa | śociṣā | bṛhat | sūrau | arocata | divi | sūryau | arocata ||5|| ||27||
atha navamaṃ sūktam ||
yuvaṃ devā ṛtunā puṇyeṇa yuktā rathena taviṣaṃ yajatrā | ārgacchataṃ nāsatyā śacībhiridaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sarvaṃ pibāthaḥ || 1 || yuvam | devā | ṛtunā | puṇyeṇa | yuktāḥ | rathena | taviṣam | yajatrā | ā | agacchatam | nāsatyā | śacībhiḥ | idam | tṛtīyam | sarvam | pibāthaḥ || 1 ||
( 4 ) kiṃca vyaktā vividhaṃ gantā nānādeśeṣu pracalan indraḥ tatra svarge vartamānaiḥ (? vartamānayai ) pūtakratāyai etannāme (? ॰nāmnyai ) samātre'pi ( svamakṣetre'pi ) prānīyata prāpitavān |
kimityucyate |
aśvānāṃ vaḍavānāṃ yūthyāṃ saṃhatim |
na kevalaṃ mahyameva prādādapi tu svajananyā api vaḍavāvṛndaṃ prāpitavān itīyāninrdaḥ samartha iti bhāvaḥ |
ita na iti nipātadvayamanarthakam ||
( 5 ) athānayābhisūryau stūyate |
sa prasiddho'gniḥ cikituḥ cetanāvata yajamānasya |
karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī |
cetanāvantaṃ yajamānam aceti jānāti |
kīdṛśaḥ saḥ |
havyavāṭ haviṣāṃ voḍhā sumadrathaḥ svayameva raṃhaṇasvabhāvaḥ |
‘ sumata svayamityarthaḥ ’ ( niru. 6. 22 ) iti yāskaḥ |
asahāyagamana- śīla ityarthaḥ |
kiṃca bṛhat mahān yo'gniḥ śukeṇa śukena śociṣā tejasā sūraḥ suṣṭhu īrayitā prerakaḥ san rātrau bhuvi arocata dīpyate |
atha ca divā divi (?) dyotamāne svaloke sūryaḥ suṣṭhu prerayitādityaḥ arocata dīpyate |
ubhābhyāmahoratrātmakaḥ sarvaḥ kāla ākrānta ityabhiprāyaḥ ||

‘ yuvaṃ devā ’ iti catūṛcaṃ navamaṃ sūktaṃ kāṇvasya medhyasyāśvinaṃ traiṣṭubham |
anukramyate- ‘ yuvaṃ devā catuṣkaṃ medhya āśvinaṃ traiṣṭubham ’ iti |
viniyogaḥ sūtravadagantavyaḥ ||
( 1 ) he yajatrā yajanīyau pūjyau devau dyotamānāśvinau puṇyeṇa purā bhavena ṛtunā karmaṇā anugrahabuddhyā vā upetau yuvāṃ yuvāṃ prati rathena raṃhaṇasvabhāvena syandanena yuktāḥ saṃyuktā aśvāḥ taviṣaṃ balaṃ yathā bhavati tathā vahantīti śeṣaḥ |
tasmāt he nāsatyā nāsatyau satyabhūtau yuvāṃ śacībhiḥ anugrahalakṣaṇaiḥ karmabhiḥ saha idaṃ tṛtīyaṃ savanamuddiśya āgacchatamāgacchatam |
āgatya ca pibāthaḥ pibataṃ sobhamiti śeṣaḥ ||
yuvāṃ devānya ekādaśārsa: satyā: satyasya dadṛśe purastāt | asmākaṃ yajñaṃ sarvaṃ juṣāṇā pāta somamaśvinā dīrghamī || 2 || yuvām | devā: | traya: | ekādaśārsa: | satyā: | satyasya | dadṛśe | purastāt | asmākam | yajñam | sarvam | juṣāṇā | pātam | somam | aśvinā | dīrghamī iti dīrdi'abhī ||2||
punāyyaṃ tadaśvinā kṛtaṃ vāṃ vṛṣabho divo rjasa: pṛthivyā: | sahasraṃ śaṃsā uta ye gaviṣṭhau sarvā~ ita upa yātaṃ pibadhyai || 3 || punāyyam | tat | aśvinā | kṛtam | vām | vṛṣabha: | diva: | rjasa: | pṛthivyā: | sahasram | śaṃsā: | uta | ye | go'iṣṭhau | sarvān | ita | tān | upa | yāta | pibadhyai ||3||
ayaṃ vāṃ bhāgo nihito yajatremā girau nāsatyopa yātam | pibataṃ somaṃ madhumantame pra dāśvāṃsamavataṃ śacībhi: || 4 || ayam | vām | bhāga: | ni'hita: | yajatrā | imā: | gira: | nāsatyā | upa | yātam | pibatam | somam | madhu'mantam | asme iti | pra | dāśvāṃsam | avatam | śacībhi: ||4|| ||28||
( 2 ) he aśvināśvinau yuvāṃ dvau apare ca traya ekādaśaśa: trayastriṃśaddevā: |
te ca ‘aṣṭau vasava: ekādaśa rudrā dvādaśādityā: prajāpatiśca vaṣaṭkāraśca’ iti śrutiprasiddhā: |
sarve yūyaṃ satyā: yathārthabhūtā: santa: satyasāvitathasya yajñasya purastāt purodeśe dadṛśe dṛśyamānā bhavata |
ato'smākaṃ saṃvandhinaṃ savanam |
sūyate'bhiṣūyate somo yatreti savana: |
taṃ tādṛśaṃ yajñaṃ yajanīyamadhvaraṃ juṣāṇā juṣamāṇau sevamānau yuvāṃ somam abhiṣutasomarasaṃ pātaṃ pibatama |
kīdṛśau yuvām |
dīyamī |
dīdi: atiśayena dīvya- māna: agni: jaṭharānalo yayostau tādṛśau |
bhipajo: bhavataudīptāmivamapyucitamiti bhāva: |
( 3 ) he aśvināśvinau pṛthivyā: bhūmerheto: diva: dyulokāt sakāśāt rajasa: udakasya vṛṣabha: barṣitā sūryo'stīti yat tatsarvaṃ vāṃ yuvayo: kṛtaṃ karma punāyyaṃ stutyaṃ bhavati |
bhavadanugrahādeva yuṣmatpitā sūrya: pṛthivyāṃ varṣati |
no cet tena tu svaraśmināḍībhi: bhūmerjalamākṛṣṭamasti |
sa kathaṃ varṣadākarṣaka: san |
tasmāt bhavatorevaitatkarmetyabhiprāya: |
yata evamata: gaviṣṭhau gavāmeṣaṇe nimitte sati te 1 prasiddhā: sahasrasaṃkhyākā: śaṃsā: |
śasyante stūyanta iti śaṃsā yajñā: |
ārabdhā: santīti śeṣa: |
tān sarvānt sarvānapi yajñān yuvāmupayātaṃ samīpe gacchatam |
vacanavyatyayaśchāndasa: |
kimartham |
pibadhyai somaṃ pātum |
uta ityanarthaka: |
( 4 ) he yajatrā yajanīyāvaśvinau ayaṃ purovartī vāṃ yuvayo: bhāga: bhajanīyo'ṃśa: somalakṣaṇa: nihita: niyamena sthāpita: |
atastaṃ gṛhītumāgacchatamiti (grahītu°) śeṣa: |
kiṃca he nāsatyā nāsatyau satyabhūtāśvinau yuvāmimā: saṃnikṛṣṭā: gira: stutilakṣaṇā vāca: upayātaṃ samīpe gacchataṃ śrotum |
atha ca madhumantaṃ mādhuryopetaṃ somaṃ pibatam |
kiṃca asme asmākaṃ saṃbandhinaṃ dāśvāṃsaṃ havirdattavantaṃ yajamānaṃ śacībhi: abhimataphalapradānalakṣaṇai: karmabhi: pra avataṃ prakarṣeṇa rakṣatam |

1. ‘ye’ iti mantrapāṭha: |
kṛ. 3-51
atha daśamaṃ sūktam ||
yamutvijo bahudhā kalpayantaḥ sacetaso yajñamimaṃ vahanti | yo anucāno brāhmaṇo yukta āsītkā svittatra yajamānasya saṃvit || 1 || yam | ṛtvijaḥ | bahudhā | kalpayantaḥ | sa'cetasaḥ | yajñam | imam | vahanti | yaḥ | anucānaḥ | brāhmaṇaḥ | yuktaḥ | āsīt | kā | svit | tatra | yajamānasya | sa'vit || 1 ||
ekaṃ evāgnirbahudhā samiddha ekaḥ sūryo viśvamanu prabhūtaḥ | ekaivoṣāḥ sarvvamidaṃ vi bhātyekaṃ vā idaṃ vi babhūva sarvvam || 2 || ekaḥ | eva | agniḥ | bahudhā | sama'iddhaḥ | ekaḥ | sūryaḥ | viśvam | anu | pra'bhūtaḥ | ekā | eva | uṣāḥ | sarvvam | idam | vi | bhāti | ekam | vai | idam | vi | babhūva | sarvvam || 2 ||
jyotiṣmantaṃ ketumantaṃ tricakraṃ sukhaṃ rathaṃ suṣadaṃ bhūrivāram |
citramaghā yasya yoge'dhijajñe taṃ vāṃ huve ati riktaṃ pibadhyai |
3 |
‘ yamutvijaḥ ’ iti trayātmakaṃ daśamaṃ sūktaṃ kāṇvasya medhyasyārṣaṃ vaiśvadevaṃ traiṣṭubham |
anukrāntaṃ ca- ‘ yamutvijatraṃ ’ vaiśvadevāya ṛtvikstutirvā ’ iti |
ādyā ṛtvigdevatyā vā ityarthaḥ |
viniyogaḥ sūtrādavagantavyaḥ |
( 1 ) sacetasaḥ samānaprajñāḥ ṛtvijaḥ yamimaṃ yajñaṃ yajanīyakarmaviśeṣaṃ bahudhā anekaprakāreṇa kalpayantaḥ racayantaḥ santaḥ vahanti devān prāpayanti |
tamimaṃ yajñaṃ kurvan brāhmaṇaḥ ṛtvigalakṣaṇo vipraḥ yajñakarmaviśeṣe yuktaḥ samāhitaḥ āsīt |
tatra karmaṇi kā svit |
sviditi vitarke |
kā vā yajamānasya saṃvit prajñā |
na kiñcitkarā ityarthaḥ |
yatra karmaṇi ṛtvigeva sāvadhāno'sti tatra yajamānasya prajñāpāṭavena kiṃ prayojanamasti |
na kiñcidapīti bhāvaḥ |
kīdṛśo brāhmaṇaḥ |
anucānaḥ guroḥ sakāśādadhītavedāṅgaḥ |
( 2 ) ekaḥ kevala evāgniḥ dānādiguṇayukto devaḥ bahudhā vāḍavavaidyutādyanekaprakāreṇa samiddhaḥ samyak dīptāṃ bhavati |
tathā ekaḥ ekākī sūryaḥ prāṇināṃ suṣṭhu karmasverayitā devaḥ viśvaṃ sarvaṃ jagadanu anupraviśya |
‘ tatsṛṣṭvā tadevānuprāviśat ’ ( tai. u. 2. 6) iti śruteḥ |
prabhūtaḥ anekātmatayā prādu- bhūto'sti |
‘ sūrya ātmā jagatastasthuṣaśca ’ ( ṛ. saṃ. 1. 115. 1) iti śruteḥ |
kiṃca ekā ekākitveva uṣāḥ tamo vivāsayantī devī sarvaṃ samastamidaṃ dṛśyamānaṃ jagat vi bhāti viśeṣeṇa dīpayati |
tasmādekaṃ vai ekamevādvitīyaṃ brahma idamātrāhastambaparyantaṃ jagat vi babhūva viśeṣeṇābhavaditi yukta- miti śeṣaḥ |
yadāgnyādayo devā apyekākinaḥ santo'nekabhavanasamarthāstadā paraṃ brahma ekaṃ sadanakaṃ bhave- diti kimu vaktavyamityabhiprāyaḥ |
( 3 ) he viśvedevāḥ vām |
vacanavyatyayaścchandasaḥ |
vo yuṣmākaṃ madhye atiriktaṃ niratiśayaṃ taṃ tacchabdavācyaṃ paramātmānaṃ pitradhyai somaṃ pātuṃ huve āhvayāmi |
taṃ kam |
yasya paramātmanaḥ yoge sevyasevakabhāvasaṃbandhe sati citramaghā |
citraṃ cāyanīyaṃ maghaṃ gavāśādilakṣaṇaṃ dhanaṃ yasyāḥ sā citramaghā |
1. anukramaṇyāmetannopalabhyate | maik.-pṛ. 31 draṣṭavyam || 2. ‘ ati | riktaṃ ’ iti padapāṭhaḥ |
jyotiṣmantam | ketu'mantam | tri'cakram | su'kham | ratham | su'sadam | bhūri'vāram | citra'maghā | yasya | yoge | adhi'jajñe | tam | vām | huve | ati | riktam | pibadhyai ||3||||29||
athaikādaśaṃ sūktam ||
imāni vāṃ bhāgadheyāni sisrata indrāvaruṇā pra mahe suteṣu vām |
yajñeyajñe ha sarvanā bhuraṇyatho yatsunvate yajamānāya śikṣathaḥ ||
1 ||
imāni | vām | bhāga'dheyāni | sisrate | indrāvaruṇā | pra | mahe | suteṣu | vām | yajñe'yajñe | ha | sarvanā | bhuraṇyathaḥ | yat | sunvate | yajamānāya | śikṣathaḥ || 1 ||
niṣidhvarīroṣadhīrāpa āstāmindrāvaruṇā mahimānamāśata |
yā sisratū rajasaḥ pāre adhvano yayoḥ śatrurnakirādaiva ohate ||
2 ||
pūjanīyānekadhanopetā lakṣmīḥ adhi jajñe adhikaṃ prādurabhūt yudhiṣṭhirādestam |
kīdṛśaṃ tam |
jyotiṣmantaṃ jyotiṣī sūryādīni prakāśyatvena santi yasya tam |
‘tasya bhāsā sarvamidaṃ vibhāti’ iti śruteḥ |
ketu- mantam |
ketavaḥ prajñāḥ vipayāḥ santi yasya tam |
‘yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavit’ (mu. u. 1. 1. 9) iti śruteḥ |
keturiti prajñānāma |
‘ketuḥ ketaḥ’ iti tannāmasu pāṭhāt |
tricakraṃ triṣu bhūrādilokeṣu cakraṃ cakramaṇaṃ trivikramāvatāre yasya tam |
‘idaṃ viṣṇurvicakrame’ (ṛ. saṃ. 1. 23. 17) ityādidhruteḥ |
yadvā triṣu lokeṣu apratihataṃ cakraṃ sudarśanākhyaṃ yasya tam |
mukhaṃ niratiśayānandātmakam |
‘oṃ kaṃ brahma kham’ (iti) śruteḥ |
rathaṃ rahaṇasvabhāvam |
gamanaśīlamiti yāvat |
‘apāṇipādo javano grahītā’ (śve. u. 3. 19) iti śruteḥ |
supadam |
śobhane ātmasvarūpe sīdati tiṣṭhatīti supat supratiṣṭhaḥ tam |
‘prajñā pratiṣṭhā prajñānaṃ brahma’ iti śruteḥ |
bhūrivāraṃ bhūribhirbahubhiḥ prāṇibhiḥ vāro varaṇīyastam |
sarvātmakatvāt sarvapriyatvamucitamasya |
‘ātmanaḥ kāmāya sarvaṃ priyaṃ bhavati’ (bṛ. u. 2. 4. 5) iti śruteḥ ||
29 ||
‘imāni vām’ iti saptarcamekādaśaṃ sūktaṃ kāṇvasya suparṇasyāryamaindrāvaruṇaṃ jāgatam |
‘imāni vāṃ sapta suparṇa aindrāvaruṇaṃ jāgatam’ ityanukrāntatvāt |
viniyogaḥ sūtrādavagantavyaḥ ||
(1) he indrāvaruṇā indrāvaruṇau imāni idānīṃ kṛtāni bhāgadheyāni bhavadaṃśabhūtāni caru- puroḍāśādihavīṃṣi vāṃ yuvāṃ prati sisrate prāptumicchanti |
ataḥ suteṣu abhiṣuteṣu someṣu satsu mahe mahate pūjanāya vāṃ yuvāṃ pra prārthaye |
upasargavaśādyogyakriyādhyāhāraḥ |
yat yasmāt yajñeyajñe pratiyaraṃ savanā triṣvapi savaneṣu bhuraṇyatho ha gacchathaḥ khalu |
kiṃca sunvate somābhiṣavaṃ kurvate yajamānāya śikṣathaḥ |
abhimataṃ prayacchathaḥ |
śikṣatirdānakarmā |
‘praṇāti śikṣati’ iti dānakarmasu pāṭhāt ||
(2) he indrāvaruṇā indrāvaruṇau bhavantau yadā āstām oṣadhyādikartṛtvenāmṛtāṃ tadā ni:- sidhvarīḥ nitarāṃ maṅgalasvabhāvā oṣadhīḥ vrīhyādyā oṣadhayaḥ āpaḥ udakāni ca mahimānaṃ bhavato- rmāhātmyam āśata vyāpnuvanti |
kiṃca yā yau bhavantau rajaso'ntarikṣalokasya saṃbandhinḥ adhvano
ni:'sidhvarī: | oṣadhī: | āpa: | āstām | indrāvaruṇā | mahimānam | āśata | yā | sisrtu: | rajasa: | pāre | ardhana: | yayo: | śatru: | naki: | adeva: | ohate ||2||
satyaṃ tadindrāvaruṇā kuśasya vāṃ madhva ūrmiṃ duhate sapta vāṇī: | tābhirdāśvāṃsamavataṃ śubhaspatī yo vāmadabdho abhi pāti cittibhi: || 3 || satyam | tat | indrāvaruṇā | kuśasya | vām | madhva: | ūrmim | duhate | sapta | vāṇī: | tābhi: | dāśvāṃsam | avatam | śubha: | patī iti | ya: | vām | adabdha: | abhi | pāti | citti'bhi: || 3 ||
ghṛtapṛṣa: saumyā jīradānava: sapta svasāra: sadana ṛtasya | yā ha vāmindrāvaruṇā ghṛtaśrutāstābhirdhattaṃ yajamānāya śikṣatam || 4 || ghṛta'pṛṣa: | saumyā: | jīra'dānava: | sapta | svasāra: | sadane | ṛtasya | yā: | ha | vām | indrāvaruṇā | ghṛta'śruta: | tābhi: | dhattam | yajamānāya | śikṣatam ||4||||30||
avaucāma mahate saubhagāya satyaṃ tveṣābhyāṃ mahimānamindriyam |
asmāntsvandrāvaruṇā ghṛtaśrutābhi: sāsebhirvataṃ śubhaspatī ||
5 ||
mārgasya pāre paratīre sisrtu: yathecchaṃ saraṇaṃ kuruta: |
yayorbhavato: śatru: śātayitā adeva: devapratiyogī asurādi: naki: ohate na ko'pi tarkagocaro bhavati |
svecchayā viharatorbhavato: gatipratighātaka: śatru: na ko'pyastītyartha: ||
( 3 ) he indrāvaruṇā indrāvaruṇau yā: samasaṃkhyākā: sarpaṇasvabhāvā vā vāṇī: vācya: saptahotra- kāṇāṃ stutirūpā vāca: kuśasya etannāmno yajamānasya saṃbandhana: madhva: madhurasya somasya ūrmiṃ tadupalakṣitaṃ rasaṃ prati vāṃ yuvāṃ duhate prapūrayantīti yat tattvamavitathameva |
tasmāttābhirvāṇībhirhetubhūtābhi: yuvāṃ dāśvāṃsaṃ havirdattavantaṃ yajamānam avataṃ rakṣatam |
yajamānaṃ viśinaṣṭi |
he śubhaspatī udakasya pālayitā- rāvandrāvaruṇau yo yajamāna: adabdha: kenāpyaṃhita: san cittibhi: prajñopalakṣitai: karmabhi: vāṃ yuvā- mabhi pāti sarvato rakṣati |
tamavatamiti pūrveṇānvaya: ||
( 4 ) tā: satyavācya: ka santītyapekṣāyāmāha |
ṛtasya yajñasya sadane sthāne sapta saptasaṃkhyākā: svasāra: svayaṃsāriṇyo vācya: santi |
kīdṛśyastā: |
ghṛtapṛṣa: |
ghṛtamityudakanāma |
tena somaraso lakṣyate |
tathā ca somarasasrāviṇya ityartha: |
saumyā: somāhā: jīradānava: kṣipradānā: |
kiṃca he indrāvaruṇā indrāvaruṇau yā vācya: vāṃ yuvāmuddiśya ghṛtaśrutaḥ somarasasrāviṇyo bhavanti athavā ājyasrāviṇyo bhavanti |
heti prasiddham |
tābhirhetubhūtābhi: ghṛtaṃ yajamānaṃ puṣṇītam |
tadarthaṃ ca yajamānāya śikṣatamabhi- mataṃ dattam ||
30 ||
( 5 ) he indrāvaruṇā indrāvaruṇau tveṣābhyāṃ dīptābhyāṃ bhavadbhyāṃ saṃpāditaṃ mahimānaṃ kīrtiviśeṣaṃ vayaṃ mahate bṛhate saubhagāya aiśvaryāya abaucāma varṇayema |
kīdṛśaṃ mahimānam |
satyamitatham |
yuṣmatsva-
avaucāma | mahate | saubhagāya | satyam | tveṣābhyām | mahimārnam | indriyam | asmān | su | indrāvaruṇā | ghṛtaścutaḥ | tri'bhiḥ | sātebhiḥ | avatam | śubhaḥ | pūtī iti ||5||
indrāvaruṇā yadṛṣibhyoṃ manīṣāṃ vāco mṛtiṃ śrutaṃmadattamagre | yāni sthānānyasṛjantu dhīrā yajñaṃ tanvānāstarpasāmyapaśyam || 6 || indrāvaruṇā | yat | ṛṣi'bhyaḥ | manīṣām | vācaḥ | matim | śrutam | adattam | agne | yāni | sthānāni | asṛjanta | dhīrāḥ | yajñam | tanvānāḥ | tapasā | abhi | apaśyam ||6||
indrāvaruṇā saumanasamarhasaṃ rāyaspoṣaṃ yajamāneṣu dhattam | prajāṃ puṣṭiṃ bhūtimasmāsu dhattaṃ dīrghāyutvāya pra tiraṃta na āyuḥ || 7 || indrāvaruṇā | saumanasam | arhasam | rāyaḥ | | poṣam | ya
saptame'nuvāke daśa sūktāni|
tatra 'agna ā yāhi' iti viṃśatyṛcaṃ prathamaṃ sūktaṃ pragāthaputrasya' bhargasyārṣamāgneyam'|
prathamātṛtīyādyayujo bṛhatyo dvitīyācaturthādiyujaḥ satobṛhatyaḥ|
tathā cānukrāntam—'agna ā viṃśatirbhagaḥ prāgātha āmeyaṃ prāgāthaṃ tu' iti|
prātaranuvāka āgneye ṛtau bārhate chandasyāśvinaśastre cedaṃ sūktam|
tathā ca sūtritam—'ayamabhiragna ā yāhi' (āśva. śrau. 4. 13) iti ||
agna ā yāhyagnihotāraṃ tvā vṛṇīmahe|
ā tvāmanaktu prayatā haviṣmatī yajiṣṭhaṃ barhirāsade ||
1 ||
agna | ā | yāhi | agni'bhiḥ | hotāram | tvā | vṛṇīmahe | ā | tvām | anaktu | pra'yatā | haviṣmatī | yajiṣṭham | barhiḥ | ā'sade || 1 ||
he 'gne 'agnibhiḥ yaṣṭavyaiḥ saha 'ā 'yāhi āgaccha|
tadarthaṃ 'hotāraṃ devānāmāhvātāraṃ 'tvā tvāṃ 'vṛṇīmahe|
tvā svāmāgataṃ 'prayatā adhvaryuhastābhyāṃ niyatā 'haviṣmatī ghṛtavatī 'yajiṣṭhaṃ 'tvā 'barhiḥ 'barhiṣa āsade āsādya 'ā sarvataḥ 'anaktu ||
acchā hi tvā sahasaḥ sūno aṅgiraḥ sucaścarantyadhvare|
ūrjo napātaṃ ghṛtakeśamīmahe'gniṃ yajñeṣu pūrvyam ||
2 ||
acchā | hi | tvā | sahasaḥ | sūno iti | aṅgiraḥ | sucaḥ | caranti | adhvare | ūrjaḥ | napātam | ghṛta'keśam | īmahe | agnim | yajñeṣu | pūrvyam || 2 ||
he 'sahasaḥ 'sūno balasya putra|
balena madhyamānatvāt|
he 'aṅgiraḥ aṅgirasāṃ madhye eka|
athavāṅgiratigatikarmā|
sarvatra saṃgata|
'tvā tvām 'adhvare yāge 'acchā abhiprāptuṃ 'sucaḥ 'caranti gacchanti|
ataḥ 'ūrjaḥ annasya 'napātaṃ na pātayitāraṃ rakṣakaṃ balasya nasāraṃ vā 'ghṛtakeśaṃ pradīpta- keśasthānīyajvālaṃ 'pūthyaṃ purātanaṃ purakaṃ vā 'agniṃ 'yajñeṣu asmadīyeṣu 'īmahe staumi ||
agne kavirvedhā asi hotā pāvaka yakṣyaḥ|
mandro yajiṣṭho adhvareṣvīḍyo vipraiḥ śukra manmabhiḥ ||
3 ||
agne | kaviḥ | vedhāḥ | asi | hotā | pāvaka | yakṣyaḥ | mandraḥ | yajiṣṭhaḥ | adhvareṣu | īḍyaḥ | vipraiḥ | śukra | manma'bhiḥ || 3 ||
he 'agne 'kaviḥ medhāvī tvaṃ 'vedhāḥ vidhātā 'asi phalānām|
he 'pāvaka 'hotā devānā- māhvātā homaniṣpādako vā 'yakṣyaḥ yaṣṭavyo'si|
he 'śukra dīpta 'mandraḥ madanīyaḥ 'yajiṣṭhaḥ yaṣṭatama- svam 'adhvareṣu yajñeṣu 'viprebhiḥ medhāvibhirṛtvigbhiḥ 'manmabhiḥ mananīyaiḥ stotraiḥ 'īḍyaḥ stutyo'si ||
adrogdhā vahośato yaviṣṭha devā~ ajasra vītaye|
abhi prayāṃsi sudhitā vaso gahi mandasva dhītibhirhitaḥ ||
4 ||
1. ga-ta-na-śa-pragāthaputrabhargasyā | 2. bha1-2-ghṛtavatī suk; bha3-havirbhiryuktā suk | 3. ga-ta-śa- 'barhiḥ' nāsti | 4. ya-anaktu bhakṣayatu | 5. ta3-4-na-śa-sapūrvya | 6. gha-pāvaka śodhaka |
adrogdham | ā | vaha | uśataḥ | yaviṣṭhya | devān | ajasra | vītaye | abhi | prayāṃsi | su'dhitā | ā | vaso iti | gṛhi | mandasva | dhītibhiḥ | hitaḥ ||4||
^adrogdham adrogdhāraṃ māṃ prati he ^yaviṣṭhya yuvatama ^ajasra nitya ^ā ^vaha ānaya |

kimartham |

tvamitsaprathā asyamai trātaṛtaskaviḥ | tvāṃ viprāsaḥ samidhāna dīdiva ā vivāsanti vedhasaḥ ||5|| tvam | it | su'prathāḥ | asi | agne | trātaḥ | ṛtaḥ | kaviḥ | tvām | viprāsaḥ | sam'idhāna | dīdivaḥ | ā | vivāsanti | vedhasaḥ ||5||
he ^agne ^trātaḥ rakṣaka ^ṛtaḥ satyabhūtaḥ ^kaviḥ krāntaprajñaḥ ^tvamit tvameva ^saprathāḥ sarvataḥ pṛthuḥ ^asi bhavasi |

32 ||
śorcā śociṣṭha dīdihi viśe mayo rāsva stotre mahā~ asi | devānāṃ śarmanmama santu sūryaḥ śatrupāhaḥ svamaryaḥ || 6 || śorca | śociṣṭha | dīdihi | viśe | maryaḥ | rāsva | stotre | mahān | asi | devānām | śarman | mama | santu | sūryaḥ | śatru'sahaḥ | su'amaryaḥ || 6 ||

1. jha-bha-dhītibhiḥ karmabhiḥ | 2. gha-āvivāsanti | 3. kha-gha-ta-na-bha-śa-mu-tvaṃ devānāṃ | 4. kha-jha- ta-1-2-tejo vāryaka; ga-tejo vā; ta3-4-śa-tejovāka | 5. ta-na-bha-1-2-dahateti; bha3-dahati |
ṛgveda: [a. 6. a. 4. va. 34]
mā no martāya ripave rakṣasvine māghaśaṃsāya rīradhaḥ | astedhadbhirastaraṇibhirvipraiḥ śivebhiḥ pāhi pāyubhiḥ || 8 || mā | naḥ | martāya | ripave | rakṣasvine | mā | aghaśaṃsāya | rīradhaḥ | astedhadbhiḥ | taraṇibhiḥ | vipraiḥ | śivebhiḥ | pāhi | pāyubhiḥ || 8 || naḥ asmān martāya maraṇadharmāya ripave śatrave hiṃsitre rakṣasvine balavate mā rīradhaḥ vaśamānaya | tathā aghaśaṃsāya pāpaśaṃsakāya mā rīradhaḥ | he vipraiḥ yuvatam astedhadbhiḥ ahiṃsakaiḥ taraṇibhiḥ tārakaiḥ śivebhiḥ sukhakaraiḥ pāyubhiḥ pālanaiḥ naḥ asmān pāhi rakṣa ||
pāhi no agne ekayā pāhuta dvitīyayā | pāhi gīrbhistisṛbhirūrjāṃ pate pāhi catusṛbhirvaso || 9 || pāhi | naḥ | agne | ekayā | pāhi | uta | dvitīyayā | pāhi | gīḥbhiḥ | tisṛbhiḥ | ūrjam | pate | pāhi | catu
naḥ asmabhyaṃ sunītī sunītyā śobhanayanayena puruspṛhaṃ bahubhiḥ spṛhaṇīyaṃ svayaśastaram atyantaṃ svabhūtakīrti dhanaṃ rāsva ca dehi || yena vaṃsām pṛtanāsu śardhatastaranto arya ādiśaḥ | sa tvaṃ nau vardha paryasā śacīvaso jinva dhiyau vasuvidaḥ || 12 || yena | vaṃsām | pṛtanāsu | śardhataḥ | tarantaḥ | aryaḥ | ādiśaḥ | saḥ | tvam | naḥ | vardha | paryasā | śacīvaso iti śacī'vaso | jinva | dhiyaḥ | vasu'vidaḥ ||12|| yena dhanena pṛtanāsu saṃgrāmeṣu śardhataḥ vegaṃ kurvataḥ aryaḥ arīnśatrūn ādiśaḥ ādeścha- kṣaprakṣeptṛn tarantaḥ vaṃsāma hiṃsāma tadannaṃ dehi | he śacīvaso prajñayā vāsayitaḥ karmadhana vā saḥ prasiddhaḥ tvaṃ naḥ asmān vardha vardhaya prīṇaya | paryasā annena tvaṃ vā vardha | asmadīyena paryasā haviṣā vasuvidaḥ vasūnāṃ lambhakāni dhiyaḥ karmāṇyasmadīyāni jinva prīṇaya || śiśāno vṛṣabho yathāgniḥ śṛṅge darvivat | tigmā asya harnavo na pratidhṛṣe sujambhaḥ sahaso yahuḥ || 13 || śiśānaḥ | vṛṣabhaḥ | yathā | agniḥ | śṛṅge iti | darvivat | tigmāḥ | asya | harnavaḥ | na | prati'dhṛṣe | su'jambhaḥ | sahasaḥ | yahuḥ || 13 || ayam agniḥ śṛṅge śiśānaḥ tīkṣṇīkurvan vṛṣabhaḥ yathā darvivat kampayati śiraḥ | evaṃ śastrasthānīyā jvālāḥ śiśānastīkṣṇīkurvan darvivat kampayati śiraḥ | asya agneḥ hanavo na hanava iva hanusthānīyā jvālāḥ tigmāḥ na pratidhṛṣe pratidharṣitumaśakyāḥ | yo'gniḥ sujambhaḥ sudaṃṣṭraḥ sahaso yahuḥ sahasaḥ putraḥ asya hanava ityarthaḥ || nuhi teṃ agne vṛṣabha pratidhṛṣe jambhāso yadvititṛṣe | sa tvaṃ nau hotaḥ suhutaṃ haviṣkṛdhi vasvāṃ no vāyā~ puru || 14 || nuhi | te | agne | vṛṣabha | prati'dhṛṣe | jambhāsaḥ | yat | vi'tiṣṭhase | saḥ | tvam | naḥ | hotariti | su'hutam | haviḥ | kṛdhi | vasvaṃ | naḥ | vāyā~ | puru ||14|| he vṛṣabha varṣaka te tava jambhāsaḥ jambhā dantasthānīyā jvālāḥ nahi pratidhṛṣe pratidharṣituṃ na śakyāḥ | yat yasmāt vitiṣṭhase vividhaṃ gacchasi | pravadhaṃsa ityarthaḥ | he hotaḥ homaniṣpādaka sa tvaṃ haviḥ asmaddattaṃ suhutaṃ kṛdhi kuru | naḥ asmabhyaṃ vāyā~ varaṇīyāni puru bahūni vasva dehi || śeṣe vanaiṣu mātroḥ saṃ tvā martāsa indhate | atandro havyā vahasi haviṣkṛt ādideveṣu rājasi || 15 || 1. ga-śardhatī vegaṃ kurvataḥ; gha-jñā-1-2-śa-śardhataḥ vegaṃ kurvataḥ; tā-4-śardhata vegaṃ kurvataḥ; na-vardhata vegaṃ kurvata | 2. jha-tara-4-na-bha-1-3-śa-śṛṃge sthānīyā | 3. kha-ga-ta-na-bha-dā- tigmāḥ ' nāsti |
śerṣe | vaneṣu | mātroḥ | sam | tvā | matausaḥ | indhate | atandraḥ | hunyā | vahasi | haviḥ'kṛtaḥ | āt | ita | deveṣu | rājasi || 15 ||

‘pro asmai’ iti dvādaśarcaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktaṃ kāṇvasya pragāthasyā

ā | yāhi | kṛṇavāma | te | indra | brahmāṇi | vardhanā | yebhiḥ | śaviṣṭha | cākanaḥ | bhadram | iha | śravasyate | bhadrāḥ | indrasya | rātayaḥ || 4 ||

‘ tarobhirvaḥ ’ iti pañcadaśarcaṃ saptamaṃ sūktaṃ pragāthaputrasya kalerārṣam |
prathamātṛtīyādyayujo bṛhatstho dvitīyācaturthyādiyujaḥ satobṛhatḥ pañcadaśī tvanuṣṭup |
tathā cānukramanikā— ‘ tarobhiḥ pañconā kaliḥ prāgāthaḥ ’ prāgāthamantyānuṣṭup ’ iti |
mahāvrate niṣkevalye bārhatatṛcāśītāvetatsūtam |
tathā ca pañcamāraṇyake sūtritaṃ—‘ tarobhirvo vidadvasumityuttamāsuddharati ’ ( ai. ā. 5. 2. 4 ) iti |
abhiṭome'cchāvākaśastre'yaṃ pragāthaḥ stotriyaḥ |
tathā ca sutritaṃ— ‘ tarobhirvo vidadvasuṃ taraṇirisipāsatīti pragāthau stotriyānurūpau ’ ( āśva. śrau. 5. 16 ) iti |
cāturviṃśike'hanyayameva pragāthaḥ stotriyaḥ |
sutritaṃ ca—‘ tarobhirvo vidadvasuṃ taraṇirisipāsati ’ ( āśva. śrau. 7. 4 ) iti |

dvitīyācaturthīṣaṣṭhyaḥ satobṛhatyaḥ |
saptamyādyāḥ ṣaṭ bṛhatyaḥ |
trayo- daśyuṣṇig caturdaśyanuṣṭup pañcadaśī purauṣṇigādyadvādaśakā aṣṭakā |
'ādyaścetpurauṣṇig' (anu. 5. 1. kha-ta-bha-u tvaṃ; ga-tvaṃ |
2. kha-ta-bha-śa-ta kiṃ; ga-kiṃ tat |
3. ga-ta-bha-śa-rathaṃ kīdṛśaṃ |

5. kha-ga-gha-ta-śa-mukhyāṃ |
2) iti hi talakṣaṇam |
indro devatā |
mahāvrate niṣkevalye bārhatatṛcāśītāvādita ekādaśarcaḥ |
tathā ca pañcamāraṇyake sūtritaṃ—' yo rājā carṣaṇīnāmityekādaśa ' ( ai. ā. 5. 2. 4 ) iti ||
yo rājā carṣaṇīnāṃ yātā rathebhiradhriguḥ |
viśvāsāṃ tarutā pṛtanānāṃ jyeṣṭho yo vṛtrahā guṇe ||
1 ||
yaḥ | rājā | carṣaṇīnām | yātā | rathebhiḥ | adhriguḥ | viśvāsām | tarutā | pṛtanānām | jyeṣṭhaḥ | yaḥ | vṛtrahā | guṇe || 1 ||
yaḥ indraḥ carṣaṇīnāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ rājā svāmī rathebhiḥ yātā gantā ca adhriguḥ adhrata- gamano'nyaiḥ viśvāsāṃ pṛtanānāṃ senānāṃ tarutā tārakaḥ |
yaḥ ca jyeṣṭhaḥ guṇairjyāyān |
yaśca vṛtrahā vṛtraṃ hatavān |
taṃ mahābhāgamindraṃ guṇe staumi ||
indraṃ taṃ śumbha puruhanmanvase yasya dvitā vidhurtarīṃ |
hastāya vajraḥ prati dhāyi darśato maho dive na sūryaḥ ||
2 ||
indram | tam | śumbha | puruhanman | avase | yasya | dvitā | vidhurtarīṃ | hastāya | vajraḥ | prati | dhāyi | darśataḥ | mahaḥ | dive | na | sūryaḥ || 2 ||
he puruhanman ṛṣe tvaṃ tam indraṃ śumbha haviṣpradānādinālaṃkuru |
kimartham |
avase rakṣaṇāya |
evamātmānaṃ saṃbodhya bravīti |
yasya tava vidhurtari vidhāraka indre dvitā dvitva- mastyaupamanaupamyam |
tava śatrūn hantumagravaṃ tvadanugrahāyānaudhayaṃ ceti dvaitamasti |
tatropyavihvaṃ darśayati |
hastāya karāya hananāya śatrūṇāṃ darśataḥ darśanīyaḥ mahaḥ mahān vajraḥ prati dhāyi pratinihito bhavati dive prakāśāya divasāya vā athavā divi dṛśyamānaḥ sūryaḥ iva ||
tasminneva śabde ' nakiṣṭam ' iti pragātho vaikalpikaḥ |
stotriyaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—' nakiṣṭaṃ karmaṇā naśasa tvā vṛhanto adrayaḥ ' ( āśva. śrau. 7. 4 ) iti ||
nakiṣṭaṃ karmaṇā naśadyaścakāra sadāvṛdham |
indraṃ na yajñairviśvagūrtamurvasamadhṛṣṭaṃ dhṛṣṇvojasam ||
3 ||
nakiḥ | tam | karmaṇā | naśat | yaḥ | cakāra | sadāvṛdham | indram | na | yajñaiḥ | viśvagūrtam | ṛvasam | adhṛṣṭam | dhṛṣṇu'ojasam || 3 ||
taṃ janamanyo marṣako janaḥ karmaṇā hananādidhyāpāreṇa nakiḥ naśat naiva vyāpnoti |
yaḥ indraṃ cakāra indramevānukūlaṃ yajñaiḥ sādhanaiḥ |
kīdṛśamindram |
sadāvṛdhaṃ sadā vardhakaṃ viśvagūrtaṃ sarvaiḥ stutyam ṛvasaṃ mahāntam adhṛṣṭam anyaiḥ dhṛṣṇvojasaṃ dharṣakabalam ||
apāḻhamugraṃ pṛtanāsu sāsahiṃ yasminmahīruhurujaryaḥ |
saṃ dhenavo jāyamāne anonavurdyāvaḥ kṣāmau anonavuḥ ||

yadghāva indra te śataṃ śataṃ bhūmīruta syuḥ |
na tvā vajrinsahasraṃ sūryā anu na jātaṣṭa rodasī ||

he indra te tava pratimānārthaṃ yat yadi dhāvaḥ dyulokāḥ syuḥ tathāpi nāpnuvanti |
uta api ca bhūmīḥ bhūmyaste tava mūrtipratibimbāya śataṃ syuḥ tathāpi nāpnuvanti |
he vajrin tvā tvāṃ sahasraṃ sūryāḥ agaṇitā api sūryāḥ na anu bhavanti |
na prakāśayantītyarthaḥ |

kiṃ bahunā |

1. gha-jña-asmabhyaṃ viśvebhirdevebhiḥ | 2. kha-ga-gha-ta-na-bha-śa-rayivaṃtaḥ | 3. gha-'rttāraṃ martyaṃ yajamānaṃ | 4. gha-vipra prājña | 5. kha-ta-na-bha-śa-devarātiḥ; gha-tava rāti |
agnim | vaḥ | deva'yajyā | agnim | pra'yati | adhvare | agnim | dhīṣu | prathamam | agnim | arvāta | agnim | kṣetrāya | sādhase || 12 ||
he yajamānāḥ yuṣmākaṃ devayajyā devayāgena nimittena devayāgārtham agniṃ staumīti śeṣaḥ |
tathā agnim adhvare yāge prayati prakarṣeṇa gacchati pravṛtte sati staumi prathamam itaradevebhyaḥ |
tathā agnim arvāta āgate ātṛvye staumi yajñavighnaparihārārtham |
tathā kṣetrāya kṣetrārthaṃ kṣetrasambandhane sādhase sādhanāya kṣetralābhāya staumi |
yajñānte kṣetralābharūpāya phalāya ca staumi |
evamādau madhye'nte ca sarvadā staumītyarthaḥ ||
agiriṣāṃ saruye dedātu na īśe yo vāryāṇām |
agniṃ toke tanaye śaśvadīmahe vasuṃ santaṃ tanūpām ||

1. ga-vo yuṣmākaṃ | 2. gha-staumi tathābhi dhīṣu subuddhiṣu prathamamitaradevebhyaḥ mukhyaṃ staumi | 3. ga-gha-taṃ-na-bha-yajñe vighnaṃ; kha-yajñairvipa0 | 4. gha-tathābhi | 5. kha-ga-gha-ta-na-śa- kṣetrāya ’ nāsti | 6. bha-’kṣetrāya ’ nāsti | 7. gha-purumīḻha bahusektaḥ | 8. gha-tathābhi | 9. kha-gha-ta-na-bha-agniḥ | 10. ta-śa-purumīḻha |
agni naḥ asmākaṃ dveṣaḥ dveṣṭṝn yotavai pṛthakkartuṃ guṇīmasi guṇīmaḥ stumaḥ |
tathā agni śaṃ sukhaṃ yośca bhayānāmamiśraṇaṃ ca dātave dātum |
athavā śaṃ sukhasya yomiśraṇāya ca guṇīmasi |
asmin pakṣe dveṣo yotavā ityanena saha samuccayārthaśabdaḥ |
so'gniḥ viṣvāsu sarvāsu viśva prajāsu avitaiva rakṣitā rājeva ṛpūṇām ṛṣīṇāmasmākaṃ vastu: vāsako devaḥ havyo bhuvat bhavatu |

‘udīrāthām’ ityaṣṭādaśarcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktamātreyasya gopavanasya saptavadhrervāārṣa gāyatramāśvinam |

kṣeti kṣemebhiḥ sādhubhirnakiye ghanti hanti yaḥ | agre suvīraṃ edhate || 9 || kṣeti | kṣemebhiḥ | sādhubhiḥ | nakiḥ | yam | ghanti | hanti | yaḥ | agre | suvīraḥ | edhate ||9||
he 'agre yo manuṣyaḥ 'sādhubhiḥ sādhayadbhiḥ 'kṣemebhiḥ pālanaiḥ saha 'kṣeti svagṛhe nivasati |
tathā 'yaṃ janaṃ 'nakiḥ na kecana 'ghanti na hiṃsanti |

sa manuṣyastava stotā khalu |
anyathā tasyaitāvanna ghaṭate |

6 ||
'ā me havam' iti navacaṃ pañcamaṃ sūktam |
kṛṣṇo nāmāṅgirasa ṛṣiḥ |

ā me haveṃ nāsatyāśvinā gacchataṃ yuvam | madhvaḥ somasya pītaye || 1 || ā | me | havam | nāsatyā | aśvinā | gacchatam | yuvam | madhvaḥ | somasya | pītaye || 1 ||
he 'nāsatyā | asatyamanayornāstīti nāsatyau | tādṛśau he 'aśvinā aśvinau 'yuvaṃ yuvāṃ 'me madīyaṃ 'havam āhvānaṃ śrutvā | yadvā | havam | hūyate'treti havo yajñaḥ | kevalasthādhikaraṇe'p chāndasaḥ | madīyaṃ yajñaṃ prati 'madhvaḥ madakarasya 'somasya 'pītaye pānāya tadartham 'ā 'gacchatam ||

he 'aśvinā aśvinau 'me madīyam 'imaṃ 'stomaṃ stotraṃ 'kiṃca 'me madīyam 'imam idamāgamanaviṣayaṃ 'havam āhvānaṃ ca 'śṛṇutam ||

he 'vājinīvasū annayuktadhanau | atreniranuvādārthaḥ | yadvā | vājo vajanaṃ kriyā | tadvatī vājinī | tadyuktadhanavantau he 'aśvinā aśvinau 'ayaṃ 'kṛṣṇaḥ nāma mantradraṣṭā ṛṣiḥ 'vāṃ yuvāṃ 'havate stutibhirāhvayati | kimartham | 'madhvaḥ 'somasya 'pītaye iti ||

8 ||
1. kha-bha1-dṛḍha ādyaṃgopete; ga-ta-bhara-dṛḍhā ādyaṃgopete; śa- dṛḍhāṃgopete |
2. ga- ‘ ca ’ nāsti |
3. kha-ga-ta-na-bha-śa-pratyāgacchanti stutibhirvinā nāgacchantīti |
‘ubhā hi dastrā’ iti pañcarcaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ sūktam |
triśvako nāma kṛṣṇasya putraḥ kṛṣṇa eva varṣiḥ |
jagatī chandaḥ |
aśvinau devatā |
tathānukramyate—‘ubhā hi pañca viśvako vā kārṣṇirjāgatam’ iti |
viniyogo liṅgādavagantavyaḥ ||
ubhā hi dastrā bhiṣajā mayobhuvobhā dakṣasya varcaso babhūvathuḥ |
tā vāṃ viśvako havate tanukṛthe mā no vi yauṣṭaṃ sakhyā mumocatam ||
1||
ubhā | hi | dastrā | bhiṣajā | mayaḥ'bhuvā | ubhā | dakṣasya | varcasaḥ | babhūvathuḥ | tā | vām | viśvakaḥ | havate | tanu'kṛthe | mā | naḥ | vi | yauṣṭam | sakhyā | mumocatam ||1||
he aśvinau ‘dastrā darśanīyau |
yadvā |
‘dasu upakṣaye’ |
sarveṣāṃ śatrūṇāmupakṣapayitārau |
‘bhiṣajā vaidyoṃ |
yadvā |
bhīrtānāṃ trāsayitārau |
yadā narā aśvinau stuvanti tadā tau teṣāṃ bhītimapanayata ityarthaḥ |
pṛpodarāditvādrūpasiddhiḥ |
tādṛśau ata eva ‘mayobhuvā mayasaḥ sukhasya bhāvayitārau ‘ubhā parasparaṃ dvitvasaṅkhyāpūrakau ‘ubhā ubhau dvau yuvāṃ ‘dakṣasya etannāmasya prajāpateḥ ‘vacasaḥ stuteḥ saṃbandhinau babhūvathuḥ |
‘hi prasiddhau |
purā yuvāṃ dakṣaṇāstāviṣāthāṃ khalu |
‘tā’ tādṛśau praśastau ‘vāṃ yuvāṃ ‘viśvakaḥ etannāmaka ṛṣiḥ ‘tanukṛthe |
tanoti kulamiti tanuḥ putraḥ |
tasya viṣṇāpo nimittaṃ ‘havate stutibhirāhvayati |
tasmāt ‘naḥ asmākaṃ ‘sakhyā sakhyāni yaṣṭayaṣṭa- vyatayā jātāni sakhitvāni ‘mā ‘vi ‘yauṣṭaṃ mā pṛthakurutam |
yauterluṅi sici rūpam |
kiṃca yuvāṃ ‘mumocatam asmānāgantuṃ rathe sthitvāśvapramahānmuñcatam |
muñcaterloṭi ‘bahula chandasi’ iti śapaḥ śluḥ |
adāgamaḥ |
atra viṣṇāpanāmānaṃ putramuddiśya tasya pitā viśvako yuvāmāhvayatītyātmānamāha |
yadvā |
viśvakasya pitā kṛṣṇo nāmarṣimaṃ putro viśvakaḥ putrārthaṃ yuvāmadhihvayatīti vadati ||
kathā nūnaṃ vāṃ vimanā upa stavadhurvaṃ dhiyaṃ dadhurvasyeṣṭaye |
tā vāṃ viśvako havate tanukṛthe mā no vi yauṣṭaṃ sakhyā mumocatam ||
2||
kathā | nūnam | vām | vi'manāḥ | upa | stavat | dhiyam | dadhuḥ | vasyaḥ'iṣṭaye | tā | vām | viśvakaḥ | havate | tanu'kṛthe | mā | naḥ | vi | yauṣṭam | sakhyā | mumocatam ||2||
he aśvinau ‘vimanāḥ etannāmaka ṛṣiḥ ‘nūnaṃ purā ‘kathā kathaṃ ‘vāṃ yuvām ‘upa ‘stavata kathamupāstaut |
tena stutau ‘yuvaṃ yuvāṃ ‘vasyaṣṭaye |
vasyo vasīyaḥ praśastaṃ dhanam |
tasyābhilaṣi- tasyeṣṭaye'dhigamanāya yadvā’ vasiṣṭhanasyeṣṭaye’ prāptaye ‘dhiyaṃ buddhiṃ vimanase ‘dadhuḥ |
adadāyāṃ khalu |
vasya iti vasumacchabdādīyasuni ‘vinmatorluk’ iti luk |
īyasuna īkāralopaścchandasaḥ |
tādṛśau yuvāṃ ‘viśvako ‘havate iti gatam ||
yuvaṃ hi ṣmā purubhujamedhatuṃ viṣṇāpve dadhurvasyeṣṭaye |
tā vāṃ viśvako havate tanukṛthe mā no vi yauṣṭaṃ sakhyā mumocatam ||
3||
yuvam | hi | sma | puru'bhujā | imam | edhatum | viṣṇāpve | dadhuḥ | vasyaḥ'iṣṭaye | tā | vām | viśvakaḥ | havate | tanu'kṛthe | mā | naḥ | vi | yauṣṭam | sakhyā | mumocatam ||3||
1. kha-ga-jña-ta1-2-3-na-bha-śa-tau |
2. kha-ta-na-śa-prayastī |
3. kha-yadvāvasiṣṭaḥ ga-bha3-yadvā viśiṣṭaḥ; ta-na-bhara-mu-yadvāvaśiṣṭaḥ; śa-yadvā avaśiṣṭaḥ |
he 'purubhujā purubhujau puruṇāṃ dhanādidānena bhojayitārau |
yadvā |
bahūnāṃ stotṝṇāṃ pāla- yitārāvaśvinau |
'yuvam |
'hi 'vam ityavadhāraṇe |
yuvāmeva 'imamedhatam |
edhate : 'edhivahośratuḥ' ' ( u. sū. 1. 78 ) iti catupratyayaḥ |
'citvādantodāttaḥ |
imāṃ dhanādivṛddhiṃ 'viṣṇāve |
viṣṇoḥ sarveṣāṃ devānāṃ mukhyatvāt tadgrahaṇe 'sarve devā gṛhītā bhavanti |
viṣṇavādīn karmaṇā vyāpnotīti viṣṇāpuḥ |
pṛṣodarādiḥ |
yadvā |
viṣṇuṃ sarvagraheṣu vyāsaṃ somaṃ daśāpavitreṇāpunātīti |
tasya caturthī viṣṇāpu iti |
etannāmake mama putre pautre vā |
putrāya pautrāya vā |
imāṃ dhanādivṛddhiṃ 'dadathuḥ adattam |
kimartham |
'vasyaiṣṭaye vasīyasaḥ praśastadhanasyeṣṭaya icchāṃ pūrayitum |
'kriyārthopapadasya ' ( pā. sū. 2. 3. 14 ) iti caturthī |
'tā 'vām iti pūrvavadvyākhyeyam ||
uta tyaṃ vīraṃ dhanasāgṛjīṣiṇaṃ dūre citsantaṃmarvase havāmahe |
yasya svādiṣṭhā sumatiḥ pituryathā mā no vi yauṣṭaṃ saruyā mumocatam ||
4||
uta | tyam | vīram | dhana'sām | ṛjīṣiṇam | dūre | cit | santam | avase | havāmahe | yasya | svādiṣṭhā | su'matiḥ | pituḥ | yathā | mā | naḥ | vi | yauṣṭam | saruyā | mumocatam ||4||
he aśvinau 'uta api ca 'vīraṃ karmaṇi samarthaṃ 'dhanasāṃ dhanānāṃ saṃbhaktāram 'ṛjīṣiṇam |
ṛjīṣo'rjito'bhidhutaḥ ' somaḥ ' |
tadantaṃ 'dūre 'cit dūra eva 'santaṃ bhavantaṃ vinaṣṭamiva 'tyaṃ taṃ viṣṇāpavam 'avase asmākaṃ rakṣaṇāya 'havāmahe āhvayāmaḥ |
putro hi pitaraṃ rakṣati |
kiṃca 'yasya putrasya pautrasya vā 'sumatiḥ śobhanā stutiḥ 'svādiṣṭhā svādutamā |
atiśayena devānāṃ svādukāriṇorityarthaḥ |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
'pituryathā |
piturviśvasya stutiyrthā devānāṃ prītikarī' tadvat |
tasmāttamāhvayāma iti śeṣaḥ |
'mā 'no iti gatārthaḥ ||
ṛtena devaḥ savitā śamāyata ṛtasya śṛṅgamuruviyā vi prapathe |
ṛtaṃ sāsāha mahi citpṛtanyato mā no vi yauṣṭaṃ saruyā mumocatam ||
5||
ṛtena | devaḥ | saviā | śam'āyate | ṛtasya | śṛṅgam | uruviyā | vi | prapathe | ṛtam | sāsāha | mahi | cit | pṛtanyataḥ | mā | naḥ | vi | yauṣṭam | saruyā | mumocatam ||5||
ṛṣiḥ satyapraśaṃsāṃ karoti |
he aśvinau 'devaḥ 'dyotanān : 'savitā sarvasya svasvakamaṇi preraka etannāmako devaḥ 'ṛtena satyena 'śamāyate sāyaṃkāle svakiraṇasamūhaṃ śamayati |
tato'nantaraṃ sa eva savitā 'ṛtasya satyasya 'śṛṅgam agram 'urviyā uru vistīrṇaṃ yathā bhavati tathā prātaḥkāle 'vi 'prapathe viśeṣeṇa prathayati |
sarvato vistārayati |
kiṃca 'pṛtanyataḥ pṛtanāmicchataḥ saṃyuyutsoḥ śatroḥ 'mahi 'cit mahadapi balam 'ṛtaṃ satyaṃ 'svayamevābhibhavati |
itthambhūtaṃ praśastamabhūt |
tasmādyuvāmapi tenartena 'naḥ asmākaṃ sakhyāni 'mā 'vi 'yauṣṭam |
atrāgantumaśvarabhmīna sujhatamiti ||
9 ||
'yugmī vām ' iti ṣarcaṃ saptamaṃ sūktam |
tathā cānukramyate—' yugmī ṣaḍvāsiṣṭho vā yugmīkaḥ : priyamedho vā prāgāthaṃ ha ' iti |
vasiṣṭhaputro yugmīka ṛṣirāṅgirasaḥ priyamedho vā |
ubhayatra
1. ga-u-sū- 'vadyośratuḥ | 2. śa-tadgrahaṇe | 3. gha-upārjitaḥ abhidhutasomaḥ; jha-taya-bha-śa- ṛjīṣopārjitobhidhutasomaḥ | 4. ga-prītikāriṇī | 5. kha-ta-bha-śa-devī | 6. gha-satya sāsāha |
vāśabdādyadobhāvapi na syātāṃ tadā prakṛta āṅgirasa: kṛṣṇa eva ṛṣi: |
ayujo bṛhatyo yuja: satobṛhatya: |
aśvinau devatā |
prātaranuvāka āśvine kratau bārhate chandasyāśvinaścedaṃ sūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ghunnī vāṃ yatstha iti bārhatam ’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 15) iti ||
ghunnī vāṃ stomau aśvinā krivinaṃ sekū ā gaṃtam |
madhva: sutasya sa dvivi priyo narā pātaṃ gaurāvireriṇe ||
1 ||
ghunnī | vām | stoma: | aśvinā | krivi: | na | sekai | ā | gatam | madhva: | sutasya | sa: | dvivi | priya: | narā | pātam | gaurau'iva | iriṇe || 1 ||
he aśvinau aśvinau ayamanupakṣīṇastotro ghunnīka etannāmaka ṛṣi: vāṃ yuvayo: stoma: stotā bhavati |
yuṣmatstutau kṛtāyāṃ stotrāṇi nālpībhavanti’ kiṃtu punarvardhanta ityartha: |
tatra dṛṣṭānta: |
krivinaṃ |
kriviriti rūpanāma |
kūpo yathā seke udakasecane vṛṣṭau bhavantyāṃ nālpodako bhavati tadvat |
yadvā |
vāṃ yuvayo: stomo yuṣmadviṣayā stutiryukthyabhavati khalu |
stotre kṛte tasmā annādikaṃ prayacchata ityartha: |
tasmādyuvāmasmadīyaṃ yajñaṃ prati ā gatam |
stotrāṇi śrotumāgacchatam |
he narā narau netārauśvinau sa: ayaṃ stotā divi dyotamāne asmin yajñe sutasya abhisutasya madhva: madakarasya somasya priya: stotrakāritvena priyatamo bhavati |
tatastena sutaṃ somaṃ pātaṃ yuvāṃ śīghramāgasya pibatam |
tatra dṛṣṭānta: |
gaurāvi va yathā tṛṣitau gaurāvivanāmako mṛgau iriṇe taṭākādiṣūdakapānārthaṃ śīghramāgacchatastadvat ||
pibataṃ gharmaṃ madhumantamaśvinā barhi: sīdataṃ narā |
tā mandasānā manuṣo duroṇa ā ni pātaṃ vedasā vaya: ||
2 ||
pibatam | gharmam | madhu'mantam | aśvinā | barhi: | sīdatam | narā | tā | mandasānā | manuṣa: | duroṇe | ā | ni | pātam | vedasā | vaya: || 2 ||
he aśvinau aśvinau madhumantaṃ madavantam |
madakāriṇamityartha: |
tādṛśaṃ rasavantaṃ vā gharmam |
‘nṛ kṣaraṇadīptyo: |
’ pātreṣu kṣarantaṃ somaṃ pibatam |
yadvā |
madhumantam |
madhurmadakara: soma: |
taddhantaṃ gharmaṃ mahāvīrapātragataṃ kṣīraṃ pibataṃ somaṃ ceti |
na tu sāhacaryeṇa tadasaṃbhavāt |

punaḥ upa naḥ ityādarārthaḥ ||
dvitā yo vṛtrahantamo vida indraḥ śatakratuḥ | urpa no haribhiḥ sutam || 32 || dvitā | yaḥ | vṛtrahan'tamaḥ | vide | indraḥ | śata'kratuḥ | urpa | naḥ | hari'bhiḥ | sutam ||32||
