tva śravasyupamānyukthyā suteṣvindra girvaṇaḥ ||
2 ||
1. ga-gha-jha-tṛ-1-2-na-yaṃ tvāṃ; ta3-4-yaṃ tāṃ; śa-yaṃ taṃ |
2. kha-ta-śa-vā |
3. ga-havirbharaṇa |
masve | su'śipa | hari'vaḥ | tat | īmahe | tve iti | ā | bhuṣanti | vedhasaḥ | tava | śravāṃsi | upa'māni | ukthyā | suteṣu | indra | girvaṇaḥ || 2 ||
he ᵥsuśipa śobhanahano śobhanoṣṇīṣin vā ᵥharivaḥ aśvavan ᵥgirvaṇaḥ gīrbhirvandanīya ᵥindra ᵥtve tvayi ᵥvedhasaḥ paricārakāḥ ᵥā ᵥbhuṣanti ābhavanti |
ᵥmatsva somena mādyamānam |
kiṃca ᵥtat tvāṃ vayam ᵥīmahe yācāmahe |
kiṃ yācyamityatrāha |
ᵥsuteṣu someṣvabhiputeṣu satsu ᵥtava ᵥśravāṃsi annāni ᵥupamāni upamānabhūtāni ᵥukthyā praśaṃsthāni ca santviti ||
śaryantaiva sūrye viśvedindrasya bhakṣata |
vasūni jāte janamān orjasā prati bhāgaṃ na dīṃdhima ||
3 ||
śaryantaḥ'iva | sūryam | viśvā | ita | indrasya | bhakṣata | vasūni | jāte | janamāne | orjasā | prati | bhāgam | na | dīṃdhima || 3 ||
he asmadīyā janāḥ ᵥśaryantaiva ᵥsūrya yathā samāśritā raśmayaḥ sūrya bhajante tathā ᵥindrasya ᵥviśvat viśvānyeva dhanāni ᵥbhakṣata bhajata |
sa ca yāni ᵥvasūni dhanāni ᵥjāte utpanne ᵥjanamāne janiṣyamāṇe ca ᵥojasā balena karoti ᵥbhāgaṃ ᵥna pithya bhāgamiva tāni dhanāni ᵥprati ᵥdīṃdhima pratidhārayāmeti |
yadvā |
śaryantaiva sūrya yathā samāśritā raśmayaḥ sūryamupatiṣṭhante tathendrasya viśvā vibhaktumicchantiḥ samāśritā maruta indramupatiṣṭhanta iti śeṣaḥ |
upasthāya ca maruto vasūni udakalakṣaṇāni dhanāni jāte jātāya janamāne janiṣyamāṇāya ca manuṣyāya ca ojasā balena bhakṣata vibhajante |
tatra cāsmākaṃ yo bhāgastaṃ bhāgam |
neti saṃpratyarthe |
pratītyeṣo'nvityetasya sthāne |
anu dīṃdhima vayamanudhyāyāma |
tathā ca yāskaḥ—‘ samāśritāḥ sūryamupatiṣṭhante |
api vopamārthe syātsūryamindramupatiṣṭhanta iti |
sarvāṇīndrasya dhanāni vibhakṣyamāṇāḥ |
sa yathā dhanāni vibhajati jāte ca janiṣyamāṇe ca taṃ vayaṃ bhāgamanudhyāyāma’ (niru. 6. 7) iti ||
anarśarāti vasudāmupa stuhi bhadrā indrasya rātayaḥ |
so asya kāmaṃ vidhato na ropati mano dānārya codayan ||
4 ||
anarśa'rātim | vasu'dām | upa | stuhi | bhadrāḥ | indrasya | rātayaḥ | saḥ | asya | kāmam | vidhataḥ | na | ropati | manaḥ | dānārya | codayan || 4 ||
he stotaḥ ᵥanarśarātim apāpakadānam |
apāpiṣṭhasya dātāramityarthaḥ |
tathā ca yāskaḥ— ‘anarśarātimanaślīladānamaślīlaṃ pāpakam’ (niru. 6. 23) iti |
ᵥvasudāṃ dhanasya dātāramindram ᵥupa ᵥstuhi |
yataḥ ᵥindrasya ᵥrātayaḥ dānāni ᵥbhadrāḥ kalyāṇāni |
mahadaiśvaryakārīṇītyarthaḥ |
yataśca ᵥsaḥ indraḥ svakīyaṃ ᵥmanaḥ ᵥdānāya abhīṣṭapradānāya ᵥcodayan prerayan ᵥvidhataḥ paricarataḥ ᵥasya stotuḥ ᵥkāmam icchāṃ ᵥna ᵥropati na hinasti ||
1. na-bha1-taṃ | 2. kha-ga-jha-ta1-2-3-śa-vibhajate | 3. ṛ-ta-na-bha3-saṃpratyartha dha; śa-saṃpratyarthe tva | 4. kha-ta-na-bha3-śa-manuṣyāyema; ga-°manudhārayema | 5. kha-ga-ta-na-bha3-śa-vibhajyamānāḥ | 6. kha-ta1-2-3-na-bha3-°manudhyāyema |
tvamindra pratūrtiṣu viśvā asi spṛdhaḥ | aśtithā janitā viśvatūrasi tvaṃ tūrya taruṣyataḥ || 5 || tvam | indra | pra'tūrtiṣu | abhi | viśvāḥ | asi | spṛdhaḥ | aśtithā | janitā | viśvataḥ | asi | tvam | tūrya | taruṣyataḥ || 5 || he indra tvaṃ pratūrtiṣu saṃgrāmeṣu viśvāḥ sarvāḥ spṛdhaḥ yuddhakāriṇīḥ śatrusenāḥ abhi asi abhinavasi | kiṃca he tūrya śatrūṇāṃ bādhakendra tvam aśtithā daivyānāmaśastīnāṃ hantā asi | janitā asurebhyo'śastīnāṃ janayitā cāsi | viśvataḥ sarvasya śatruvargasya hiṃsitā cāsi | taruṣyataḥ bādhakāṃśca bādhamāno'si ||
anu te śuṣmaṃ turyantamīyatuḥ kṣoṇī śiśuṃ na mātaro | viśvāste spṛdhaḥ śrathayanta manyave vṛtraṃ yadindra turvasi || 6 || anu | te | śuṣmam | turyantam | īyatuḥ | kṣoṇī iti | śiśum | na | mātarau | viśvāḥ | te | spṛdhaḥ | śrathayanta | manyave | vṛtram | yat | indra | turvasi || 6 || he indra te tava śuṣmaṃ balaṃ turyantaṃ hiṃsantaṃ śatruṃ kṣoṇī dyāvāpṛthivyau mātarā mātarau śiśuṃ na śiśumivā anu īyatuḥ anugacchataḥ | gamanamātre dṛṣṭāntaḥ | kiṃca he indra tvaṃ yat yasmāt vṛtraṃ śatruṃ turvasi haṃsi ataḥ te tava manyave krodhāya viśvāḥ sarvāḥ spṛdhaḥ saṃgrāma- kāriṇyaḥ senāḥ śrathayanta śrathitāḥ khinnā bhavanti ||
ita ūtī vo ajaraṃ prahetāramaprahitam | āśuṃ jetāraṃ hetāraṃ rathītamamṛtaṃ tubhyāvṛdham || 7 || itaḥ | ūtī | vaḥ | ajaram | pra'hetāram | apra'hitam | āśum | jetāram | hetāram | rathi'tamam | atūrtam | tubhyā'vṛdham || 7 || he asmadīyajanāḥ vaḥ yūyam ajaraṃ jarārahitaṃ prahetāraṃ śatrūṇāṃ prerakam aprahitaṃ kenāpya- preṣitam āśuṃ vegavantaṃ jetāraṃ śatrūṇāṃ hetāraṃ gantāraṃ rathītamaṃ rathināṃ śreṣṭham atūrtaṃ kenāpya- hiṃsitaṃ tubhyāvṛdham udakasya vardhayitāramindram ūtī ūtyai rakṣaṇāya itaḥ kuruta | puraskuruteti yāvat ||
iṣkartāraṃ niṣkṛtaṃ sahaskṛtaṃ śatasūrti śatakratum | samānamindramarvase havāmahe vasaṃvānaṃ vasujuvam || 8 || iṣkartāram | niḥ'kṛtam | sahaḥ'kṛtam | śata'mūrtima | śata'kratum | samānam | indram | avase | havāmahe | vasaṃvānam | vasu'juvam || 8 ||
1. ta-śa-sa abhi | 2. kha-ta1-2-3-mu-daityānāṃ | 3. kha-jha-tara-3-haṃtāraṃ; gha-hotāraṃ | 4. gha-detānāmāhātāraṃ |
vikkatāraṃ śatrūṇāṃ saṃsaktāraṃm vaniṣkṛtaṃ svayamanyairasaṃskṛtaṃ vsahaskṛtaṃ balena kṛtaṃ vśatamūrti bahurakṣaṇaṃ vśatakratuṃ bahumajñaṃ bahukarmāṇaṃ vā vsamānaṃ bahūnāṃ sādhāraṇaṃ vvasavānaṃ dhananyācchādayantaṃ vvasūjuvaṃ yajamānebhyo vasūnāṃ prerayitāram vindramavase rakṣaṇāya vayaṃ vhavāmahe hayāmaḥ ||
3 ||
'atha te' iti dvādarcaṃ saptamaṃ sūktaṃ bhṛgugotrasya nemasyārṣam |
'ayamasmi jaritaḥ' iti dvenendro nemasamīpametya svakīyaṃ māhātmyamavocat atastasya dvacasya sa evarṣiḥ 'yasya vākyaṃ sa ṛṣiḥ' (anu. 2. 4) iti nyāyāt |
ṣaṣṭhī jagatau |
saptamyādyāstisro'nuṣṭubhaḥ |
śiṣṭāstriṣṭubhaḥ |
indro devatā |
'yadvāgvadantī' 'devīṃ vācam' ityete vāgdevasye śiṣṭā aindrayaḥ |
tathā cānukrāntam— ayaṃ te dvādaśa namo bhārgavastraiṣṭubhaṃ ṣaṣṭhī jagatau paśastisro'nuṣṭubho'yamiti dvenendra ātmanamastau- dupāntye vācyau' iti |
vāgdevatye paśau 'yadvāgvadantī' iti vapāyā anuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca— 'yadvāgvadantyavicetanāni pataṅgo vācaṃ manasā bibharti' (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti |
pūrvokta eva paśau 'devīṃ vācam' iti haviṣo yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'devīṃ vācamajanayanta devā janīyanto nvagrava iti tisraḥ' (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti |
prayāṇasamaye vayasāmanojñā vācaḥ śruvaitāṃ japet |
sūtritaṃ ca—'kanikradajjanuṣaṃ prabuvāṇa iti sūkte japeddevīṃ vācamajanayanta devā iti ca' (āśva. gṛ. 3. 10. 9) iti ||
ayaṃ te emi tanvā purastādviśvai devā abhi mā yanti paścāta | yadā mahyaṃ dīrgho bhāgamindrādinamayā kṛṇavo vīryāṇi || 1 || ayam | te | emi | tanvā | purastāt | viśvai | devāḥ | abhi | mā | yanti | paścāta | yadā | mahyam | dīrghaḥ | bhāgam | indra | āt | it | mayā | kṛṇavaḥ | vīryāṇi || 1 || he vindra vte tava vpurastāt agrataḥ vtanvā putreṇa saha vayam aham vemi śatrūnabhibhavituṃ gacchāmi | tavāgrato gacchantaṃ māṃ vviśvai vdevāḥ tvayā saha vpaścāt vabhi vyanti abhigacchanti | vyadā tvaṃ vmahyaṃ bhārgavāya nemāya vbhāgaṃ śatruṣu sthitaṃ bhāgaṃ vdīrghaḥ dhārayasi vādit anantara- meva vmayā saha macchatrūnjetuṃ vvīryāṇi pauruṣāṇi vkṛṇavaḥ kuru | yadi śatruṣu sthitaṃ dhanaṃ mahyaṃ vitsasi^2 tarhi śatrujayārthaṃ gacchataḥ saputrasya mama sāhāyyaṃ kurviteti bhāvaḥ ||
dadhāmi te madhuno bhakṣamagre hitastai bhāgaḥ suto astu somaḥ | asaśca tvaṃ dakṣiṇataḥ sakhā me'dhā vṛtrāṇi jaṅghanāva bhūrī || 2 || dadhāmi | te | madhunaḥ | bhakṣam | agre | hitaḥ | te | bhāgaḥ | sutaḥ | astu | somaḥ | asaḥ | ca | tvam | dakṣiṇataḥ | sakhā | me | adhā | vṛtrāṇi | jaṅghanāva | bhūrī || 2 || he indra vte tubhyaṃ vmadhunaḥ madakarasya somasya vbhakṣamagre prathamaṃ vdadhāmi dhārayāmi | vsutaḥ vabhiṣutaḥ vbhāgaḥ bhajanīyaḥ vsomaḥ vte tava hṛdaye vhitaḥ nihitaḥ vastu bhavatu | api ca vtvaṃ vme mama vdakṣiṇataḥ dakṣiṇapārśve vsakhā san vasaḥ sthito bhava | adha atha vbhūri bahūni vvṛtrāṇi asmadīyānchatrūn vjaṅghanāva tvamahaṃ cobhau āvāṃ hanvaḥ ||
pra su stomaṃ bharata vājayanta indrāya satyaṃ yadi satyamasti |
nendro astīti nema u tva āha ka īṃ dadarśa kamabhi bravāma ||
3 ||
1. kha-ga-ta-bha-preraka |
2. kha-ga-ta-bha2-śa-vadaṃti |
3. ta-na-decchāṃtatsi |
pra | su | stoma̭m | bharata | vāja̭'yantaḥ | indra̭ya | sa॒tyam | yadi̭ | sa॒tyam | asti̭ | na | indra̭: | asti̭ | iti̭ | nermaḥ | oṃ iti̭ | tvaḥ | āha̭ | kaḥ | īm | dadarśa̭ | kam | abhi̭ | stuvāma || 3 ||
he janāḥ ᵛvājayantaḥ saṃgrāmamicchanto yūyam |
'pausye vāje' iti saṃgrāmanāmasu pāṭhāt |
ᵛindrāya ᵛsatyaṃ satyabhūtaṃ ᵛstomaṃ ᵛsu suṣṭhu ᵛpra ᵛbharata |
indro'stītyetat ᵛyadi ᵛsatyamasti bhavati |
indrāstitve kaḥ saṃdehaḥ |
tatrāha |
ᵛnema ᵛu bhārgavo nema eva ᵛindraḥ nāma ᵛhvaḥ kaścit ᵛna ᵛasti iti ᵛāha |
tatra kāraṇaṃ darśayati |
ᵛka ᵛīm enamindraṃ ᵛdadarśaṃ adrākṣīt |
na ko'pyapaśyat |
ataḥ ᵛkaṃ vayam ᵛabhi ᵛṣṭvāma abhiṣṭumaḥ |
ta
viśvā | ita | tā | te | sarvaneṣu | pra'vācyā | yā | cakrathai | magha'van | indra | sunvate | pārāvatam | yat | puru'saṃbhṛtam | vasu | apa'avṛṇoḥ | śarabhāya | ṛṣi'bandhave || 6 ||
svasamīpamāgatamindraṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃtuṣṭa ṛṣirendrasya vividhāni karmāṇi dānaṃ ca ‘viśvattā te’ ityanena ṛcena stauti |
he maghavanindra tvaṃ savaneṣu yajñeṣu sunvate somābhiṣavaṃ kurvate yajamānāya yā yāni karmāṇi cakratha akaroḥ te tava tā tāni karmāṇi pravācyā pravaktavyāni viśvet anantānyeva |
kiṃca tvaṃ pārāvataṃ parāvatnāma kasya kasyacicchatroḥ svabhūtaṃ yat dhanamasti tat ṛṣibandhave śarabhāya śarabhanāmna ṛṣaye apāvṛṇoḥ apavṛtavānasi |
purusaṃbhṛtaṃ yathā bahusaṃbhṛtaṃ bhavati ||
4 ||
pra nūnaṃ dhāvatā pṛthaghe yo vo avāvarit |
ni ṣīṃ vṛtrasya marmaṇi vajramindro apīpatat ||
7 ||
pra | nūnam | dhāvata | pṛthak | na | iha | yaḥ | vaḥ | avāvarīt | ni | sīm | vṛtrasya | marmaṇi | vajram | indraḥ | apīpatat || 7 ||
yaḥ śatruḥ nūnam idānīṃ pra dhāvata pradhāvati pṛthak iha pṛthak tiṣṭhati ca vaḥ yuṣmān na avāvarīt na vicārayati ca tasya vṛtrasya śatroḥ marmaṇi marmasthāne indraḥ vajraṃ kuliśaṃ ni apīpatat nitarāmapātayat ||
manojavā ayamāna āyasīmataratpuram |
divaṃ suparṇo gatvāya somaṃ vajriṇa ābharat ||
8 ||
manaḥ'javāḥ | ayamānaḥ | āyasīm | atarat | puram | divam | su'parṇaḥ | gatvāya | somam | vajriṇe | ābharat || 8 ||
suparṇaḥ garutmān manojavāḥ manovegaḥ ayamānaḥ gacchan āyasīṃ hiraṇmayīṃ puraṃ nagarīm atarat atārīt |

samudre antaḥ śayata udrā vajro abhīvṛtaḥ |
bharantyasmai saṃyataḥ puraḥ'prasavaṇā balim ||
9 ||
samudre | antaḥ | iti | śayate | udrā | vajraḥ | | abhi'vṛtaḥ | bharanti | asmai | sam'udyataḥ | puraḥ'prasavaṇāḥ | balim || 9 ||
yaḥ vajraḥ samudre antaḥ samudrasya madhye śayate śete yaśca udrā udakena abhivṛtaḥ asmai vajrāya saṃyataḥ saṃgrāmasya |
‘sagan saṃyataḥ’ iti saṃgrāmanāmasu pāṭhāt |
puraḥprasavaṇāḥ purastādgacchataḥ śatravaḥ balim upahāraṃ bharanti dhārayanti |
tasya chedyā bhavantītyarthaḥ ||

2. na-bha-śa-ṛṣipriyāya śarabhāya |

5. kha-gha-ta-na-śa-tasmā asmai |

v rāṣṭrī rājanaśīlā vdevānāṃ vmandrā mādayitrī' vā vyat yadā vvāk vavicetanāni vijñānarahitānaprajñātānarthān vvadantī prajñāpayantī vniṣasāda yajñe niṣīdati tadā vcatasraḥ diśaḥ prati vūrjam annaṃ vpayāṃsi tatkāraṇabhūtāni udakāni vduduhe |
vasyāḥ mādhyamikāyā vācaḥ svabhūtaṃ yat vparamaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ tat vka vjagāma ka gacchatīti na dṛśyata ityarthaḥ |
tathā ca yāskaḥ—'yadvā- gadantyavicetanānvijñānāni rāṣṭrī devānāṃ niṣasāda mandrā madanā catasro'nu diśa ūrjaṃ duduhe payāṃsi ka svitsyāḥ paramaṃ jagāmeti svapṛthivīṃ gacchatīti vā yadādityaraśmayo harantīti vā' (niru. 11. 28) iti ||
devī vācamajanayanta devāstāṃ viśvarūpāḥ paśavo vadanti | sā nau mandreṣamūrjaṃ duhānā dhenurvāgasmānupa suṣṭutaitu || 11 || devīm | vācam | ajanayanta | devāḥ | tām | viśvarūpāḥ | paśavaḥ | vadanti | sā | naḥ | mandrā | iṣam | ūrjam | duhānā | dhenuḥ | vāk | asmān | upa | su'stutā | ā | etu || 11 || eṣā mādhyamikā vāk sarvaprāṇyantaḥpātā dharmābhivādinī bhavatīti vibhutimupadarśayati' | yāṃ vdevīṃ dyotamānāṃ mādhyamikāṃ vvācaṃ vdevāḥ mādhyamikāḥ vajanayanta janayanti vtāṃ vācaṃ vviśvarūpāḥ sarvarūpā vyaktavāco'vyaktavācaśca vpaśavo vvadanti | tatpūrvakaratvādvākpravṛtteḥ | vsā vvāk devī vmandrā madanā stutyā harṣayitrī vā vṛṣṭipradānenāsmabhyam viṣam annam vūrjaṃ payo- ghṛtādirūpaṃ rasaṃ ca vduhānā kṣarantī vdhenuḥ dhenubhūtā vsuṣṭutā asmābhiḥ stutā' vasmān nemān vupa vetu vupagacchatu | varṣaṇāyoyuktetyarthaḥ | tathā ca yāskaḥ—'devīṃ vācamajanayanta devāstāṃ viśvarūpāḥ paśavo vadanti vyaktavācaścāvyaktavācaśca sā no madanāṃ ca rasaṃ ca duhānā dhenu- rvāgasmānupaitu suṣṭutā' (niru. 11. 29) iti ||
sakhai viṣṇo vitaraṃ vi kramasva dyaurdehi lokaṃ vajrāya viṣkambhe | hanāva vṛtraṃ riṇacāva sindhūnindrasya yantu prasave visṛṣṭāḥ || 12 || sakhai | viṣṇo | iti | vi'taram | vi | kramasva | dyauḥ | dehi | lokam | vajrāya | vi'skambhe | hanāva | vṛtram | riṇacāva | sindhūn | indrasya | yantu | pra'save | vi'sṛṣṭāḥ || 12 || he vsakhe vviṣṇo tvaṃ vvitaram atyantaṃ vvi vkramasva vikramaṃ kuru | he vdyauḥ tvaṃ vvajrāya vajrasya vviṣkambhe viṣkambhanāya vlokam avakāśaṃ vdehi prayaccha | he viṣṇo tvaṃ cāhaṃ cobhau āvāṃ

pṛṣṭhasya pañcame'hani prauge vāyavyatṛcasya ‘ ā no yajñaṃ ’ ityādike dve ṛcāvācaye |

mādhyandinasavane somātirekaṃ ekaṃ śaśamupajāyate |

sūtritaṃ ca—‘ baṇmahā~ asi sūryodu tyaharśataṃ vapuriti pragāthau stotriyā- nurūpau ’ (āśva. śrau. 6. 7) iti |
ayameva pragāthaścaturviṃśike'hani mādhyandine brāhmaṇācchaṃsino vaikalpiko'nurūpaḥ |

sūtritaṃ ca—‘ mātā rudrāṇāṃ duhitā vasūnāmiti japitvotmutsṛjateyustvakṣan^ ’ (āśva. gṛ. 1. 24. 25) iti ||
ṛdhagitthā sa martyaḥ śaśame devatāṃtaye | yo nūnaṃ mitrāvaruṇāviṣtya ācuke havyadāṃtaye || 1 || ṛdhak | ityā | saḥ | martyaḥ | śaśame | deva'tāṃtaye | yaḥ | nūnam | mitrāvaruṇau | abhiṣṭaye | ā'cuke | havya'dāṃtaye || 1 ||

asyāmṛci uṣasaḥ stutiḥ sūryaprabhāyā vā |
^yā ^iyaṃ ^nīcī avāṅmukhī ^arkiṇī stuti- matī ^rūpā rūpavatī ^rohiṇyā prakāśayuktā ^kṛtā uṣāḥ ^sūryaprabhā votpāditā ^sā ^antaḥ brahmāṇḍasya madhye ^bāhuṣu bāhusthānīyāsu ^daśasu daśasaṃkhyākāsu dikṣu ^āyatī āgacchantī ^citreva citrā gauriva ^pratyadarśi sarvairadṛśyata ||
prajā ha tisro atyāramīyunya ^nyā arkamabhito viviśre |
bṛhadva tasthau bhuvaneṣvantaḥ parvamāno harita ā viveśa ||
14 ||
pra'jāḥ | ha | tisraḥ | ati'āryam | īyuḥ | ni | anyāḥ | arkam | abhitaḥ | triviśre | bṛhat | ha | tasthau | bhuvaneṣu | antariti | parvamānaḥ | haritaḥ | ā | viveśa || 14 ||
yāḥ ^tisraḥ ^prajāḥ ^atyāyamīyuḥ atyāyamāyan ^anyāḥ tā imāḥ prajāḥ ^arkam arcanīya- mabhima ^abhito ^viviśre abhito niviṣṭāstato na parābabhūvuḥ |
^bhuvaneṣvantaḥ madhye ^bṛhat mahān asāvādityaḥ |
prajāpatirityeke |
^tasthau prakāśayansthitat |
^haritaḥ diśaḥ ^pavamānaḥ vāyuḥ ^ā ^viveśa āviṣṭaḥ |
tathā caitareyabrāhmaṇaṃ—'prajā ha tisro atyāyamīyuriti yā vai tā imāḥ prajā- stisro atyāyamāyantānīmāni vayāṃsi vajñāvagadhāśverapādāḥ |
nyanyā arkamabhito viviśra iti tā imāḥ prajā arkamabhito niviṣṭā imamevāgniṃ bṛhat tasthau bhuvaneṣvantarityada u eva bṛhadbhuvaneṣvantara- sāvādityaḥ pavamāno harita ā viveśeti vāyureva pavamāno diśo harita āviṣṭaḥ' (ai. ā. 2. 1. 1) iti |
vājasaneyino'pyāmanti—'sa tapa'tapyata sa prajā asṛjata tā asya prajāḥ sṛṣṭāḥ parābabhūvustānīmāni vayāṃsi' ityupakramya 'prajā ha tisro atyāyamīyuriti yā amūḥ prajā atyāyan^ nyanyā^ arkamabhito viviśra ityabhirvā arkastamimāḥ prajā abhito niviṣṭāstā imāḥ parābhūtāḥ |
bṛhadva tasthau bhuvaneṣvantariti prajāpatimevaitadabhyanūktaṃ pavamāno harita ā viveśeti diśo vai haritastā ayaṃ pavamāna āviṣṭaḥ' (śa. brā. 2. 5. 1. 1-5) iti ||

adhyāyopākaraṇotsarjanayormaṇḍalāntyahome ‘āgne yāhi’ ityeṣā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘mā cidāgne yāhi marutsakhā yatte rājannagne’ (āśva. gṛ. 3. 5. 7) iti ||
adarśa gātuvittamo yasminvratānyādhuḥ | upo ṣu jñātamāryasya vardhanamagniṃ nakṣanta no giraḥ || 1 || adarśa | gātuvit'tamaḥ | yasmin | vratāni | ā'dhuḥ | upo iti | su | jñātam | āryasya | vardhanam | agnim | nakṣanta | naḥ | giraḥ || 1 ||
'yasmin agnau 'vratāni karmāṇi 'ādhuḥ yajamānā ādadhan 'gātuvittamaḥ atiśayena mārgāṇāṃ jñātā so'gniḥ 'adarśa prādurabhūta |
kiṃca 'su 'jātaṃ samyak prādurbhūtam asya 'āryasya uttamavarṇasya 'vardhanaṃ vardhayitāram 'agni 'naḥ asmākaṃ 'giraḥ stutirūpā vācaḥ 'upo 'nakṣanta upagacchantyeva ||
‘nakṣa gatau’ iti dhātuḥ ||
1. kha-ga-ta-‘upajihvikā······kāṣṭhādikaṃ’ nāsti |
na-bha-śa-‘tyupajihvikā upadahikā (na-śa-upadīhikā) |
2. ta1 (prānte)-anu.-paṃcamī virāḍpā saptamyādyayujaḥ |
pra daivodāso agnirdevā acchā na majmanā |
anu mātaraṃ pṛthivīṃ vi vāvṛte tasthau nākasya sānavi ||
2 ||
pra | daivaḥ'dāsaḥ | agniḥ | devān | acchā | na | majmanā | anu | mātaram | pṛthivīm | vi | vāvṛte | tasthau | nākasya | sānavi || 2 ||
^daivodāsaḥ divodāsenāhūyamānaḥ ^agniḥ ^mātaram |
sarvasya lokasya dhāraṇavattvāt pṛthivī mātā |
tāṃ ^pṛthivīm ^acchā prati ^devān tasya divodāsasya yajñe devān ^anu^ prati havirvoḍhuṃ ^na ^pra^ ^vi vāvṛte |
yasmādenamagni divodāsaḥ ^majmanā balena ājuhāva tasmādayamagniḥ ^nākasya svargasya ^sānavi samucchrite deśe svāyatana eva ^tasthau atiṣṭhat ||
yasmādrejanta kṛṣṭayaścarkṛtyāni kṛṇvataḥ |
sahasrāṃ medhasātāviva tmanāgniṃ dhībhiḥ saparyata ||
3 ||
yasmāt | rejanta | kṛṣṭayaḥ | carkṛtyāni | kṛṇvataḥ | sahasra'sām | medhasātā'iva | tmanā | agnim | dhībhiḥ | saparyata || 3 ||
^yasmāt kāraṇāt ^carkṛtyāni kartavyāni karmāṇi ^kṛṇvataḥ kurvāṇāt manuṣyāt ^kṛṣṭayaḥ itare manuṣyāḥ ^rejanta kampante tasmādidānīṃ he janāḥ^ yūyaṃ ^sahasrāṃ gavāṃ dhanānāṃ ca mahasrasya dātāram ^agni ^medhasātā yajñe ^dhībhiḥ kartavyaiḥ karmabhiḥ ^tmanā ātmanaiva ^saparyata paricarata ||
pra yaṃ rāye ninīṃṣasi marto yastai vaso dāśat |
sa vīraṃ dhatte agra ukthaśaṃsinaṃ tmanā sahasrapoṣiṇam ||
4 ||
pra | yam | rāye | ninīṃṣasi | marttaḥ | yaḥ | te | vaso iti | dāśat | saḥ | vīram | dhatte | agne | uktha'śaṃsinam | tmanā | sahasra'poṣiṇam || 4 ||
he ^vaso vāsaka ^agne tvaṃ ^yaṃ tava stotāraṃ ^rāye dhanārthaṃ ^pra ^ninīṃṣasi praṇetumicchasi ^yaḥ ca ^marttaḥ manuṣyaḥ ^te tubhyaṃ ^dāśat havīṃṣi prayacchati ^saḥ manuṣyaḥ ^ukthaśaṃsinam ukthānāṃ^ śaṃsitāraṃ ^tmanā ātmanaiva ^sahasrapoṣiṇaṃ bahudhanaṃ ^vīraṃ putraṃ ^dhatte dhārayati ||
sa duḻhe cidbhi tṛṇatti vājamarvatā sa dhatte akṣiti śravaḥ |
tve devatrā sadā puruvaso viśvā vāmāni dhīmahi ||
5 ||
saḥ | duḻhe | cit | abhi | tṛṇatti | vājam | arvatā | saḥ | dhatte | akṣiti | śravaḥ | tve iti | deva'trā | sadā | puruvaso iti puru'vaso | viśvā | vāmāni | dhīmahi ||5||
he ^puruvaso bahudhanāme yastubhyaṃ havīṃṣi prayacchati ^saḥ yajamānaḥ ^duḻhe ^cit dṛḍhe'pi śatrupure sthitaṃ ^vājam annam ^arvatā adhena ^abhi ^tṛṇatti hinasti | tathā ^saḥ yajamānaḥ ^akṣiti akṣīṇaṃ śravaḥ | ^tve iti ^deva'trā | sadā | ^puruvaso iti puru'vaso | ^viśvā | ^vāmāni | ^dhīmahi ||5||
1. kha-ta-bha-^ 'anu' nāsti | 2. kha-ta-1-2-pra vi vavṛte; ga-na4-bha-pra vavṛte | 3. kha-ta-na- yājanāḥ; ga-śa-yajamānāḥ | 4. gha-stotrāṇāṃ | 5. kha-ga-gha-ta-bha-śa-arvatā adhena |
vśrava: annaṃ vdhatte dhārayati|
tathā ca sati tubhyaṃ haviṣāṃ pradātāro vayamapi vdevatrā deve vtve tvayi sthitā: vviśvā sarvāṇi vvāmāni vananīyāni dhanāni vsadā sarvadā vdhīmahi dhārayāma ||
13 ||
yo viśvā daryate vasu hotā mandro janānām |
madhorna pātrā prathamānyasmai pra stomā yantyagnaye ||
6 ||
ya: | viśvā | daryate | vasu | hotā | mandra: | janānām | madho: | na | pātrā | prathamāni | asmai | pra | stomā: | yanti | agnaye || 6 ||
vhotā devānāmāhvātā vmandra: modamāna: vya: agni: vviśvā sarvāṇi vvasu vasūni dhanāni vjanānāṃ janebhya: vdayate prayacchati tasmai vasmai vagnaye vmadhonaṃ madakarasya somasyeva vprathamāni mukhyāni pātrāṇi vstomā: vpra vyanti gacchanti ||
aśvaṃ na gīrbhī rdhyaṃ sudānavo marmṛjyante devayava: |
ubhe toke tanaye
agne ᵥnavīyasī navatarā ᵥsumati: anugrahabuddhi: ᵥna: asmān ᵥaccha' prati ᵥvājebhi: agne: saha ᵥkuvit bahuvāram' |
'salilaṃ kuvit' iti bahunāmasu pāṭhāt |
ᵥāgamata āgacchatu |
preṣṭham priyāṇāṃ stuhāsāvātithiyam | agniṃ rathānāṃ yamam || 10 || preṣṭham | oṃ iti | priyāṇām | stuhi | ā'sāva | atithiyam | agnim | rathānām | yamam || 10 || he ᵥāsāva stota: ᵥpriyāṇāṃ ᵥpreṣṭhaṃ priyatamam ᵥatithiyam abhyāgataṃ ᵥrathānāṃ ᵥyamaṃ yantāram ᵥagnim eva ᵥstuhi || || 14 ||
urdito yo nirdito vedito vasvā yujñiyau vurvartati | duṣṭarā yasya pravaṇe normayau dhiyā vājaṃ siṣāsata: || 11 || ud'itā | ya: | ni'rdita | vedita | vasu | ā | yujñiya: | vurvartati | dustarā: | yasya | pravaṇe | na | ūrmaya: | | dhiyā | vājam | siṣāsata: || 11 || ᵥveditā vettā ᵥyajñiya: yajñārha: ᵥvya:³ agni: ᵥuditā uditānyavratāni ᵥniditā niditāni śrutāni ca ᵥvasu vasūni dhanāni ᵥā ᵥvavartati āvartayati⁴ | ᵥdhiyā karmaṇā ᵥvājaṃ saṃgrāmaṃ ᵥsiṣā-sata: saṃbhaktumicchata: ᵥyasya cāgnervālā:⁵ ᵥpravaṇe ᵥnormaya: pravaṇābhimukhā: samudrastha taraṅgā iva ᵥduṣṭarā: tartumaśakyā: | tamagniṃ he stota: stuhītyartha: ||
mā nau haṇītāmatithivrsurami: purupraśasta eṣa: | ya: suhotā svadhvara: || 12 || mā | na: | haṇītām | atithiya: | vasu: | agni: | puru'praśasta: | eṣa: | ya: | su'hotā | su'adhvara: || 12 || ᵥvasu: vāsaka: ᵥatithi: atithivat priya: ᵥpurupraśasta: bahubhi: stuta: ᵥsuhotā suṣṭhu devānā-māhvātā ᵥsvadhvara: suyajñaśca ᵥya: ᵥagni: sa: ᵥeṣa: agni: ᵥna: asmabhyaṃ ᵥmā ᵥhaṇītāṃ kenāpi na rudhyatām | kenāpyanavaruddha: san agni: asmabhyamabhīṣṭaṃ prayacchatvityartha: ||
mo te riṣan ye acchoktibhirvaso'gne kebhiścidaivai: | kīriściddhi tvāmīṭṭe dūtyāya rātahavya: svadhvara: || 13 || mo iti | te | riṣan | ye | acchoktibhi: | vaso iti | agne | kebhi: | cit | evai: | kīri: | cit | hi | tvām | īṭṭe | dūtyāya | rāta'havya: | su'adhvara: || 13 || he ᵥvaso vāsaka ᵥagne tvāṃ ᵥye manuṣyā: ᵥacchoktibhi: abhiṣṭutibhi: ᵥkebhi: kai: sukhakarai: ᵥevai: ᵥcit abhigamanairapi ᵥte stotāra: ᵥmo ᵥriṣan maiva hiṃsantām | ᵥrātahavya: dattahaviṣka:⁶ ᵥkīriścit stotāpi ᵥdūtyāya havirvahanādilakṣaṇāya dūtakarmaṇe ᵥtvāmīṭṭe ᵥhi stauti khalu ||
1. kha-ta1-2-accha abhilakṣya (kha-ālakṣya; jha-'abhilakṣya' nāsti; ta2-ābhilakṣya ) prayacchati; gha-śa-accha abhilakṣya; ta4-na-bhara-prayacchati |
2. kha-ta-na-bha-bahu |
3. ga-ta-bha-'ya:' nāsti |
4. kha-ga-gha-ta-śa-āvartayate |
5. kha-ta-na-bha-vāme° |
6. gha-śa-dattahaviṣka: svadhvara: |
āgne yāhi marutsakhā rudraibhiḥ somapītaye | sobharyā upa suṣṭuti mādayasva svarṇare || 14 || ā | agne | yāhi | marutsakhā | rudraibhiḥ | somapītaye | sobharyāḥ | upa | suṣṭutim | mādayasva | svaḥ'nare || 14 ||
he ٧agne ٧marutsakhā¹ marutāṃ priyasvaṃ ٧svarṇare asmākaṃ yajanalakṣaṇe karmaṇi ٧somapītaye soma- pānāya ٧rudraibhiḥ rudrairmarudbhiḥ saha ٧ā ٧yāhi āgaccha |
